            try {
              if (paused && sc && sc.scene) sc.scene.resume();
            } catch (e) {}
            playing = null;
            resolve();
          }, 900);
        };
        const onKey = (e) => {
          if (e.key === ' ' || e.key === 'Enter') {
            e.preventDefault();
            e.stopPropagation();
            if (box.classList.contains('show')) advance();
          } else if (e.key === 'Escape') {
            e.preventDefault();
            e.stopPropagation();
            finish();
          } else e.stopPropagation();
        };
        window.addEventListener('keydown', onKey, true);
        root.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
          if (e.target.closest('.skip')) return finish();
          if (box.classList.contains('show')) advance();
        });
        // 2. Chapter card, then Ms. Green's story box.
        setTimeout(() => {
          if (ended) return;
          box.classList.add('show');
          advance();
        }, 2900);
      }, canvas ? 1900 : 0);
    });
    return playing;
  }

  // ---- loops --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  setInterval(() => {
    const sc = getScene();
    if (sc && sc.add && sc.events && sc.player && !sc.__lqOldMap) {
      try {
        attach(sc);
      } catch (e) {
        console.warn('old map attach failed', e);
      }
    }
  }, 1000);

  setInterval(() => {
    const sc = getScene();
    if (!sc || !sc.store || !sc.store.game || playing) return;
    // The quest opens: tell the player once.
    if (isOpen()) {
      const d = data(true);
      if (!d.hinted && !(sc.chat && sc.chat.isOpen)) {
        d.hinted = Date.now();
        save();
        toast('📜 Town news', 'Ms. Green found something strange in the Library.', 'Ask her: "Did you find something old?"');
        refreshQuest();
      }
      // Story is ready and the chat is closed: play the prelude once.
      // Then into the village of 70 years ago (past.js), with its lead-in film.
      if (d.storyAt && !d.seen && !(sc.chat && sc.chat.isOpen)) {
        d.seen = Date.now();
        save();
        playPrelude().then(() => window.LQPast && window.LQPast.enter({ film: true }));
      }
      // Asked Ms. Green to go back to the village.
      if (d.goPast && !(sc.chat && sc.chat.isOpen)) {
        d.goPast = 0;
        save();
        if (window.LQPast) window.LQPast.enter({ film: false });
      }
    }
    if (isStarted()) syncQuest();
  }, 1500);

  if (/[?&]oldmap=1\b/.test(location.search)) {
    const wait = setInterval(() => {
      const sc = getScene();
      if (sc && sc.player) {
        clearInterval(wait);
        setTimeout(playPrelude, 800);
      }
    }, 500);
  }

  window.LQOldMap = {
    list: RELICS,
    status,
    tellStatus,
    onNpcMessage,
    isOpen,
    isStarted,
    isFound,
    isTold,
    foundCount,
    toldCount,
    drawRelic,
    spot: (id) => {
      const sc = getScene();
      const r = RELICS.find((x) => x.id === id);
      return sc && r ? spotFor(sc, r) : null;
    },
    // Watch the Chapter 2 prelude without changing progress.
    preview: playPrelude,
    // Handy while testing: start the quest over.
    reset() {
      const d = data(false);
      if (!d) return;
      Object.assign(d, { hinted: 0, started: 0, found: {}, told: {}, storyAt: 0, seen: 0 });
      save();
      refreshQuest();
    },
  };
})();

</script>
    <script>
/*
 * Chapter 2 · "70 Years Ago": the village of Oriaris (English Town seventy years ago) as a playable area.
 * Setting: NewGoal.md ("Ý tưởng mở rộng") and Chapter2.md.
 *
 *  - Area 'english-town-past' (64×48). It uses the SAME tile grid as today's English Town, so things line up with the
 *    Old Map quest: the well is where the fountain is now, Oriaris Hall stands where the Town Hall is, the east wall
 *    passes (42,16) where the wall stone is today, and rice fields cover the land of the school and the station.
 *  - No new NPCs: the story is told by objects (signs, an inscription, a letter, a notice) in the past tense.
 *  - The whole chapter is pale grey (CSS filter on the game canvas while the player is here).
 *  - Lead-in film (play()): in the real map, with the camera: the village, Lord Gusest's speech on the hall steps,
 *    the people cheer, he leaves and sends food. Then the player walks around.
 *  - Exit: the glowing path at the south end ("Back to today") leads to the Library in English Town.
 *
 * Hooks in the game code: 03-core-areas-and-map.js registers `area` (needs the `ue` class there);
 * 10-core-characters-and-scene.js (drawWorld) calls paint() for theme 'past'. Entry from oldmap.js (after the
 * Ms. Green prelude) or Ms. Green later ("take me back"). Progress: game.ach.past = { visits, read }.
 * Testing: index.html?past=1 (film) or ?past=2 (no film), LQPast.enter(), LQPast.play().
 */

  const READ_RADIUS = 46;

  const ROOMS = {

  // Things to read inside (tile = room tile). Walk up to them like the signs outside.
  const NOTES = [

      en: 'A portrait of Lord Gusest. He looked kind, but very tired.',

      title: '🗺️ A war map on the desk',

      vi: 'Các kỵ sĩ đã đi về phía bắc, tới biên giới Bắc Ire. Có người đã vẽ một đường đỏ ở đó.',

    },
    {

      title: '🪝 An empty peg',
      en: 'An empty peg by the door. The young soldier took his helmet to the wall.',
      vi: 'Một chiếc móc trống cạnh cửa. Người lính trẻ đã mang mũ sắt ra bức tường.',
    },
    {
      id: 'diary', room: 'Brown Family Home', x: 6, y: 4,
      title: '📔 A small diary',
      en: '“Day three. We waited. Mother cooked rice soup. I was not hungry.”',
      vi: '“Ngày thứ ba. Chúng tôi chờ đợi. Mẹ nấu cháo. Tôi không thấy đói.”',
    },
  ];
  const ALL_READ = () => SIGNS.length + NOTES.length;

  // ---- obstacles ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  function makeObstacles(a) {
    const r = [];
    const add = (kind, x, y, w = 1, h = 1) => r.push({ kind, x, y, w, h });
    const inGate = (side, v) => GATES.some((g) => g.side === side && v >= g.from && v <= g.to);
    // The wall: one tile thick, with two gates.
    for (let x = WALL.x1; x <= WALL.x2; x++) {
      add('wall', x, WALL.y1);
      if (!inGate('south', x)) add('wall', x, WALL.y2);
    }
    for (let y = WALL.y1 + 1; y < WALL.y2; y++) {
      add('wall', WALL.x1, y);
      if (!inGate('east', y)) add('wall', WALL.x2, y);
    }
    // The well (where the fountain is today)
    add('well', 26, 14, 2, 2);
    // Big rice paddies east (school + park land today) and south (station land today), with one-tile dikes to walk on.
    [[46, 2, 8, 7], [55, 2, 8, 7], [46, 14, 8, 7], [55, 14, 8, 7], [46, 22, 8, 7], [55, 22, 8, 7]].forEach((p) => add('paddy', ...p));
    [[2, 35, 7, 6], [10, 35, 6, 6], [2, 42, 7, 5], [10, 42, 6, 5]].forEach((p) => add('paddy', ...p));
    [[21, 35, 8, 6], [30, 35, 8, 6], [39, 35, 8, 6], [48, 35, 7, 6], [56, 35, 7, 6]].forEach((p) => add('paddy', ...p));
    [[22, 42, 7, 5], [30, 42, 8, 5], [39, 42, 8, 5], [48, 42, 7, 5], [56, 42, 7, 5]].forEach((p) => add('paddy', ...p));
    // Village life inside the wall
    [[4, 9], [13, 8], [36, 9], [39, 15], [4, 16], [9, 20], [38, 21], [20, 22]].forEach(([x, y]) => add('tree', x, y));
    [[10, 8], [12, 9]].forEach(([x, y]) => add('hay', x, y));
    add('cart', 14, 4, 2, 1);
    [[11, 7], [12, 7]].forEach(([x, y]) => add('barrel', x, y));
    add('banner', 26, 7);
    add('banner', 34, 7);
    // Little vegetable gardens behind the cottages
    [[2, 29, 4], [8, 29, 4], [30, 29, 4], [35, 29, 4]].forEach(([x, y, w]) => add('garden', x, y, w, 1));
    // Trees outside the wall
    [[45, 33], [61, 33], [63, 30], [44, 2], [63, 13], [1, 34], [1, 44], [63, 46], [20, 46]].forEach(([x, y]) => add('tree', x, y));
    // Watchtowers outside both gates
    add('tower', 15, 32, 2, 2);
    add('tower', 20, 32, 2, 2);
    add('tower', 43, 7, 2, 2);
    add('tower', 43, 13, 2, 2);
    // Torches along the inside of the wall
    [[2, 2], [10, 2], [22, 2], [36, 2], [41, 6], [41, 20], [41, 28], [2, 14], [2, 22], [15, 30], [21, 30]].forEach(([x, y]) => add('torch', x, y));
    // Rice drying racks between the wall and the south fields
    add('rack', 24, 32, 3, 1);
    add('rack', 30, 32, 3, 1);
    add('rack', 36, 32, 3, 1);
    // Market stall in the square, a wood pile, a hen house
    add('stall', 30, 16, 2, 1);
    add('woodpile', 37, 13, 2, 1);
    add('coop', 5, 20, 2, 2);
    // Signs (readable)
    SIGNS.forEach((s) => add('sign', s.x, s.y));
    return r.filter((o) => o.x >= 0 && o.y >= 0 && o.x + o.w <= a.w && o.y + o.h <= a.h);
  }

  const area = {
    id: ID,
    name: 'Oriaris Village · 70 Years Ago',
    w: W,
    h: H,
    theme: 'past',
    buildings: BUILDINGS,
    places: PLACES,
    roadsH: [{ y: 10, h: 3 }],
    roadsV: [{ x: 17, w: 3 }],
    spawn: SPAWN,
    npcs: ['gusest'],
    portals: {},
    warps: WARPS,
    makeObstacles,
  };

  // ---- painting (Phaser Graphics, called once for the area texture) ------------------------------------------------
  // Everything is drawn with code (no image files). The world is shown through a pale filter (applyFilter), so the
  // drawing relies on light/shadow more than on colour: every object gets a soft shadow, a lit top and a dark base.
  function rnd(seed) {
    let s = seed | 0;
    return () => {
      s = (s + 0x6d2b79f5) | 0;
      let t = Math.imul(s ^ (s >>> 15), 1 | s);
      t = (t + Math.imul(t ^ (t >>> 7), 61 | t)) ^ t;
      return ((t ^ (t >>> 14)) >>> 0) / 4294967296;
    };
  }
  // Multiply a colour (k < 1 darker, k > 1 lighter).
  const shade = (c, k) => {
    const f = (v) => Math.max(0, Math.min(255, Math.round(v * k)));
    return (f((c >> 16) & 255) << 16) | (f((c >> 8) & 255) << 8) | f(c & 255);
  };
  const T = 32;
  let R = rnd(1);
  const pick = (arr) => arr[Math.floor(R() * arr.length)];
  // Things that stick up above their tile: drawn as their own sprites, in front of or behind the player.
  const TALL = new Set(['tower', 'tree', 'well', 'banner', 'torch', 'stall', 'coop', 'rack']);
  const shadow = (g, cx, cy, w, h, a = 0.22) => g.fillStyle(0x1a160f, a).fillEllipse(cx, cy, w, h);
  const poly = (g, color, alpha, pts) => g.fillStyle(color, alpha).fillPoints(pts.map(([x, y]) => ({ x, y })), true);

  function paint(g, a) {
    if (a.id !== ID && window.LQFront) return window.LQFront.paint(g, a);
    R = rnd(7070);
    const W2 = a.w * T;
    const H2 = a.h * T;
    // 1. Grass: three tones per tile, then blades, clover and a few pale flowers.
    // Soft patches instead of per-tile squares, so no grid shows through.
    g.fillStyle(0x8e9a68, 1).fillRect(0, 0, W2, H2);
    for (let i = 0; i < a.w * a.h * 0.9; i++) {
      const c = pick([0x96a271, 0x86925f, 0x9aa676, 0x8a965f]);
      g.fillStyle(c, 0.35).fillEllipse(R() * W2, R() * H2, 30 + R() * 60, 18 + R() * 36);
    }
    for (let i = 0; i < a.w * a.h * 0.25; i++) g.fillStyle(0x7f8b56, 0.25).fillEllipse(R() * W2, R() * H2, 10 + R() * 18, 6 + R() * 10);
    for (let i = 0; i < a.w * a.h * 1.6; i++) {
      const px = R() * W2;
      const py = R() * H2;
      const c = R() < 0.6 ? 0x6c7848 : 0xa6b07c;
      g.lineStyle(1.4, c, 0.9).lineBetween(px, py, px - 2, py - 5).lineBetween(px, py, px + 1, py - 6).lineBetween(px, py, px + 3, py - 4);
    }
    for (let i = 0; i < 160; i++) {
      const px = R() * W2;
      const py = R() * H2;
      g.fillStyle(R() < 0.5 ? 0xe9e2c8 : 0xd8c27a, 0.85).fillCircle(px, py, 1.6).fillCircle(px + 3, py + 1, 1.4).fillCircle(px + 1, py - 2, 1.4);
    }

    // 2. The village square: trodden earth with a soft grassy edge and pebbles.
    const sq = { x: SQUARE.x * T, y: SQUARE.y * T, w: SQUARE.w * T, h: SQUARE.h * T };
    g.fillStyle(0xb6a582, 1).fillRoundedRect(sq.x - 6, sq.y - 6, sq.w + 12, sq.h + 12, 26);
    g.fillStyle(0xbfae8b, 1).fillRoundedRect(sq.x + 10, sq.y + 8, sq.w - 20, sq.h - 16, 20);
    for (let i = 0; i < 120; i++) g.fillStyle(pick([0x9e8d6c, 0xcdbd99, 0xa89676]), 1).fillEllipse(sq.x + R() * sq.w, sq.y + R() * sq.h, 4 + R() * 4, 3 + R() * 2);

    // 3. Dirt roads: worn middle, two wheel ruts, stones, grass creeping over the edges.
    const dirt = (x, y, w, h, vertical) => {
      g.fillStyle(0xa8966f, 1).fillRect(x, y, w, h);
      if (vertical) g.fillStyle(0xbba985, 1).fillRect(x + 8, y, w - 16, h);
      else g.fillStyle(0xbba985, 1).fillRect(x, y + 8, w, h - 16);
      const rut = (rx, ry, rw, rh) => g.fillStyle(0x92805e, 0.8).fillRect(rx, ry, rw, rh);
      if (vertical) {
        rut(x + w * 0.3, y, 4, h);
        rut(x + w * 0.66, y, 4, h);
      } else {
        rut(x, y + h * 0.32, w, 4);
        rut(x, y + h * 0.66, w, 4);
      }
      for (let i = 0; i < (w * h) / 260; i++) {
        const px = x + R() * w;
        const py = y + R() * h;
        g.fillStyle(0x8a7a5c, 0.6).fillEllipse(px + 1, py + 1, 5, 3);
        g.fillStyle(pick([0xcfc2a2, 0xb7a787, 0xd8ccae]), 1).fillEllipse(px, py, 4, 3);
      }
      // grass tufts over both edges
      const n = (vertical ? h : w) / 9;
      for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
        const t = R() * (vertical ? h : w);
        [0, 1].forEach((side) => {
          const px = vertical ? x + (side ? w - 2 : 2) : x + t;
          const py = vertical ? y + t : y + (side ? h - 2 : 4);
          g.lineStyle(1.5, 0x748052, 1).lineBetween(px, py, px - 2, py - 6).lineBetween(px, py, px + 2, py - 7);
        });
      }
    };
    a.roadsH.forEach((r) => dirt(0, r.y * T, W2, r.h * T, false));
    a.roadsV.forEach((r) => dirt(r.x * T, 0, r.w * T, H2, true));
    // crossings: smooth the middle again
    a.roadsH.forEach((h) => a.roadsV.forEach((v) => g.fillStyle(0xb4a27e, 1).fillRect(v.x * T + 6, h.y * T + 6, v.w * T - 12, h.h * T - 12)));

    // 4. Flat objects only. Tall things (buildings, towers, trees…) are separate sprites sorted against the player,
    //    so the player can walk behind them (see tallSprites below).
    [...a.obstacles].filter((o) => !TALL.has(o.kind)).sort((p, q) => p.y + p.h - (q.y + q.h)).forEach((o) => drawThing(g, o, a));
    // 6. The way back to today
    a.warps.forEach((w) => {
      const cx = (w.x + w.w / 2) * T;
      const cy = (w.y + 0.5) * T;
      for (let i = 0; i < 6; i++) g.fillStyle(0xffe6a8, 0.07).fillEllipse(cx, cy, w.w * T + 40 - i * 9, T + 22 - i * 4);
      g.fillStyle(0xfff6dc, 0.55).fillEllipse(cx, cy, w.w * T - 16, 12);
      for (let i = 0; i < 14; i++) g.fillStyle(0xfff6dc, 0.7).fillCircle(cx + (R() - 0.5) * w.w * T, cy - R() * 26, 1.5);
    });
  }

  function drawThing(g, o) {
    const x = o.x * T;
    const y = o.y * T;
    const w = o.w * T;
    const h = o.h * T;
    switch (o.kind) {
      case 'wall':
        return wallTile(g, o);
      case 'well':
        return well(g, x, y, w, h);
      case 'paddy':
        return paddy(g, x, y, w, h);
      case 'tree':
        return tree(g, x + 16, y + 26);
      case 'tower':
        return tower(g, x, y, w, h);
      case 'hay':
        shadow(g, x + 18, y + 27, 28, 9);
        g.fillStyle(0xb39a52, 1).fillEllipse(x + 16, y + 18, 28, 22);
        g.fillStyle(0xcdb56c, 1).fillEllipse(x + 14, y + 15, 22, 15);
        for (let i = 0; i < 9; i++) g.lineStyle(1, 0x9a8240, 0.9).lineBetween(x + 6 + i * 2.5, y + 10 + (i % 3), x + 4 + i * 2.6, y + 24);
        return;
      case 'cart':
        shadow(g, x + w / 2, y + 28, w - 4, 8);
        g.fillStyle(0x6b5233, 1).fillRect(x + 4, y + 6, w - 12, 16);
        g.fillStyle(0x8a6a44, 1).fillRect(x + 4, y + 6, w - 12, 4);
        for (let xx = x + 10; xx < x + w - 10; xx += 10) g.fillStyle(0x5a4429, 1).fillRect(xx, y + 10, 2, 12);
        g.fillStyle(0xc9b06a, 1).fillEllipse(x + w / 2 - 4, y + 6, w - 22, 10);
        [x + 14, x + w - 22].forEach((cx) => {
          g.fillStyle(0x3d2f1f, 1).fillCircle(cx, y + 24, 8);
          g.fillStyle(0x7a5e3a, 1).fillCircle(cx, y + 24, 5);
          g.fillStyle(0x3d2f1f, 1).fillCircle(cx, y + 24, 2);
        });
        g.lineStyle(3, 0x6b5233, 1).lineBetween(x + w - 8, y + 14, x + w + 6, y + 18);
        return;
      case 'barrel':
        shadow(g, x + 17, y + 28, 20, 6);
        g.fillStyle(0x6e5132, 1).fillRoundedRect(x + 7, y + 5, 18, 23, 5);
        g.fillStyle(0x8a6842, 1).fillRect(x + 10, y + 6, 6, 21);
        g.fillStyle(0x4a4640, 1).fillRect(x + 7, y + 9, 18, 2).fillRect(x + 7, y + 21, 18, 2);
        g.fillStyle(0x9a7a50, 1).fillEllipse(x + 16, y + 6, 18, 6);
        return;
      case 'banner':
        g.fillStyle(0x4b3a26, 1).fillRect(x + 14, y - 22, 4, 52);
        g.fillStyle(0xc9a24a, 1).fillCircle(x + 16, y - 23, 3);
        poly(g, 0x7d2e2e, 1, [[x + 18, y - 18], [x + 34, y - 18], [x + 34, y + 6], [x + 26, y + 2], [x + 18, y + 6]]);
        g.fillStyle(0x9a3c3c, 1).fillRect(x + 18, y - 18, 16, 3);
        crest(g, x + 26, y - 8, 1.1);
        return;
      case 'garden':
        g.fillStyle(0x6e5c40, 1).fillRoundedRect(x + 2, y + 6, w - 4, h - 10, 4);
        for (let xx = x + 8; xx < x + w - 4; xx += 9) {
          g.fillStyle(0x5c7a3c, 1).fillCircle(xx, y + 16, 4);
          g.fillStyle(0x7a9a50, 1).fillCircle(xx - 1, y + 14, 2.4);
        }
        g.lineStyle(2, 0x8a6a44, 1).strokeRect(x + 2, y + 6, w - 4, h - 10);
        return;
      case 'torch': {
        for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) g.fillStyle(0xffd27a, 0.06).fillCircle(x + 16, y + 6, 22 - i * 5);
        shadow(g, x + 18, y + 29, 10, 4);
        g.fillStyle(0x4b3a26, 1).fillRect(x + 14, y + 8, 4, 21);
        g.fillStyle(0x3a3a3a, 1).fillRect(x + 11, y + 7, 10, 4);
        poly(g, 0xd9822e, 1, [[x + 10, y + 8], [x + 22, y + 8], [x + 19, y - 1], [x + 16, y - 9], [x + 13, y - 1]]);
        poly(g, 0xffd27a, 1, [[x + 13, y + 8], [x + 19, y + 8], [x + 17, y + 1], [x + 16, y - 3], [x + 15, y + 1]]);
        return;
      }
      case 'rack': {
        shadow(g, x + w / 2, y + 30, w, 8);
        g.fillStyle(0x5e4630, 1).fillRect(x + 4, y + 2, 4, 28).fillRect(x + w - 8, y + 2, 4, 28);
        g.fillStyle(0x7a5e3a, 1).fillRect(x + 2, y + 4, w - 4, 4);
        for (let xx = x + 11; xx < x + w - 8; xx += 8) {
          poly(g, 0xb79a52, 1, [[xx - 4, y + 8], [xx + 4, y + 8], [xx + 3, y + 22], [xx, y + 27], [xx - 3, y + 22]]);
          g.fillStyle(0xd5bc72, 1).fillRect(xx - 2, y + 9, 2, 12);
          g.fillStyle(0x6b5a30, 1).fillRect(xx - 4, y + 12, 8, 2);
        }
        return;
      }
      case 'stall': {
        shadow(g, x + w / 2, y + 30, w + 6, 8);
        g.fillStyle(0x5e4630, 1).fillRect(x + 3, y - 12, 3, 40).fillRect(x + w - 6, y - 12, 3, 40);
        g.fillStyle(0x7a5e3a, 1).fillRect(x + 1, y + 12, w - 2, 14);
        g.fillStyle(0x8f7048, 1).fillRect(x + 1, y + 12, w - 2, 4);
        for (let i = 0; i < 8; i++) g.fillStyle(i % 2 ? 0xd9cdb4 : 0x9a8466, 1).fillRect(x - 3 + i * ((w + 6) / 8), y - 16, (w + 6) / 8, 9);
        for (let i = 0; i < 8; i++) g.fillStyle(i % 2 ? 0xd9cdb4 : 0x9a8466, 1).fillTriangle(x - 3 + i * ((w + 6) / 8), y - 7, x - 3 + (i + 1) * ((w + 6) / 8), y - 7, x - 3 + (i + 0.5) * ((w + 6) / 8), y - 2);
        [[x + 12, 0xb7964e], [x + 26, 0xd8cfb0], [x + 42, 0x9a6e44], [x + 54, 0xb7964e]].forEach(([cx, c]) => {
          g.fillStyle(0x6b5233, 1).fillEllipse(cx, y + 13, 12, 6);
          g.fillStyle(c, 1).fillCircle(cx - 2, y + 10, 3).fillCircle(cx + 2, y + 10, 3).fillCircle(cx, y + 8, 3);
        });
        return;
      }
      case 'woodpile':
        shadow(g, x + w / 2, y + 30, w, 7);
        for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++)
          for (let j = 0; j < 5 - i; j++) {
            const cx = x + 10 + j * 11 + i * 5.5;
            const cy = y + 25 - i * 8;
            g.fillStyle(0x5a4429, 1).fillCircle(cx, cy, 5.5);
            g.fillStyle(0xb8955f, 1).fillCircle(cx, cy, 3.8);
            g.fillStyle(0x8f7048, 1).fillCircle(cx, cy, 1.5);
          }
        return;
      case 'coop': {
        shadow(g, x + w / 2 + 3, y + h - 6, w, 12);
        g.fillStyle(0x6b5233, 1).fillRect(x + 8, y + 22, w - 16, h - 30);
        for (let xx = x + 10; xx < x + w - 10; xx += 7) g.fillStyle(0x7d6142, 1).fillRect(xx, y + 22, 4, h - 30);
        poly(g, 0xb79a5a, 1, [[x + 2, y + 25], [x + w - 2, y + 25], [x + w - 10, y + 6], [x + 10, y + 6]]);
        for (let yy = y + 9; yy < y + 25; yy += 4) g.lineStyle(1, 0x95793e, 1).lineBetween(x + 8, yy, x + w - 8, yy);
        g.fillStyle(0x2e261c, 1).fillRoundedRect(x + w / 2 - 6, y + h - 20, 12, 12, 5);
        // little ramp and scattered grain; the hens themselves are alive (see the flock below)
        g.fillStyle(0x7a5e3a, 1).fillRect(x + w / 2 - 5, y + h - 8, 10, 8);
        for (let i = 0; i < 14; i++) g.fillStyle(0xd8c07e, 0.9).fillCircle(x + 4 + ((i * 37) % (w + 20)), y + h + 4 + ((i * 13) % 18), 1.2);
        return;
      }
      case 'sign':
        shadow(g, x + 16, y + 29, 22, 6);
        g.fillStyle(0x4b3a26, 1).fillRect(x + 8, y + 10, 3, 19).fillRect(x + 21, y + 10, 3, 19);
        g.fillStyle(0x7a5e3a, 1).fillRoundedRect(x + 2, y + 1, 28, 15, 3);
        g.fillStyle(0x8f7048, 1).fillRect(x + 3, y + 2, 26, 3);
        g.fillStyle(0xe2d3a8, 1).fillRect(x + 6, y + 7, 20, 2).fillRect(x + 6, y + 11, 14, 2);
        g.fillStyle(0x3a2e20, 1).fillCircle(x + 5, y + 4, 1).fillCircle(x + 27, y + 4, 1);
        return;
      default:
        return;
    }
  }

  function hen(g, cx, cy) {
    shadow(g, cx, cy + 4, 10, 3, 0.2);
    g.fillStyle(0xefe9dc, 1).fillEllipse(cx, cy, 11, 8);
    g.fillStyle(0xf7f3ea, 1).fillCircle(cx + 4, cy - 4, 3.4);
    g.fillStyle(0xc8432f, 1).fillRect(cx + 3, cy - 9, 3, 2);
    g.fillStyle(0xd9a13a, 1).fillTriangle(cx + 7, cy - 4, cx + 10, cy - 3, cx + 7, cy - 2);
  }

  // Stone wall: irregular blocks, a lit top, crenels on the outer rows and a shadow on the ground below.
  function wallTile(g, o) {
    const x = o.x * T;
    const y = o.y * T;
    const r = rnd(o.x * 131 + o.y * 977);
    const STONES = [0x8e897f, 0x98938a, 0x86817a, 0xa19c92];
    g.fillStyle(0x1a160f, 0.18).fillRect(x, y + T, T, 6);
    g.fillStyle(0x5d5850, 1).fillRect(x, y, T, T);
    const rows = [[1, 9], [11, 9], [21, 10]];
    rows.forEach(([ry, rh], i) => {
      let cx = x + (i % 2 ? -6 : 0);
      while (cx < x + T) {
        const sw = 9 + Math.floor(r() * 9);
        const sx = Math.max(x, cx + 1);
        const ex = Math.min(x + T, cx + sw - 1);
        if (ex - sx > 2) {
          const c = STONES[Math.floor(r() * STONES.length)];
          g.fillStyle(c, 1).fillRoundedRect(sx, y + ry, ex - sx, rh - 1, 2);
          g.fillStyle(shade(c, 1.12), 1).fillRect(sx + 1, y + ry, ex - sx - 2, 2);
          g.fillStyle(shade(c, 0.82), 1).fillRect(sx + 1, y + ry + rh - 3, ex - sx - 2, 2);
        }
        cx += sw;
      }
    });
    // walkway cap and crenels on top
    g.fillStyle(0xb2ada2, 1).fillRect(x, y - 3, T, 4);
    if ((o.x + o.y) % 2 === 0) {
      g.fillStyle(0x9a958b, 1).fillRect(x + 4, y - 10, T - 8, 8);
      g.fillStyle(0xb8b3a8, 1).fillRect(x + 4, y - 10, T - 8, 2);
    }
    // a little moss
    if (r() < 0.35) g.fillStyle(0x6f7d4c, 0.8).fillEllipse(x + r() * T, y + T - 3, 8, 4);
  }

  function well(g, x, y, w, h) {
    const cx = x + w / 2;
    const cy = y + h / 2 + 4;
    shadow(g, cx + 4, cy + 18, w + 6, 18, 0.25);
    // stone ring
    g.fillStyle(0x6f6a62, 1).fillEllipse(cx, cy + 6, 54, 34);
    for (let i = 0; i < 16; i++) {
      const ang = (i / 16) * Math.PI * 2;
      g.fillStyle(i % 2 ? 0x9a958b : 0x8a857c, 1).fillEllipse(cx + Math.cos(ang) * 22, cy + 4 + Math.sin(ang) * 13, 12, 9);
    }
    g.fillStyle(0x26323a, 1).fillEllipse(cx, cy + 3, 30, 16);
    g.fillStyle(0x3f5560, 1).fillEllipse(cx - 4, cy + 1, 16, 6);
    g.fillStyle(0xdfe8ea, 0.5).fillEllipse(cx - 7, cy, 6, 2);
    // posts, beam, little thatch roof, rope and bucket
    g.fillStyle(0x5e4630, 1).fillRect(cx - 25, cy - 30, 5, 36).fillRect(cx + 20, cy - 30, 5, 36);
    g.fillStyle(0x7a5e3a, 1).fillRect(cx - 27, cy - 26, 54, 5);
    poly(g, 0xb39a5a, 1, [[cx - 32, cy - 26], [cx + 32, cy - 26], [cx + 22, cy - 44], [cx - 22, cy - 44]]);
    for (let i = 0; i < 5; i++) g.fillStyle(i % 2 ? 0xc6ad6a : 0xa58c4c, 1).fillRect(cx - 30 + i, cy - 30 - i * 3, 60 - i * 2, 2);
    g.fillStyle(0x8a7340, 1).fillRect(cx - 23, cy - 46, 46, 4);
    g.lineStyle(1, 0x3a3127, 1).lineBetween(cx + 2, cy - 21, cx + 2, cy - 6);
    g.fillStyle(0x6b5233, 1).fillRect(cx - 3, cy - 8, 10, 8);
    g.fillStyle(0x4a4640, 1).fillRect(cx - 3, cy - 6, 10, 1);
  }

  // A big rice paddy: earth dike, water with sky reflections, rows of rice (some fields ripe and golden).
  function paddy(g, x, y, w, h) {
    const r = rnd(x * 7 + y * 13);
    const ripe = r() < 0.35;
    g.fillStyle(0x1a160f, 0.15).fillRect(x + 2, y + h, w, 5);
    g.fillStyle(0x86744f, 1).fillRoundedRect(x, y, w, h, 6);
    g.fillStyle(0x9a875f, 1).fillRect(x + 2, y + 1, w - 4, 4);
    g.fillStyle(0x7f9893, 1).fillRoundedRect(x + 6, y + 7, w - 12, h - 13, 4);
    g.fillStyle(0x96aea9, 1).fillRect(x + 8, y + 9, w - 16, 5);
    // sky reflections and ripples on the water
    for (let i = 0; i < (w * h) / 700; i++) g.fillStyle(0xd3dfdb, 0.4).fillRoundedRect(x + 10 + r() * (w - 50), y + 14 + r() * (h - 30), 18 + r() * 26, 3, 1.5);
    for (let i = 0; i < (w * h) / 1400; i++) g.lineStyle(1, 0xc4d2cd, 0.5).strokeEllipse(x + 20 + r() * (w - 40), y + 20 + r() * (h - 40), 10, 4);
    const GREEN = [0x5f8a3c, 0x72a04a, 0x679444];
    const GOLD = [0xc9a84d, 0xd8bb62, 0xb89640];
    for (let yy = y + 20; yy < y + h - 8; yy += 15)
      for (let xx = x + 14 + (Math.round((yy - y) / 15) % 2) * 6; xx < x + w - 12; xx += 13) {
        const c = ripe ? pick2(r, GOLD) : pick2(r, GREEN);
        // one rice clump: a dark reflection, five blades fanning out, a light tip (ripe ones bow with grain)
        g.fillStyle(0x4a5e5a, 0.4).fillEllipse(xx, yy + 1, 10, 3);
        g.lineStyle(1.8, shade(c, 0.78), 1).lineBetween(xx, yy, xx - 5, yy - 9).lineBetween(xx, yy, xx + 5, yy - 9);
        g.lineStyle(1.8, c, 1).lineBetween(xx, yy, xx - 2, yy - 12).lineBetween(xx, yy, xx + 2, yy - 12).lineBetween(xx, yy, xx, yy - 13);
        g.lineStyle(1.2, shade(c, 1.25), 1).lineBetween(xx - 1, yy - 5, xx - 2, yy - 11);
        if (ripe) {
          g.fillStyle(0xe6cd78, 1).fillEllipse(xx + 4, yy - 11, 4, 6).fillEllipse(xx - 4, yy - 10, 4, 5);
        }
      }
  }
  const pick2 = (r, arr) => arr[Math.floor(r() * arr.length)];

  function tree(g, cx, by) {
    const r = rnd(cx * 3 + by * 5);
    shadow(g, cx + 6, by + 2, 34, 11, 0.24);
    g.fillStyle(0x4b3a26, 1).fillRect(cx - 3, by - 16, 6, 17);
    g.fillStyle(0x5e4a30, 1).fillRect(cx - 3, by - 16, 2, 17);
    const LEAF = [0x45603d, 0x52703f, 0x5e7d48];
    [[0, -24, 15], [-9, -18, 11], [9, -17, 11], [-4, -31, 10], [6, -29, 10]].forEach(([dx, dy, rr], i) => {
      g.fillStyle(LEAF[i % 3], 1).fillCircle(cx + dx, by + dy, rr);
    });
    for (let i = 0; i < 6; i++) g.fillStyle(0x7b9a5c, 0.8).fillCircle(cx - 8 + r() * 12, by - 34 + r() * 14, 2.5);
    g.fillStyle(0x2f4229, 0.35).fillEllipse(cx + 4, by - 12, 22, 8);
  }

  // Watchtower: about four tiles high, ladder, railing, a guard with a helmet and a spear, a thatch roof.
  function tower(g, x, y, w, h) {
    const base = y + h - 4; // feet on the ground
    const P = y - 30; // platform floor
    const cx = x + w / 2;
    shadow(g, cx + 10, base + 2, w + 26, 16, 0.26);
    // back legs (darker), then front legs
    g.fillStyle(0x4a3826, 1).fillRect(x + 14, P, 5, base - P - 6).fillRect(x + w - 19, P, 5, base - P - 6);
    g.fillStyle(0x5e4630, 1).fillRect(x + 6, P, 7, base - P).fillRect(x + w - 13, P, 7, base - P);
    g.fillStyle(0x7a5e3a, 1).fillRect(x + 6, P, 2, base - P).fillRect(x + w - 13, P, 2, base - P);
    // cross braces
    g.lineStyle(3, 0x5e4630, 1);
    g.lineBetween(x + 10, P + 14, x + w - 10, base - 24).lineBetween(x + w - 10, P + 14, x + 10, base - 24);
    g.lineBetween(x + 10, base - 22, x + w - 10, base - 2).lineBetween(x + w - 10, base - 22, x + 10, base - 2);
    // ladder
    g.fillStyle(0x8a6a44, 1).fillRect(cx - 7, P + 8, 2, base - P - 8).fillRect(cx + 5, P + 8, 2, base - P - 8);
    for (let yy = P + 14; yy < base; yy += 7) g.fillStyle(0x8a6a44, 1).fillRect(cx - 7, yy, 14, 2);
    // platform
    g.fillStyle(0x6b5233, 1).fillRect(x - 2, P, w + 4, 10);
    g.fillStyle(0x8f7048, 1).fillRect(x - 2, P, w + 4, 3);
    for (let xx = x; xx < x + w; xx += 8) g.fillStyle(0x5a4429, 1).fillRect(xx, P + 3, 1, 7);
    // guard (behind the railing): boots hidden, tunic, belt, helmet, face, spear
    const gx = cx + 4;
    g.lineStyle(2, 0x6b5a45, 1).lineBetween(gx + 9, P - 2, gx + 9, P - 58);
    g.fillStyle(0xbfc3c4, 1).fillTriangle(gx + 6, P - 56, gx + 12, P - 56, gx + 9, P - 66);
    g.fillStyle(0x5c5246, 1).fillRect(gx - 7, P - 22, 14, 20);
    g.fillStyle(0x7a6c5a, 1).fillRect(gx - 7, P - 22, 14, 4);
    g.fillStyle(0x3d342a, 1).fillRect(gx - 7, P - 11, 14, 2);
    g.fillStyle(0xe2c4a4, 1).fillRect(gx + 7, P - 18, 3, 6);
    g.fillStyle(0xe2c4a4, 1).fillCircle(gx, P - 27, 5.5);
    g.fillStyle(0x8e9294, 1).fillEllipse(gx, P - 31, 14, 8);
    g.fillStyle(0xaeb2b4, 1).fillRect(gx - 8, P - 29, 16, 2);
    g.fillStyle(0x2a241d, 1).fillRect(gx - 2, P - 27, 1.5, 1.5).fillRect(gx + 2, P - 27, 1.5, 1.5);
    // railing in front of the guard
    g.fillStyle(0x6b5233, 1).fillRect(x - 2, P - 14, w + 4, 4);
    g.fillStyle(0x8f7048, 1).fillRect(x - 2, P - 14, w + 4, 1);
    for (let xx = x; xx <= x + w; xx += 11) g.fillStyle(0x5e4630, 1).fillRect(xx - 1, P - 14, 3, 14);
    // roof posts and thatch roof
    g.fillStyle(0x5e4630, 1).fillRect(x, P - 40, 3, 40).fillRect(x + w - 3, P - 40, 3, 40);
    poly(g, 0xb39a5a, 1, [[x - 10, P - 36], [x + w + 10, P - 36], [cx + 4, P - 70], [cx - 4, P - 70]]);
    for (let i = 0; i < 6; i++) {
      const yy = P - 38 - i * 5;
      const half = (w / 2 + 10) * (1 - i / 7);
      g.fillStyle(i % 2 ? 0xc6ad6a : 0xa58c4c, 1).fillRect(cx - half, yy, half * 2, 3);
    }
    g.fillStyle(0x8a7340, 1).fillRect(cx - 5, P - 72, 10, 5);
    g.fillStyle(0x1a160f, 0.22).fillRect(x - 6, P - 36, w + 12, 3);
  }

  // Gold shield with a brown tower: the Oriaris crest (same shape as in oldmap.js).
  function crest(g, cx, cy, s) {
    g.fillStyle(0xd9a93a, 1).fillRect(cx - 4 * s, cy - 4 * s, 8 * s, 5 * s);
    g.fillTriangle(cx - 4 * s, cy + 1 * s, cx + 4 * s, cy + 1 * s, cx, cy + 5 * s);
    g.fillStyle(0x6b4a12, 1).fillRect(cx - 1.5 * s, cy - 2.5 * s, 3 * s, 5 * s);
    g.fillRect(cx - 2.5 * s, cy - 3 * s, 1 * s, 1 * s).fillRect(cx - 0.5 * s, cy - 3 * s, 1 * s, 1 * s).fillRect(cx + 1.5 * s, cy - 3 * s, 1 * s, 1 * s);
  }

  // Shared house parts --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  function door(g, dx, by, wd = 18, ht = 26) {
    g.fillStyle(0x3a2d1f, 1).fillRoundedRect(dx - wd / 2 - 3, by - ht - 3, wd + 6, ht + 3, { tl: 6, tr: 6, bl: 0, br: 0 });
    g.fillStyle(0x6b5233, 1).fillRoundedRect(dx - wd / 2, by - ht, wd, ht, { tl: 5, tr: 5, bl: 0, br: 0 });
    for (let i = 1; i < 3; i++) g.fillStyle(0x553f27, 1).fillRect(dx - wd / 2 + (wd / 3) * i, by - ht + 2, 1, ht - 2);
    g.fillStyle(0x3a3a3a, 1).fillRect(dx - wd / 2, by - ht + 7, wd, 2).fillRect(dx - wd / 2, by - 8, wd, 2);
    g.fillStyle(0xc9a24a, 1).fillCircle(dx + wd / 2 - 4, by - ht / 2, 1.6);
    g.fillStyle(0x8f887a, 1).fillRect(dx - wd / 2 - 4, by, wd + 8, 4);
  }
  function windowBox(g, wx, wy, ww = 14, wh = 12, shutters = true) {
    if (shutters) {
      g.fillStyle(0x5e4630, 1).fillRect(wx - 7, wy - 1, 6, wh + 2).fillRect(wx + ww + 1, wy - 1, 6, wh + 2);
      g.fillStyle(0x4a3826, 1).fillRect(wx - 5, wy + 2, 2, wh - 4).fillRect(wx + ww + 3, wy + 2, 2, wh - 4);
    }
    g.fillStyle(0x4a3826, 1).fillRect(wx - 2, wy - 2, ww + 4, wh + 4);
    g.fillStyle(0x2b3236, 1).fillRect(wx, wy, ww, wh);
    g.fillStyle(0xe8c77a, 0.35).fillRect(wx + 1, wy + 1, ww / 2 - 1, wh / 2 - 1);
    g.fillStyle(0x4a3826, 1).fillRect(wx + ww / 2 - 1, wy, 2, wh).fillRect(wx, wy + wh / 2 - 1, ww, 2);
    g.fillStyle(0x7a5e3a, 1).fillRect(wx - 3, wy + wh + 1, ww + 6, 3);
  }
  function foundation(g, x, y, w) {
    const r = rnd(x + y);
    g.fillStyle(0x6f6a62, 1).fillRect(x - 2, y - 7, w + 4, 8);
    for (let xx = x; xx < x + w; xx += 8 + r() * 6) g.fillStyle(r() < 0.5 ? 0x8e897f : 0x9a958b, 1).fillRoundedRect(xx, y - 6, 7 + r() * 4, 6, 2);
  }
  function chimney(g, cx, top) {
    g.fillStyle(0x6f6a62, 1).fillRect(cx - 6, top, 12, 18);
    g.fillStyle(0x8e897f, 1).fillRect(cx - 6, top, 4, 18);
    g.fillStyle(0x55504a, 1).fillRect(cx - 7, top - 2, 14, 4);
    [[0, -8, 6], [4, -16, 7], [-1, -25, 8], [5, -34, 7]].forEach(([dx, dy, rr], i) => g.fillStyle(0xe8e4dc, 0.28 - i * 0.05).fillCircle(cx + dx, top + dy, rr));
  }

  function building(g, b) {
    const x = b.x * T;
    const y = b.y * T;
    const w = b.w * T;
    const h = b.h * T;
    shadow(g, x + w / 2 + 8, y + h + 2, w + 20, 18, 0.26);
    if (b.kind === 'hall') return hall(g, b, x, y, w, h);
    if (b.kind === 'barn') return barn(g, b, x, y, w, h);
    if (b.kind === 'wood') return woodHouse(g, b, x, y, w, h);
    return thatchHouse(g, b, x, y, w, h);
  }

  // Straw-roofed cottage: thick layered thatch, daub walls with a timber frame, stone footing.
  function thatchHouse(g, b, x, y, w, h) {
    const wallTop = y + h * 0.42;
    const by = y + h;
    // walls
    g.fillStyle(0xddd1b4, 1).fillRect(x + 2, wallTop, w - 4, by - wallTop);
    g.fillStyle(0xcfc2a2, 1).fillRect(x + 2, by - 16, w - 4, 10);
    // timber frame: posts, beam, braces
    g.fillStyle(0x5a4429, 1);
    [x + 2, x + w / 2 - 2, x + w - 6].forEach((px) => g.fillRect(px, wallTop, 4, by - wallTop));
    g.fillRect(x + 2, wallTop + 4, w - 4, 3);
    g.lineStyle(3, 0x5a4429, 1).lineBetween(x + 6, by - 8, x + w / 2 - 2, wallTop + 8).lineBetween(x + w - 6, by - 8, x + w / 2 + 2, wallTop + 8);
    foundation(g, x + 2, by, w - 4);
    // window on the side away from the door
    const dx = b.door * T + 16;
    const winX = dx < x + w / 2 ? x + w - 34 : x + 18;
    windowBox(g, winX, wallTop + 14, 14, 12, true);
    g.fillStyle(0x6f7d4c, 1).fillRect(winX - 2, wallTop + 29, 18, 4);
    g.fillStyle(0xd9cfa0, 1).fillCircle(winX + 2, wallTop + 29, 2).fillCircle(winX + 9, wallTop + 28, 2);
    door(g, dx, by - 2, 18, 26);
    // thatch roof: rounded mass, layered bands with ragged edges, bound ridge
    const ry = y - 10;
    const rh = h * 0.56 + 10;
    g.fillStyle(0x1a160f, 0.25).fillRoundedRect(x - 8, wallTop - 2, w + 16, 10, 5);
    g.fillStyle(0x9a8044, 1).fillRoundedRect(x - 10, ry, w + 20, rh, 18);
    const r = rnd(x * 3 + y);
    const bands = 6;
    for (let i = bands - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
      const yy = ry + 6 + (i * (rh - 10)) / bands;
      const c = i % 2 ? 0xc8ae6c : 0xb79c58;
      g.fillStyle(c, 1).fillRoundedRect(x - 9 + i * 0.5, yy, w + 18 - i, (rh - 10) / bands + 4, 8);
      for (let xx = x - 8; xx < x + w + 8; xx += 5) {
        const len = 4 + r() * 5;
        g.fillStyle(shade(c, 0.8), 1).fillRect(xx, yy + (rh - 10) / bands + 1, 2, len - 2);
        g.lineStyle(1, shade(c, 1.12), 0.9).lineBetween(xx + 1, yy + 2, xx + 1, yy + 6);
      }
    }
    g.fillStyle(0x8a7038, 1).fillRoundedRect(x - 4, ry - 4, w + 8, 10, 5);
    for (let xx = x; xx < x + w; xx += 12) g.fillStyle(0x6b5a30, 1).fillRect(xx, ry - 4, 2, 10);
    g.fillStyle(0xd8c07e, 0.6).fillRoundedRect(x - 2, ry - 3, w + 4, 3, 2);
    chimney(g, x + w - 22, ry - 12);
  }

  // Plank house: vertical boards, a shingle roof with a lit ridge, a little porch roof over the door.
  function woodHouse(g, b, x, y, w, h) {
    const wallTop = y + h * 0.44;
    const by = y + h;
    const PL = [0x7f6142, 0x8a6a48, 0x76593c];
    for (let xx = x + 2; xx < x + w - 2; xx += 7) {
      g.fillStyle(PL[Math.floor(((xx - x) / 7) % 3)], 1).fillRect(xx, wallTop, 7, by - wallTop);
      g.fillStyle(0x4a3826, 1).fillRect(xx + 6, wallTop, 1, by - wallTop);
      g.fillStyle(0x9a7a52, 0.6).fillRect(xx + 1, wallTop, 1, by - wallTop);
    }
    g.fillStyle(0x5a4429, 1).fillRect(x + 2, wallTop, 4, by - wallTop).fillRect(x + w - 6, wallTop, 4, by - wallTop);
    g.fillStyle(0x5a4429, 1).fillRect(x + 2, wallTop + 2, w - 4, 3);
    foundation(g, x + 2, by, w - 4);
    const dx = b.door * T + 16;
    const winX = dx < x + w / 2 ? x + w - 34 : x + 18;
    windowBox(g, winX, wallTop + 13, 14, 12, true);
    door(g, dx, by - 2, 18, 26);
    // porch roof over the door
    poly(g, 0x5d4a3a, 1, [[dx - 18, by - 30], [dx + 18, by - 30], [dx + 14, by - 38], [dx - 14, by - 38]]);
    g.fillStyle(0x4a3826, 1).fillRect(dx - 17, by - 30, 3, 28).fillRect(dx + 14, by - 30, 3, 28);
    // shingle roof
    const ry = y - 8;
    const rh = h * 0.56 + 8;
    g.fillStyle(0x1a160f, 0.25).fillRect(x - 6, wallTop - 2, w + 12, 8);
    g.fillStyle(shade(b.color, 0.8), 1).fillRect(x - 8, ry, w + 16, rh);
    const rows = 7;
    for (let i = 0; i < rows; i++) {
      const yy = ry + 4 + (i * (rh - 6)) / rows;
      const off = i % 2 ? 6 : 0;
      for (let xx = x - 8 - off; xx < x + w + 8; xx += 12) {
        const sx = Math.max(x - 8, xx);
        const ex = Math.min(x + w + 8, xx + 11);
        if (ex <= sx) continue;
        const c = shade(b.color, 0.92 + ((xx * 7 + i * 3) % 5) * 0.05);
        g.fillStyle(c, 1).fillRect(sx, yy, ex - sx, (rh - 6) / rows + 2);
        g.fillStyle(shade(c, 0.7), 1).fillRect(sx, yy + (rh - 6) / rows, ex - sx, 2);
      }
    }
    g.fillStyle(shade(b.color, 0.6), 1).fillRect(x - 8, ry, 3, rh).fillRect(x + w + 5, ry, 3, rh);
    g.fillStyle(shade(b.color, 1.3), 1).fillRect(x - 6, ry - 4, w + 12, 6);
    g.fillStyle(shade(b.color, 0.75), 1).fillRect(x - 6, ry + 1, w + 12, 2);
    chimney(g, x + 20, ry - 10);
  }

  // Big stone hall with an arched door, tall windows, buttresses, a slate roof and a square tower with a banner.
  function hall(g, b, x, y, w, h) {
    const wallTop = y + h * 0.4;
    const by = y + h;
    const r = rnd(4242);
    g.fillStyle(0x8f897d, 1).fillRect(x, wallTop, w, by - wallTop);
    for (let yy = wallTop; yy < by - 4; yy += 9) {
      const off = ((yy - wallTop) / 9) % 2 ? 10 : 0;
      for (let xx = x - off; xx < x + w; xx += 20) {
        const sx = Math.max(x, xx + 1);
        const ex = Math.min(x + w, xx + 19);
        const c = pick2(r, [0xb2ab9e, 0xa9a295, 0xbab4a8]);
        g.fillStyle(c, 1).fillRect(sx, yy + 1, ex - sx, 7);
        g.fillStyle(shade(c, 1.1), 1).fillRect(sx, yy + 1, ex - sx, 1);
      }
    }
    // buttresses
    [x - 4, x + w - 8, x + w / 2 - 50, x + w / 2 + 38].forEach((bx) => {
      g.fillStyle(0x8f897d, 1).fillRect(bx, wallTop + 6, 12, by - wallTop - 6);
      g.fillStyle(0xc2bcb0, 1).fillRect(bx, wallTop + 6, 12, 3);
    });
    // tall arched windows
    [x + 26, x + w - 46].forEach((wx) => {
      g.fillStyle(0x6f6a62, 1).fillRoundedRect(wx - 2, wallTop + 10, 20, 36, { tl: 10, tr: 10, bl: 0, br: 0 });
      g.fillStyle(0x2b3236, 1).fillRoundedRect(wx, wallTop + 12, 16, 32, { tl: 8, tr: 8, bl: 0, br: 0 });
      g.fillStyle(0xe8c77a, 0.3).fillRect(wx + 2, wallTop + 16, 5, 12);
      g.fillStyle(0x6f6a62, 1).fillRect(wx + 7, wallTop + 12, 2, 32).fillRect(wx, wallTop + 28, 16, 2);
    });
    // arched double door with studs, steps
    const dx = b.door * T + 16;
    g.fillStyle(0x6f6a62, 1).fillRoundedRect(dx - 19, by - 44, 38, 44, { tl: 19, tr: 19, bl: 0, br: 0 });
    g.fillStyle(0x4b3a26, 1).fillRoundedRect(dx - 15, by - 40, 30, 40, { tl: 15, tr: 15, bl: 0, br: 0 });
    g.fillStyle(0x3a2d1f, 1).fillRect(dx - 1, by - 38, 2, 38);
    for (let yy = by - 32; yy < by - 2; yy += 8) g.fillStyle(0x2a2a2a, 1).fillCircle(dx - 9, yy, 1.3).fillCircle(dx + 9, yy, 1.3);
    g.fillStyle(0x9a958b, 1).fillRect(dx - 26, by, 52, 4).fillRect(dx - 22, by + 4, 44, 4);
    // slate roof
    const ry = y - 2;
    const rh = h * 0.42 + 6;
    g.fillStyle(0x1a160f, 0.25).fillRect(x - 6, wallTop - 2, w + 12, 7);
    g.fillStyle(0x4c535b, 1).fillRect(x - 8, ry, w + 16, rh);
    for (let i = 0; i < 6; i++) {
      const yy = ry + 2 + i * (rh / 6);
      for (let xx = x - 8 - (i % 2) * 5; xx < x + w + 8; xx += 10) {
        const sx = Math.max(x - 8, xx);
        const ex = Math.min(x + w + 8, xx + 9);
        if (ex <= sx) continue;
        g.fillStyle(pick2(r, [0x5a626b, 0x535b64, 0x626a73]), 1).fillRect(sx, yy, ex - sx, rh / 6);
        g.fillStyle(0x3c4249, 1).fillRect(sx, yy + rh / 6 - 1, ex - sx, 1);
      }
    }
    g.fillStyle(0x7a828a, 1).fillRect(x - 6, ry - 3, w + 12, 4);
    // central tower with crenels, pyramid roof, flag
    const tw = 54;
    const tx = x + w / 2 - tw / 2;
    const tt = y - 52;
    g.fillStyle(0x9a9386, 1).fillRect(tx, tt, tw, ry - tt + 10);
    for (let yy = tt + 4; yy < ry + 8; yy += 9) g.fillStyle(0x8a8377, 1).fillRect(tx, yy, tw, 1);
    g.fillStyle(0xb2ab9e, 1).fillRect(tx, tt, 4, ry - tt + 10);
    for (let i = 0; i < 5; i++) g.fillStyle(0xa39c8f, 1).fillRect(tx + 2 + i * 11, tt - 7, 7, 8);
    g.fillStyle(0x2b3236, 1).fillRoundedRect(tx + tw / 2 - 5, tt + 14, 10, 16, { tl: 5, tr: 5, bl: 0, br: 0 });
    crest(g, tx + tw / 2, tt + 40, 2.2);
    g.fillStyle(0x4b3a26, 1).fillRect(tx + tw / 2 - 1, tt - 40, 3, 34);
    poly(g, 0x7d2e2e, 1, [[tx + tw / 2 + 2, tt - 40], [tx + tw / 2 + 30, tt - 36], [tx + tw / 2 + 24, tt - 30], [tx + tw / 2 + 30, tt - 24], [tx + tw / 2 + 2, tt - 26]]);
    crest(g, tx + tw / 2 + 13, tt - 33, 0.9);
    // banners hanging on the front wall
    [x + 8, x + w - 20].forEach((bx) => {
      poly(g, 0x7d2e2e, 1, [[bx, wallTop + 4], [bx + 12, wallTop + 4], [bx + 12, wallTop + 34], [bx + 6, wallTop + 29], [bx, wallTop + 34]]);
      crest(g, bx + 6, wallTop + 15, 1);
    });
  }

  // Wooden granary: planks, a big X-braced door, a hay loft and a gambrel-style shingle roof.
  function barn(g, b, x, y, w, h) {
    const wallTop = y + h * 0.36;
    const by = y + h;
    for (let xx = x + 2; xx < x + w - 2; xx += 8) {
      g.fillStyle(((xx - x) / 8) % 2 ? 0x8a6a48 : 0x7f6142, 1).fillRect(xx, wallTop, 8, by - wallTop);
      g.fillStyle(0x4a3826, 1).fillRect(xx + 7, wallTop, 1, by - wallTop);
    }
    g.fillStyle(0x5a4429, 1).fillRect(x + 2, wallTop, 5, by - wallTop).fillRect(x + w - 7, wallTop, 5, by - wallTop).fillRect(x + 2, wallTop + 3, w - 4, 3);
    foundation(g, x + 2, by, w - 4);
    const dx = b.door * T + 16;
    g.fillStyle(0x4a3826, 1).fillRect(dx - 24, by - 46, 48, 46);
    g.fillStyle(0x6b5233, 1).fillRect(dx - 22, by - 44, 21, 44).fillRect(dx + 1, by - 44, 21, 44);
    g.lineStyle(3, 0x8f7048, 1).strokeRect(dx - 22, by - 44, 44, 44);
    g.lineBetween(dx - 22, by - 44, dx - 1, by).lineBetween(dx - 1, by - 44, dx - 22, by);
    g.lineBetween(dx + 1, by - 44, dx + 22, by).lineBetween(dx + 22, by - 44, dx + 1, by);
    // hay loft window with hay
    g.fillStyle(0x2e261c, 1).fillRect(dx - 10, wallTop + 4, 20, 14);
    g.fillStyle(0xcdb56c, 1).fillEllipse(dx, wallTop + 16, 18, 8);
    // roof
    const ry = y - 10;
    const rh = h * 0.4 + 10;
    g.fillStyle(0x1a160f, 0.25).fillRect(x - 6, wallTop - 2, w + 12, 7);
    poly(g, 0x5d4a3a, 1, [[x - 10, wallTop], [x + w + 10, wallTop], [x + w + 2, ry + rh * 0.4], [x + w - 20, ry], [x + 20, ry], [x - 2, ry + rh * 0.4]]);
    for (let i = 0; i < 7; i++) {
      const yy = ry + 3 + i * (rh / 7);
      g.lineStyle(2, 0x45372b, 1).lineBetween(x - 8 + (i < 3 ? 12 - i * 4 : 0), yy, x + w + 8 - (i < 3 ? 12 - i * 4 : 0), yy);
    }
    g.fillStyle(0x7d6852, 1).fillRect(x + 18, ry - 3, w - 36, 5);
    // hay bales by the wall
    [[x - 12, by - 6], [x - 4, by - 16]].forEach(([hx, hy]) => {
      g.fillStyle(0xb39a52, 1).fillRoundedRect(hx, hy, 22, 14, 3);
      g.fillStyle(0xcdb56c, 1).fillRect(hx + 2, hy + 2, 18, 4);
      g.fillStyle(0x8a7038, 1).fillRect(hx + 7, hy, 2, 14).fillRect(hx + 14, hy, 2, 14);
    });
  }

  // ---- tall sprites: depth-sorted against the player ----------------------------------------------------------------
  // Each tall thing is baked once into its own texture (translateCanvas moves it to 0,0), then shown as an image.
  // Every frame: if the player's feet are above (north of) the thing's base, the thing is drawn over the player.
  const PLAYER_DEPTH = 10;
  function tallList(a) {
    const out = [];
    a.obstacles.forEach((o, i) => {
      if (!TALL.has(o.kind)) return;
      const x = o.x * T;
      const y = o.y * T;
      const w = o.w * T;
      const h = o.h * T;
      const pad = {
        tower: [16, 96, 16, 16],
        tree: [12, 30, 12, 14],
        well: [14, 40, 14, 22],
        banner: [4, 32, 14, 8],
        torch: [10, 24, 10, 8],
        stall: [10, 24, 10, 10],
        coop: [8, 4, 30, 18],
        rack: [6, 4, 6, 12],
      }[o.kind];
      out.push({ key: `past_o${i}_${o.kind}`, x0: x - pad[0], y0: y - pad[1], x1: x + w + pad[2], y1: y + h + pad[3], base: (o.y + o.h) * T, draw: (g) => drawThing(g, o, a) });
    });
    a.buildings.forEach((b, i) => {
      const x = b.x * T;
      const y = b.y * T;
      out.push({ key: `past_b${i}`, x0: x - 36, y0: y - 104, x1: x + b.w * T + 36, y1: y + b.h * T + 24, base: (b.y + b.h) * T, draw: (g) => building(g, b) });
    });
    return out;
  }
  function buildTall(sc) {
    const items = tallList(sc.area).map((t) => {
      const w = Math.ceil(t.x1 - t.x0);
      const h = Math.ceil(t.y1 - t.y0);
      if (!sc.textures.exists(t.key)) {
        const g = sc.add.graphics();
        g.translateCanvas(-t.x0, -t.y0);
        t.draw(g);
        g.generateTexture(t.key, w, h);
        g.destroy();
      }
      return { img: sc.add.image(t.x0, t.y0, t.key).setOrigin(0).setDepth(PLAYER_DEPTH - 0.6), base: t.base };
    });
    const st = { area: sc.area, items };
    st.update = () => {
      if (sc.area !== st.area) return clearTall(sc);
      if (!sc.player) return;
      const hide = !!sc.inside;
      const py = sc.player.y;
      for (const it of items) {
        it.img.setVisible(!hide);
        it.img.setDepth(py < it.base ? PLAYER_DEPTH + 0.6 : PLAYER_DEPTH - 0.6);
      }
    };
    sc.events.on('update', st.update);
    sc.__lqPastTall = st;
    st.update();
  }
  function clearTall(sc) {
    const st = sc && sc.__lqPastTall;
    if (!st) return;
    sc.events.off('update', st.update);
    st.items.forEach((it) => it.img.destroy());
    sc.__lqPastTall = null;
  }
  function syncTall(sc) {
    const st = sc.__lqPastTall;
    if (here(sc) && !sc.switching) {
      if (st && st.area !== sc.area) clearTall(sc);
      if (!sc.__lqPastTall) {
        try {
          buildTall(sc);
        } catch (e) {
          console.warn('past tall sprites failed', e);
          sc.__lqPastTall = { area: sc.area, items: [], update() {} };
        }
      }
    } else if (st) clearTall(sc);
  }

  // ---- the flock: hens, a rooster and chicks that wander, peck, and run away from the player ------------------------
  // Each group stays near its home (tile cx, cy, radius r). Drawn every frame into one Graphics just below the player.
  const FLOCKS = [
    { cx: 7, cy: 23.5, r: 2.8, hens: 5, rooster: true, chicks: 3 },
    { cx: 25, cy: 19.5, r: 2.6, hens: 3, chicks: 2 },
    { cx: 11, cy: 9.5, r: 2.2, hens: 2 },
    { cx: 21, cy: 29.5, r: 2, hens: 2, chicks: 2 },
  ];
  const HEN_SPEED = 26;
  const RUN_SPEED = 95;
  const SCARE = 46;
  function makeFlock(a) {
    const r = rnd(2024);
    const birds = [];
    const free = (x, y) => !a.isTileBlocked(Math.floor(x / T), Math.floor(y / T));
    const spot = (f) => {
      for (let i = 0; i < 40; i++) {
        const ang = r() * Math.PI * 2;
        const d = Math.sqrt(r()) * f.r * T;
        const x = (f.cx + 0.5) * T + Math.cos(ang) * d;
        const y = (f.cy + 0.5) * T + Math.sin(ang) * d;
        if (free(x, y)) return { x, y };
      }
      return { x: (f.cx + 0.5) * T, y: (f.cy + 0.5) * T };
    };
    FLOCKS.forEach((f) => {
      const add = (kind) => {
        const p = spot(f);
        birds.push({ kind, f, x: p.x, y: p.y, tx: p.x, ty: p.y, state: 'idle', t: r() * 2, face: r() < 0.5 ? -1 : 1, phase: r() * 10, flap: 0, mom: null });
      };
      for (let i = 0; i < f.hens; i++) add('hen');
      if (f.rooster) add('rooster');
      const moms = birds.filter((b) => b.f === f && b.kind === 'hen');
      for (let i = 0; i < (f.chicks || 0); i++) {
        add('chick');
        birds[birds.length - 1].mom = moms[i % moms.length] || null;
      }
    });
    return { birds, free, spot, r };
  }
  function henTick(fl, sc, dt) {
    const who = scarers(sc);
    for (const b of fl.birds) {
      b.t -= dt;
      b.phase += dt * (b.state === 'walk' || b.state === 'run' ? 14 : 3);
      b.flap = Math.max(0, b.flap - dt);
      // The nearest one who scares it: the player, or a running child.
      let px = who[0].x;
      let py = who[0].y;
      for (const s of who) {
        if (Math.hypot(b.x - s.x, b.y - s.y) < Math.hypot(b.x - px, b.y - py)) {
          px = s.x;
          py = s.y;
        }
      }
      const dp = Math.hypot(b.x - px, b.y - py);
      // Scared: run straight away from the player, flapping.
      if (dp < SCARE && b.state !== 'run') {
        const k = 70 / Math.max(1, dp);
        b.tx = b.x + (b.x - px) * k;
        b.ty = b.y + (b.y - py) * k;
        b.state = 'run';
        b.flap = 0.6;
        b.t = 1.2;
      }
      if (b.state === 'walk' || b.state === 'run') {
        const sp = (b.state === 'run' ? RUN_SPEED : HEN_SPEED) * (b.kind === 'chick' ? 1.15 : 1) * dt;
        const dx = b.tx - b.x;
        const dy = b.ty - b.y;
        const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy);
        if (Math.abs(dx) > 0.5) b.face = dx < 0 ? -1 : 1;
        if (d <= sp || b.t <= 0) {
          b.state = fl.r() < 0.6 ? 'peck' : 'idle';
          b.t = 0.8 + fl.r() * 2.2;
        } else {
          const nx = b.x + (dx / d) * sp;
          const ny = b.y + (dy / d) * sp;
          if (fl.free(nx, ny)) {
            b.x = nx;
            b.y = ny;
          } else {
            b.state = 'idle';
            b.t = 0.3;
          }
        }
      } else if (b.t <= 0) {
        // Pick the next place: chicks follow their mother, the others wander around home.
        let p;
        if (b.kind === 'chick' && b.mom) p = { x: b.mom.x + (fl.r() - 0.5) * 30, y: b.mom.y + 6 + (fl.r() - 0.5) * 16 };
        else p = fl.spot(b.f);
        b.tx = p.x;
        b.ty = p.y;
        b.state = 'walk';
        b.t = 6;
        if (b.kind === 'rooster' && fl.r() < 0.15) b.flap = 0.8;
      }
    }
  }
  function drawBird(g, b) {
    const f = b.face;
    const x = b.x;
    const walking = b.state === 'walk' || b.state === 'run';
    const bob = walking ? Math.abs(Math.sin(b.phase)) * 1.6 : 0;
    const y = b.y - bob;
    if (b.kind === 'chick') {
      g.fillStyle(0x1a160f, 0.2).fillEllipse(b.x, b.y + 4, 8, 3);
      g.fillStyle(0xf1d36a, 1).fillCircle(x, y, 3.6);
      g.fillStyle(0xf6df86, 1).fillCircle(x + f * 2.5, y - 3, 2.6);
      g.fillStyle(0xd9822e, 1).fillTriangle(x + f * 4.5, y - 3.5, x + f * 6.5, y - 3, x + f * 4.5, y - 2.3);
      g.fillStyle(0x2a241d, 1).fillRect(x + f * 3 - 0.5, y - 4, 1.2, 1.2);
      return;
    }
    const big = b.kind === 'rooster' ? 1.18 : 1;
    const body = b.kind === 'rooster' ? 0xc0743c : 0xefe9dc;
    const wing = b.kind === 'rooster' ? 0x9a5a2e : 0xd9d2c2;
    const pecking = b.state === 'peck' && Math.sin(b.phase * 2.2) > 0.2;
    g.fillStyle(0x1a160f, 0.2).fillEllipse(b.x, b.y + 6 * big, 14 * big, 4);
    // legs (step when walking)
    const step = walking ? Math.sin(b.phase) * 2 : 0;
    g.lineStyle(1.3, 0xd9a13a, 1).lineBetween(x - 1.5, y + 2, x - 1.5 + step, y + 6 * big).lineBetween(x + 1.5, y + 2, x + 1.5 - step, y + 6 * big);
    // tail
    if (b.kind === 'rooster') {
      g.fillStyle(0x2f4a3a, 1).fillTriangle(x - f * 4, y - 1, x - f * 11, y - 11, x - f * 9, y + 1);
      g.fillStyle(0x23382c, 1).fillTriangle(x - f * 5, y, x - f * 12, y - 6, x - f * 8, y + 3);
    } else g.fillStyle(0xe2dccd, 1).fillTriangle(x - f * 4, y - 1, x - f * 9, y - 7, x - f * 8, y + 1);
    // body + wing (wing lifts when flapping)
    g.fillStyle(body, 1).fillEllipse(x, y, 13 * big, 9 * big);
    if (b.flap > 0) {
      const up = Math.sin(b.flap * 40) * 4;
      g.fillStyle(wing, 1).fillTriangle(x - f * 2, y - 2, x - f * 8, y - 7 - up, x + f * 3, y - 3);
    } else g.fillStyle(wing, 1).fillEllipse(x - f * 1, y + 0.5, 7 * big, 5 * big);
    // head: down to the ground when pecking
    const hx = x + f * (pecking ? 7 : 5) * big;
    const hy = y - (pecking ? -1 : 5) * big;
    g.fillStyle(body === 0xefe9dc ? 0xf7f3ea : 0xd08446, 1).fillCircle(hx, hy, 3.4 * big);
    g.fillStyle(0xc8432f, 1).fillRect(hx - 1.5, hy - 4.6 * big, 3 * big, 2 * big);
    if (b.kind === 'rooster') g.fillStyle(0xc8432f, 1).fillCircle(hx + f * 1, hy - 4.5 * big, 1.6).fillCircle(hx - f * 1, hy - 5 * big, 1.6).fillCircle(hx + f * 2.5, hy + 2.5, 1.3);
    g.fillStyle(0xd9a13a, 1).fillTriangle(hx + f * 3, hy - 0.8, hx + f * 6, hy, hx + f * 3, hy + 1);
    g.fillStyle(0x2a241d, 1).fillRect(hx + f * 1.2 - 0.6, hy - 1.4, 1.3, 1.3);
  }
  function hensUpdate(sc, delta) {
    let st = sc.__lqPastHens;
    if (!here(sc) || sc.switching || !sc.player) {
      if (st) {
        st.g.destroy();
        sc.__lqPastHens = null;
      }
      return;
    }
    if (!st || st.area !== sc.area) {
      if (st) st.g.destroy();
      st = sc.__lqPastHens = { area: sc.area, g: sc.add.graphics().setDepth(PLAYER_DEPTH - 0.5), fl: makeFlock(sc.area) };
    }
    st.g.clear();
    if (sc.inside) return;
    henTick(st.fl, sc, Math.min(0.05, (delta || 16) / 1000));
    [...st.fl.birds].sort((a, b) => a.y - b.y).forEach((b) => drawBird(st.g, b));
  }

  // ---- villagers: men, women and children living their day in the village ----------------------------------------------

  // What they say when the player stops next to them: always the past tense (the topic of the chapter).
  const SAY = {
