  const isKid = (k) => k === 'girl' || k === 'boy' || k === 'kid';
  function folkLook(v, r) {
    const pick2 = (arr) => arr[Math.floor(r() * arr.length)];
    const female = v.kind === 'woman' || v.kind === 'girl' || !!v.dress;
    // Always draw the random picks (even when overridden) so the other villagers keep the same looks.
    const skin = pick2(OLD_SKIN);
    const hair = pick2(OLD_HAIR);
    const outfit = female ? pick2(GOWN) : pick2(TUNIC);
    const pants = pick2(BREECHES);
    return {
      gender: female ? 'female' : 'male',
      skin: v.skin != null ? v.skin : skin,
      hair: v.hair != null ? v.hair : hair,
      style: v.style || 'short',
      eye: 0x3a2a1e,
      outfit: v.outfit != null ? v.outfit : outfit,
      pants: v.pants != null ? v.pants : pants,
      hat: null,
      dress: female,
    };
  }
  // Plate armour (no helmet) for Lord Gusest. The game's character art has no armour, so we draw him with marker colours
  // and repaint every frame: magenta top -> steel (same light and shade), green legs -> dark steel greaves,
  // a gold crest on the chest, and from behind a red cloak over the back.
  const ARMOR_DIRS = ['down', 'up', 'side', 'uside', 'dside'];
  function armorize(sc, key, inPlace) {
    const out = inPlace ? key : `${key}_armor`;
    for (const dir of ARMOR_DIRS) {
      for (let f = 0; f < 3; f++) {
        const src = `ch_${key}_${dir}_${f}`;
        const dst = `ch_${out}_${dir}_${f}`;
        if (!sc.textures.exists(src) || (!inPlace && sc.textures.exists(dst))) continue;
        const img = sc.textures.get(src).getSourceImage();
        const cv = document.createElement('canvas');
        cv.width = img.width;
        cv.height = img.height;
        const cx = cv.getContext('2d');
        cx.drawImage(img, 0, 0);
        const id = cx.getImageData(0, 0, cv.width, cv.height);
        const px = id.data;
        let x0 = 1e9;
        let x1 = -1;
        let y0 = 1e9;
        let y1 = -1;
        const back = dir === 'up';
        for (let k = 0; k < px.length; k += 4) {
          const R = px[k];
          const G = px[k + 1];
          const B = px[k + 2];
          if (!px[k + 3]) continue;
          const top = R > G + 60 && B > G + 60; // magenta marker (any shade)
          const leg = G > R + 60 && G > B + 60; // green marker
          if (!top && !leg) continue;
          const l = (top ? (R + B) / 2 : G) / 255; // keep the shading of the original
          if (top && back) {
            // the cloak, seen from behind
            px[k] = 60 + l * 90;
            px[k + 1] = 22 + l * 26;
            px[k + 2] = 24 + l * 26;
          } else if (top) {
            // darker steel than white cloth, so it still reads as metal under the sepia filter of the village
            px[k] = 58 + l * 78;
            px[k + 1] = 64 + l * 82;
            px[k + 2] = 78 + l * 92;
          } else {
            px[k] = 46 + l * 60;
            px[k + 1] = 50 + l * 62;
            px[k + 2] = 60 + l * 68;
          }
          if (top) {
            const x = (k / 4) % cv.width;
            const y = Math.floor(k / 4 / cv.width);
            x0 = Math.min(x0, x);
            x1 = Math.max(x1, x);
            y0 = Math.min(y0, y);
            y1 = Math.max(y1, y);
          }
        }
        cx.putImageData(id, 0, 0);
        if (x1 >= 0) {
          const w = x1 - x0 + 1;
          if (!back) {
            // the red cloak shows behind the arms, from the shoulders down to the knees
            cx.fillStyle = '#7d2e2e';
            cx.fillRect(x0 - 1, y0 + 1, 1, y1 - y0 + 4);
            cx.fillRect(x1 + 1, y0 + 1, 1, y1 - y0 + 4);
            // plate lines across the breastplate (lames), and a bright rim on top
            cx.fillStyle = '#2b3038';
            for (let y = y0 + 4; y < y1; y += 3) cx.fillRect(x0 + 1, y, w - 2, 1);
            cx.fillStyle = '#e6ebf2';
            cx.fillRect(x0 + 1, y0 + 1, w - 2, 1);
          }
          // pauldrons: round steel plates a little wider than the shoulders
          cx.fillStyle = '#8d96a3';
          cx.fillRect(x0 - 1, y0, 3, 3);
          cx.fillRect(x1 - 1, y0, 3, 3);
          cx.fillStyle = '#dfe5ec';
          cx.fillRect(x0 - 1, y0, 3, 1);
          cx.fillRect(x1 - 1, y0, 3, 1);
          cx.fillStyle = '#2b3038';
          cx.fillRect(x0 - 1, y0 + 3, 3, 1);
          cx.fillRect(x1 - 1, y0 + 3, 3, 1);
          const mx = Math.round((x0 + x1) / 2);
          if (dir === 'down' || dir === 'dside') {
            // the Oriaris crest: a little gold shield with a brown tower
            cx.fillStyle = '#e0b440';
            cx.fillRect(mx - 1, y0 + 2, 3, 3);
            cx.fillRect(mx, y0 + 5, 1, 1);
            cx.fillStyle = '#6b4a2a';
            cx.fillRect(mx, y0 + 3, 1, 2);
          } else if (back) {
            // the cloak hangs down over the legs
            cx.fillStyle = '#5c2224';
            cx.fillRect(x0, y1 + 1, x1 - x0 + 1, 4);
            cx.fillStyle = '#7d2e2e';
            cx.fillRect(x0 + 1, y1 + 1, x1 - x0 - 1, 3);
          }
        }
        if (inPlace) sc.textures.remove(src);
        sc.textures.addCanvas(dst, cv);
      }
    }
    return out;
  }
  // Lord Gusest is a chat NPC (game/02 + Nr.gusest in game/10, drawn in marker colours): put his armour on once.
  function armorNpc(sc) {
    const n = (sc.npcs || []).find((x) => x.def && x.def.id === 'gusest');
    if (!n || !sc.textures.exists('ch_gusest_down_0')) return;
    if (sc.textures.get('ch_gusest_down_0').__lqArmor) return;
    try {
      armorize(sc, 'gusest', true);
      sc.textures.get('ch_gusest_down_0').__lqArmor = true;
      n.sprite.setTexture(n.sprite.texture.key);
    } catch (e) {
      console.warn('Gusest armour failed', e);
    }
  }
  function makeFolk(sc) {
    const C = window.LQCharacter;
    if (!C || !C.ensure) return [];
    const r = rnd(1970);
    const pad = C.pad || 0;
    const oy = (35 + pad) / (38 + pad); // origin at the feet, so a smaller person still stands on the ground
    return FOLK.map((v, i) => {
      let key = C.ensure(sc, folkLook(v, r));
      if (v.armor) {
        try {
          key = armorize(sc, key);
        } catch (e) {
          console.warn('armour failed', e);
        }
      }
      const start = v.home || (v.path && v.path[0]) || FOLK[v.follow].home;
      const p = {
        v, i, key,
        x: start[0] * T, y: start[1] * T, tx: start[0] * T, ty: start[1] * T,
        dir: v.still || 'down', flip: false, walking: false, anim: r() * 1, t: r() * 2,
        leg: 0, it: null, chaser: false, freeze: 0, said: false, bubble: null,
      };
      p.img = sc.add.image(p.x, p.y + FEET, `ch_${key}_down_0`).setOrigin(0.5, oy).setScale(HEIGHT[v.kind] || 1);
      return p;
    });
  }
  function faceTo(p, dx, dy) {
    const ax = Math.abs(dx);
    const ay = Math.abs(dy);
    if (ax < 0.01 && ay < 0.01) return;
    if (ay < ax * 0.4) {
      p.dir = 'side';
      p.flip = dx < 0;
    } else if (ax < ay * 0.4) p.dir = dy < 0 ? 'up' : 'down';
    else {
      p.dir = dy < 0 ? 'uside' : 'dside';
      p.flip = dx < 0;
    }
  }
  function folkSpot(st, home) {
    for (let k = 0; k < 30; k++) {
      const ang = st.r() * Math.PI * 2;
      const d = Math.sqrt(st.r()) * home[2] * T;
      const x = home[0] * T + Math.cos(ang) * d;
      const y = home[1] * T + Math.sin(ang) * d;
      if (st.free(x, y)) return { x, y };
    }
    return { x: home[0] * T, y: home[1] * T };
  }
  // Move p toward (tx, ty). Returns true when it got there (or is stuck).
  function folkStep(st, p, sp, px, py) {
    const dx = p.tx - p.x;
    const dy = p.ty - p.y;
    const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy);
    if (d <= sp) {
      p.x = p.tx;
      p.y = p.ty;
      return true;
    }
    const nx = p.x + (dx / d) * sp;
    const ny = p.y + (dy / d) * sp;
    faceTo(p, dx, dy);
    if (Math.hypot(nx - px, ny - py) < 14) return false; // never walk through the player: wait
    if (!st.free(nx, ny)) return true;
    p.x = nx;
    p.y = ny;
    p.walking = true;
    return false;
  }
  // ---- the working day: children worked too in those days ---------------------------------------------------------------
  // Village clock (game minutes): 05:30–11:30 and 13:00–17:30 planting rice in the south paddies, back in the village at
  // noon and in the evening, and from 19:30 home: they walk to their own door and go inside (hidden) until morning.
  // They go out through the south gate and along the dike, so the routes never cross the wall.
  const HUB = [18.5, 30.5]; // just inside the south gate
  const DOOR_Y = 28.8; // in front of the cottage doors
  function workMode(min) {
    const m = ((min % 1440) + 1440) % 1440;
    if (m < 330 || m >= 1170) return 'home';
    if (m < 690 || (m >= 780 && m < 1050)) return 'field';
    return 'village';
  }
  function workBase(v) {
    if (v.home) return v.home;
    if (v.path) return v.path[0];
    return FOLK[v.follow].home;
  }
  function workRoute(p, from, to) {
    const [door, sx, sy] = p.v.work;
    const base = workBase(p.v);
    const low = (y) => y > 26.5; // south of the cottages: go along the lane behind them
    const out = [];
    if (from === 'field') out.push([sx, 41.5], [18.5, 41.5], [18.5, 33], HUB);
    else if (from === 'home') out.push([door + 0.5, 30.5], HUB);
    else out.push(low(p.y / T) ? [p.x / T, 30.5] : [18.5, 20.5], HUB);
    if (to === 'field') out.push([18.5, 33], [18.5, 41.5], [sx, 41.5], [sx, sy]);
    else if (to === 'home') out.push([door + 0.5, 30.5], [door + 0.5, DOOR_Y]);
    else out.push(low(base[1]) ? [base[0], 30.5] : [18.5, 20.5], [base[0], base[1]]);
    return out;
  }
  // Like folkStep, but on a planned route (gates, dikes): no wall check. Still never walks through the player.
  function workStep(p, sp, px, py) {
    const dx = p.tx - p.x;
    const dy = p.ty - p.y;
    const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy);
    if (d <= sp) {
      p.x = p.tx;
      p.y = p.ty;
      return true;
    }
    faceTo(p, dx, dy);
    const nx = p.x + (dx / d) * sp;
    const ny = p.y + (dy / d) * sp;
    if (Math.hypot(nx - px, ny - py) < 14) return false;
    p.x = nx;
    p.y = ny;
    p.walking = true;
    return false;
  }
  // Returns true when the schedule moved this villager (their usual village life is skipped this frame).
  function workTick(sc, p, dt, px, py) {
    const v = p.v;
    if (!v.work) return false;
    const g = sc.store && sc.store.game;
    const want = workMode(g && typeof g.minutes === 'number' ? g.minutes : 480);
    const [door, sx, sy] = v.work;
    if (!p.mode) {
      // First frame in the village: put them where the clock says, without walking there.
      p.mode = want;
      if (want === 'field') {
        p.x = p.tx = sx * T;
        p.y = p.ty = sy * T;
      } else if (want === 'home') {
        p.x = p.tx = (door + 0.5) * T;
        p.y = p.ty = DOOR_Y * T;
        p.gone = true;
      }
    }
    if (!p.route && p.mode !== want) {
      p.route = workRoute(p, p.mode, want);
      p.goal = want;
      p.gone = false; // morning: they come out of their door
      p.chaser = false;
    }
    p.away = !!p.route || p.mode !== 'village';
    if (p.route) {
      const pt = p.route[0];
      p.tx = pt[0] * T;
      p.ty = pt[1] * T;
      if (workStep(p, (WALK[v.kind] || 32) * dt, px, py)) {
        p.route.shift();
        if (!p.route.length) {
          p.route = null;
          p.mode = p.goal;
          if (p.mode === 'home') p.gone = true;
          if (p.mode === 'village') {
            p.away = false;
            p.leg = 0;
            p.t = 0;
          }
        }
      }
      return true;
    }
    if (p.mode === 'home') return true;
    if (p.mode === 'field') {
      // Planting: step slowly along the row, bending down again and again to push the seedlings in.
      p.plant = (p.plant || 0) + dt;
      p.x = sx * T + Math.sin(p.plant * 0.25 + p.i) * 0.9 * T;
      p.y = sy * T;
      p.dir = 'down';
      p.planting = true;
      return true;
    }
    return false;
  }

  function folkTick(st, sc, dt) {
    const px = sc.player.x;
    const py = sc.player.y;
    const folk = st.folk;
    const taggers = folk.filter((p) => p.v.play === 'tag' && !p.away);
    if (taggers.length && !taggers.some((p) => p.chaser)) taggers[0].chaser = true;
    for (const p of folk) {
      const v = p.v;
      p.walking = false;
      p.planting = false;
      if (v.after) {
        // Only home after the Day of Glory (the young soldier).
        const d = data();
        p.gone = !(d && d.done);
        if (p.gone) continue;
      }
      p.t -= dt;
      p.freeze = Math.max(0, p.freeze - dt);
      if (p.gone && p.mode === 'home' && workMode(sc.store.game.minutes) === 'home') continue;
      const dp = Math.hypot(p.x - px, p.y - py);
      // Grown-ups stop and look at the player who comes close; children keep playing.
      if (!isKid(v.kind) && dp < 48) {
        faceTo(p, px - p.x, py - p.y);
        continue;
      }
      if (workTick(sc, p, dt, px, py)) continue;
      if (v.play === 'tag') {
        const home = v.home;
        const sp = TAG_RUN * dt;
        if (p.freeze > 0) continue;
        if (p.chaser) {
          // Run after the nearest other child; touching them makes them "it".
          let best = null;
          let bd = 1e9;
          for (const o of taggers) {
            if (o === p || o.freeze > 0) continue;
            const d = Math.hypot(o.x - p.x, o.y - p.y);
            if (d < bd) {
              bd = d;
              best = o;
            }
          }
          if (!best) continue;
          if (bd < 11) {
            p.chaser = false;
            best.chaser = true;
            best.freeze = 1.1; // the new "it" counts a little before running
            p.freeze = 0.4;
            continue;
          }
          p.tx = best.x;
          p.ty = best.y;
          folkStep(st, p, sp * 0.92, px, py);
        } else {
          const ch = taggers.find((o) => o.chaser);
          const dc = ch ? Math.hypot(ch.x - p.x, ch.y - p.y) : 1e9;
          if (ch && dc < 56 && ch.freeze <= 0) {
            // Run away from "it", but stay in the square.
            let tx = p.x + ((p.x - ch.x) / Math.max(1, dc)) * 40;
            let ty = p.y + ((p.y - ch.y) / Math.max(1, dc)) * 40;
            if (Math.hypot(tx - home[0] * T, ty - home[1] * T) > home[2] * T || !st.free(tx, ty)) {
              const s = folkSpot(st, home);
              tx = s.x;
              ty = s.y;
            }
            if (p.t <= 0 || Math.hypot(p.tx - p.x, p.ty - p.y) < 4) {
              p.tx = tx;
              p.ty = ty;
              p.t = 0.6;
            }
            folkStep(st, p, sp, px, py);
          } else {
            if (p.t <= 0 || Math.hypot(p.tx - p.x, p.ty - p.y) < 3) {
              const s = folkSpot(st, home);
              p.tx = s.x;
              p.ty = s.y;
              p.t = 1 + st.r() * 1.5;
            }
            folkStep(st, p, sp * 0.55, px, py);
          }
        }
        continue;
      }
      const sp = (WALK[v.kind] || 32) * dt;
      if (v.follow != null) {
        // A little one next to her mother: a step behind and to the side.
        const m = folk[v.follow];
        p.tx = m.x + 10;
        p.ty = m.y + 6;
        if (Math.hypot(p.tx - p.x, p.ty - p.y) > 6) folkStep(st, p, sp * 1.3, px, py);
        else faceTo(p, m.x - p.x, m.y - p.y);
        continue;
      }
      if (v.path) {
        if (p.leg == null) p.leg = 0;
        if (p.t > 0) continue; // a short pause at each point
        const pt = v.path[p.leg];
        p.tx = pt[0] * T;
        p.ty = pt[1] * T;
        if (folkStep(st, p, sp, px, py)) {
          p.leg = (p.leg + 1) % v.path.length;
          p.t = 0.6 + st.r() * 1.8;
        }
        continue;
      }
      if (v.still) {
        // Stands at the spot (comes back to it if it was pushed away) and looks the same way.
        p.tx = v.home[0] * T;
        p.ty = v.home[1] * T;
        if (Math.hypot(p.tx - p.x, p.ty - p.y) > 2) folkStep(st, p, sp, px, py);
        else p.dir = v.still;
        continue;
      }
      // Wander around home, with pauses.
      if (p.t > 0 && Math.hypot(p.tx - p.x, p.ty - p.y) < 1) continue;
      if (p.t <= 0 && Math.hypot(p.tx - p.x, p.ty - p.y) < 1) {
        const s = folkSpot(st, v.home);
        p.tx = s.x;
        p.ty = s.y;
        p.t = 8;
      }
      if (folkStep(st, p, sp, px, py)) p.t = 1.5 + st.r() * 4;
    }
  }
  // Depth: in front of / behind the player, and behind a house or tree when standing north of its base.
  function folkDepth(sc, p) {
    let dep = (p.y < sc.player.y ? PLAYER_DEPTH - 0.1 : PLAYER_DEPTH + 0.1) + p.y * 1e-5;
    const tall = sc.__lqPastTall && sc.__lqPastTall.items;
    if (tall) {
      for (const it of tall) {
        const im = it.img;
        if (p.x < im.x || p.x > im.x + im.width || p.y + FEET < im.y || p.y > it.base + 24) continue;
        if (p.y < it.base) dep = Math.min(dep, im.depth - 0.05);
        else dep = Math.max(dep, im.depth + 0.05);
      }
    }
    return dep;
  }
  function folkSay(sc, st, p) {
    const d = data();
    const peace = !!(d && d.done);
    const v = p.v;
    const set = v.say
      ? (peace && SAY[v.say + 'Peace']) || SAY[v.say]
      : v.mother
        ? peace
          ? SAY.motherPeace
          : SAY.mother
        : peace
          ? SAY.peace
          : SAY[isKid(v.kind) ? 'kid' : v.kind];
    const line = set[(p.i + (st.talks = (st.talks || 0) + 1)) % set.length];
    if (p.bubble) p.bubble.destroy();
    p.bubble = sc.add
      .text(p.x, p.y - 30, `${v.name ? v.name + ': ' : ''}${line[0]}\n${line[1]}`.normalize('NFC'), {
        // not Georgia: it has no Vietnamese tone marks, the browser then stacks them apart ("chiê ́n")
        fontFamily: "'Segoe UI', Arial, sans-serif",
        fontSize: '10px',
        color: '#2a241d',
        align: 'center',
        backgroundColor: '#f3ead8',
        padding: { x: 6, y: 4 },
        wordWrap: { width: 190 },
      })
      .setOrigin(0.5, 1)
      .setDepth(PLAYER_DEPTH + 2)
      .setResolution(2);
    p.bubbleT = 4.5;
  }
  function folkUpdate(sc, delta) {
    let st = sc.__lqPastFolk;
    const kill = () => {
      if (!st) return;
      st.folk.forEach((p) => {
        p.img.destroy();
        if (p.bubble) p.bubble.destroy();
      });
      sc.__lqPastFolk = null;
    };
    if (!here(sc) || sc.switching || !sc.player) return kill();
    if (!st || st.area !== sc.area) {
      kill();
      const a = sc.area;
      st = sc.__lqPastFolk = { area: a, folk: [], r: rnd(29), free: (x, y) => !a.isTileBlocked(Math.floor(x / T), Math.floor(y / T)) };
      try {
        st.folk = makeFolk(sc);
      } catch (e) {
        console.warn('past villagers failed', e);
      }
    }
    const hide = !!sc.inside || filming;
    const dt = Math.min(0.05, (delta || 16) / 1000);
    if (!hide) folkTick(st, sc, dt);
    for (const p of st.folk) {
      p.img.setVisible(!hide && !p.gone);
      if (hide || p.gone) {
        if (p.bubble) p.bubble.setVisible(false);
        continue;
      }
      // Planting rice: bend down and up (origin is at the feet, so they stay on the ground).
      const h = HEIGHT[p.v.kind] || 1;
      p.img.setScale(h, p.planting ? h * (0.9 + 0.06 * Math.sin(p.plant * 2.4 + p.i)) : h);
      // Walk frames: 1 and 2 while moving (children step faster), 0 when standing.
      p.anim += dt * (p.walking ? (isKid(p.v.kind) ? 9 : 6) : 0);
      const frame = p.walking ? 1 + (Math.floor(p.anim) % 2) : 0;
      const tex = `ch_${p.key}_${p.dir}_${frame}`;
      if (p.img.texture.key !== tex) p.img.setTexture(tex);
      p.img.setFlipX(p.flip && p.dir !== 'down' && p.dir !== 'up');
      p.img.setPosition(p.x, p.y + FEET);
      p.img.setDepth(folkDepth(sc, p));
      // Talk once each time the player comes close (and stays a moment).
      const dp = Math.hypot(p.x - sc.player.x, p.y - sc.player.y);
      if (dp < 48) {
        p.near = (p.near || 0) + dt;
        if (!p.said && p.near > 0.5) {
          p.said = true;
          folkSay(sc, st, p);
        }
      } else if (dp > 70) {
        p.said = false;
        p.near = 0;
      }
      if (p.bubble) {
        p.bubbleT -= dt;
        if (p.bubbleT <= 0) {
          p.bubble.destroy();
          p.bubble = null;
        } else p.bubble.setVisible(true).setPosition(p.x, p.y + FEET - 38 * (HEIGHT[p.v.kind] || 1)).setAlpha(Math.min(1, p.bubbleT * 2));
      }
    }
  }
  // Children (and the player) scare the hens.
  function scarers(sc) {
    const out = [{ x: sc.player.x, y: sc.player.y }];
    const st = sc.__lqPastFolk;
    if (st) st.folk.forEach((p) => isKid(p.v.kind) && p.walking && out.push({ x: p.x, y: p.y }));
    return out;
  }

  // ---- runtime ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  const getScene = () => (typeof window.__lifequestGetScene === 'function' ? window.__lifequestGetScene() : null);
  const toast = (title, sub, hint) => {
    try {
      window.LQAchievements && window.LQAchievements.toast && window.LQAchievements.toast(title, sub, hint);
    } catch (e) {}
  };
  function data() {
    const sc = getScene();
    const g = sc && sc.store && sc.store.game;
    if (!g) return null;
    if (!g.ach || typeof g.ach !== 'object') g.ach = { steps: {}, done: {} };
    if (!g.ach.past || typeof g.ach.past !== 'object') g.ach.past = { visits: 0, read: {} };
    if (!g.ach.past.read) g.ach.past.read = {};
    return g.ach.past;
  }
  const save = () => {
    try {
      const sc = getScene();
      sc && sc.store && sc.store.save();
    } catch (e) {}
  };
  const here = (sc) => !!(sc && sc.area && sc.area.id === ID);
  // The village, or the North Trail / North Camp (js/front.js): same time period, same pale filter, same calendar.
  const inPast = (sc) => here(sc) || !!(sc && sc.area && window.LQFront && window.LQFront.ids.includes(sc.area.id));

  // Pale colours while the player is in the past.
  // The game canvas and the minimap (and any other small canvas the HUD draws), not the cutscene canvases.
  let filtered = false;
  const PALE = 'grayscale(.62) sepia(.32) saturate(.9) contrast(1.02) brightness(1.03)';
  function applyFilter(sc, seconds) {
    const cv = sc && sc.game && sc.game.canvas;
    if (!cv) return;
    const want = inPast(sc) && !colorBack;
    if (want === filtered) return;
    filtered = want;
    document.querySelectorAll('canvas').forEach((c) => {
      if (c.closest('#lq-oldmap, #lq-intro, #lq-shrine')) return;
      c.style.transition = `filter ${seconds || 1.2}s ease`;
      c.style.filter = want ? PALE : '';
    });
  }
  let colorBack = false;
  // Colours back (Day 14 ending, and every visit after the chapter is done). `seconds`: how slowly they come back.
  function setColor(on, seconds) {
    colorBack = !!on;
    const sc = getScene();
    if (!sc) return;
    filtered = !(inPast(sc) && !colorBack);
    applyFilter(sc, seconds);
  }

  // Signs: walking up to one shows its text (again after you walk away).
  // Inside a room the same works with NOTES (room tiles are scaled by ins.k, like fingers.js / tenshadows.js).
  let near = null;
  function nearestText(sc) {
    const ins = sc.inside;
    if (!ins) return SIGNS.find((s) => Math.hypot(sc.player.x - (s.x * 32 + 16), sc.player.y - (s.y * 32 + 16)) < READ_RADIUS);
    if (ins.floor !== 0 || !ins.objs || !ins.objs[0]) return null;
    const unit = 32 * ins.k;
    return NOTES.find((n) => {
      if (n.room !== ins.b.name) return false;
      const x = ins.objs[0].x + (n.x + 0.5) * unit;
      const y = ins.objs[0].y + (n.y + 0.5) * unit;
      return Math.hypot(sc.player.x - x, sc.player.y - y) < 1.9 * unit;
    });
  }
  function checkSigns(sc) {
    if (!here(sc) || filming || !sc.player) {
      near = null;
      return;
    }
    const s = nearestText(sc);
    if (!s) {
      near = null;
      return;
    }
    if (near === s.id) return;
    near = s.id;
    const d = data();
    if (d && !d.read[s.id]) {
      d.read[s.id] = Date.now();
      save();
    }
    const n = d ? [...SIGNS, ...NOTES].filter((x) => d.read[x.id]).length : 0;
    toast(s.title, s.en, `${s.vi} · ${n}/${ALL_READ()}`);
  }

  // ---- lead-in film (in the real map) ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  const CSS = `
body.lq-filming>:not(#lq-past):not(:has(canvas)){visibility:hidden!important}
body.lq-filming>:has(canvas) :not(canvas){visibility:hidden}
body.lq-filming canvas:not(.lq-main){visibility:hidden!important}
body.lq-filming canvas.lq-main{visibility:visible!important}
#lq-past{position:fixed;inset:0;z-index:10040;pointer-events:none;font-family:system-ui,'Segoe UI',sans-serif}
#lq-past .lb{position:absolute;left:0;right:0;height:10vh;background:#000;transition:transform .8s ease}
#lq-past .lb.t{top:0}#lq-past .lb.b{bottom:0}
#lq-past.out .lb.t{transform:translateY(-100%)}#lq-past.out .lb.b{transform:translateY(100%)}
#lq-past .cap{position:absolute;left:50%;bottom:calc(10vh + 12px);transform:translateX(-50%);width:min(760px,calc(100vw - 32px));box-sizing:border-box;background:#1c1c1ce8;border:2px solid #b9b2a4;border-radius:14px;padding:12px 18px;color:#f2f2f2;opacity:0;transition:opacity .35s;pointer-events:auto;cursor:pointer}
#lq-past .cap.show{opacity:1}
#lq-past .who{font:800 12px system-ui,sans-serif;color:#e0c27a;letter-spacing:.04em;margin-bottom:3px}
#lq-past .en{margin:0;font:600 clamp(16px,2.4vw,20px)/1.4 Georgia,'Times New Roman',serif}
#lq-past .vi{margin:5px 0 0;font:14px/1.4 system-ui,sans-serif;color:#b8b8b8}
#lq-past .hint{font-size:11px;color:#8d8d8d;text-align:right;margin-top:4px}
#lq-past .skip{position:absolute;top:calc(10vh + 10px);right:14px;background:#0009;color:#fff;border:1px solid #ffffff55;border-radius:999px;padding:7px 14px;font:700 13px system-ui,sans-serif;cursor:pointer;pointer-events:auto}
#lq-past .card{position:absolute;left:50%;top:38%;transform:translate(-50%,-50%);text-align:center;color:#eee;text-shadow:0 2px 12px #000;opacity:0;transition:opacity .8s;white-space:nowrap;padding:30px 60px;background:radial-gradient(closest-side,#000a,#0000)}
#lq-past .card.show{opacity:1}
#lq-past .card small{display:block;font:700 13px system-ui,sans-serif;letter-spacing:.35em;color:#d6d0c4;text-transform:uppercase}
#lq-past .card b{display:block;font:800 clamp(28px,5.5vw,48px)/1.1 Georgia,'Times New Roman',serif;margin:6px 0}
#lq-past .card span{font:600 14px system-ui,sans-serif;color:#cfcfcf}
`;
  // who: '' = narrator. cam: tile to look at. act: what happens in the scene at this line.
  const FILM = [
    { cam: [27, 15], zoom: 1, en: 'The village of Oriaris. The colors were pale, and the days were hard.', vi: 'Làng Oriaris. Màu sắc nhợt nhạt, và những ngày tháng thật vất vả.', act: 'crowd' },
    { cam: [30, 9], zoom: 1.35, who: 'Lord Gusest', en: 'People of Oriaris! This war will pass. Peace will come back.', vi: 'Hỡi người dân Oriaris! Cuộc chiến này rồi sẽ qua. Hòa bình sẽ trở lại.', act: 'gusest' },
    { cam: [30, 9], zoom: 1.35, who: 'Lord Gusest', en: 'Our knights are brave. They will win!', vi: 'Các kỵ sĩ của chúng ta rất dũng cảm. Họ sẽ chiến thắng!' },
    { cam: [28, 12], zoom: 1.15, en: 'The people cheered. For a moment, nobody was afraid.', vi: 'Dân làng reo hò. Trong khoảnh khắc ấy, không ai còn sợ hãi.', act: 'cheer' },
    { cam: [28, 18], zoom: 1, en: 'Lord Gusest left, but he sent food to every family.', vi: 'Lãnh chúa Gusest rời đi, nhưng ngài đã gửi lương thực cho từng gia đình.', act: 'leave' },
    { cam: null, zoom: 1, en: 'Now the village waits. Live here, day by day, until the war is over.', vi: 'Giờ đây cả làng chờ đợi. Hãy sống ở đây, từng ngày một, cho đến khi chiến tranh kết thúc.', act: 'end' },
  ];
  let filming = false;
  function drawPerson(g, x, y, cloth, hair, cape) {
    g.fillStyle(0x000000, 0.25).fillEllipse(x, y + 13, 14, 5);
    if (cape) g.fillStyle(cape, 1).fillRect(x - 7, y - 4, 14, 17);
    g.fillStyle(cloth, 1).fillRect(x - 5, y - 4, 10, 14);
    g.fillStyle(0x3b3127, 1).fillRect(x - 4, y + 10, 3, 4).fillRect(x + 1, y + 10, 3, 4);
    g.fillStyle(0xe2c4a4, 1).fillCircle(x, y - 9, 5);
    g.fillStyle(hair, 1).fillRect(x - 5, y - 15, 10, 4);
  }
  // ---- film player (shared by the lead-in and the Day 14 ending) ------------------------------------------------------
  // opts: { card: {small, big, sub}, lines: [{cam, zoom, who, en, vi, act}], setup(sc, actors) -> {tick(time), act(name)} }
  function ensureCss() {
    if (document.getElementById('lq-past-css')) return;
    const st = document.createElement('style');
    st.id = 'lq-past-css';
    st.textContent = CSS + DAY_CSS;
    document.head.appendChild(st);
  }
  function runFilm(opts) {
    const sc = getScene();
    if (!sc || !here(sc) || filming) return Promise.resolve(false);
    filming = true;
    ensureCss();
    closeDayCard();
    return new Promise((resolve) => {
      const cam = sc.cameras.main;
      const zoom0 = cam.zoom;
      const kb = sc.input && sc.input.keyboard;
      if (kb) {
        kb.enabled = false;
        try {
          kb.resetKeys();
        } catch (e) {}
      }
      cam.stopFollow();
      const actors = sc.add.graphics().setDepth(9.6);
      const scene = opts.setup ? opts.setup(sc, actors) : {};
      const tick = (time) => {
        actors.clear();
        if (scene.tick) scene.tick(time);
      };
      sc.events.on('update', tick);
      const lines = opts.lines;
      const root = document.createElement('div');
      root.id = 'lq-past';
      root.innerHTML =
        '<div class="lb t"></div><div class="lb b"></div>' +
        `<div class="card"><small>${opts.card.small}</small><b>${opts.card.big}</b><span>${opts.card.sub}</span></div>` +
        '<div class="cap"><div class="who"></div><p class="en"></p><p class="vi"></p><div class="hint">Click / Space ▶</div></div>' +
        '<button class="skip" type="button">Skip ⏭</button>';
      document.body.appendChild(root);
      if (sc.game && sc.game.canvas) sc.game.canvas.classList.add('lq-main');
      document.body.classList.add('lq-filming');
      const card = root.querySelector('.card');
      const cap = root.querySelector('.cap');
      const who = root.querySelector('.who');
      const en = root.querySelector('.en');
      const vi = root.querySelector('.vi');
      let i = -1;
      let typing = 0;
      let full = '';
      let ended = false;
      const look = (shot, ms) => {
        if (shot.cam) cam.pan(shot.cam[0] * 32 + 16, shot.cam[1] * 32 + 16, ms, 'Sine.easeInOut', true);
        else cam.pan(sc.player.x, sc.player.y, ms, 'Sine.easeInOut', true);
        cam.zoomTo(zoom0 * (shot.zoom || 1), ms, 'Sine.easeInOut', true);
      };
      const show = () => {
        const s = lines[i];
        look(s, i === 0 ? 1 : 1400);
        if (s.act && scene.act) scene.act(s.act);
        who.textContent = s.who || '';
        who.style.display = s.who ? '' : 'none';
        vi.textContent = s.vi;
        full = s.en;
        en.textContent = '';
        clearInterval(typing);
        let n = 0;
        typing = setInterval(() => {
          n += 1;
          en.textContent = full.slice(0, n);
          if (n >= full.length) clearInterval(typing);
        }, 28);
      };
      const next = () => {
        if (ended) return;
        if (i >= 0 && en.textContent.length < full.length) {
          clearInterval(typing);
          en.textContent = full;
          return;
        }
        i += 1;
        if (i >= lines.length) return finish();
        show();
      };
      const finish = () => {
        if (ended) return;
        ended = true;
        clearInterval(typing);
        window.removeEventListener('keydown', onKey, true);
        // Skipping still runs every remaining action once (e.g. the colours come back), so the story state is right.
        if (scene.act) for (let k = i + 1; k < lines.length; k++) if (lines[k].act) scene.act(lines[k].act, true);
        root.classList.add('out');
        document.body.classList.remove('lq-filming');
        cap.classList.remove('show');
        card.classList.remove('show');
        sc.events.off('update', tick);
        actors.destroy();
        cam.zoomTo(zoom0, 600, 'Sine.easeInOut', true);
        cam.startFollow(sc.player, true, 0.1, 0.1);
        if (kb) kb.enabled = true;
        setTimeout(() => {
          root.remove();
          filming = false;
          resolve(true);
        }, 850);
      };
      const onKey = (e) => {
        if (e.key === ' ' || e.key === 'Enter') {
          e.preventDefault();
          next();
        } else if (e.key === 'Escape') {
          e.preventDefault();
          finish();
        }
      };
      window.addEventListener('keydown', onKey, true);
      cap.addEventListener('click', next);
      root.querySelector('.skip').addEventListener('click', finish);
      const first = lines[0].cam || [sc.player.x / 32, sc.player.y / 32];
      cam.centerOn(first[0] * 32 + 16, first[1] * 32 + 16);
      if (scene.act && lines[0].act) scene.act(lines[0].act);
      card.classList.add('show');
      setTimeout(() => {
        if (ended) return;
        card.classList.remove('show');
        cap.classList.add('show');
        next();
      }, 2600);
    });
  }

  // ---- lead-in film --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  function play() {
    return runFilm({
      card: { small: 'Chapter 2', big: '70 Years Ago', sub: 'Chương 2 · 70 năm trước' },
      lines: FILM,
      setup(sc, actors) {
        const RC = rnd(42);
        const crowd = [];
        for (let i = 0; i < 16; i++)
          crowd.push({
            x: (22.5 + (i % 8) * 1.25 + RC() * 0.5) * 32,
            y: (11.6 + Math.floor(i / 8) * 1.3 + RC() * 0.4) * 32,
            c: [0x7a6a58, 0x6b7280, 0x8a7a5a, 0x5d6b5a][i % 4],
            h: [0x3b2a1e, 0x6b5a45, 0x2a2a2a, 0x8a7a6a][(i * 3) % 4],
          });
        const gus = { x: 30.5 * 32, y: 8.1 * 32, on: false, away: 0, t0: 0 };
        let cheer = 0;
        let showCrowd = false;
        return {
          tick(time) {
            if (showCrowd)
              crowd.forEach((p, i) => drawPerson(actors, p.x, p.y - (cheer ? Math.abs(Math.sin(time * 0.012 + i)) * 5 : 0), p.c, p.h));
            if (gus.t0) gus.away = Math.min(1, (performance.now() - gus.t0) / 1600);
            if (gus.on && gus.away < 1) {
              const gy = gus.y - gus.away * 64;
              drawPerson(actors, gus.x, gy, 0x2f3540, 0x4a2e1c, 0x7d2e2e); // dark brown hair: Gusest was only 20
              crest(actors, gus.x, gy - 1, 0.9);
            }
          },
          act(a, skipping) {
            if (a === 'crowd') showCrowd = true;
            if (a === 'gusest') gus.on = true;
            if (a === 'cheer') cheer = 1;
            if (a === 'leave') {
              cheer = 0;
              gus.t0 = performance.now();
              if (!skipping) toast('🍞 Food for every family', 'Bread and rice', 'Bánh mì và gạo cho mọi nhà');
            }
            if (a === 'end') showCrowd = false;
          },
        };
      },
    }).then((ran) => {
      if (!ran) return;
      startChapter();
      const t = todayTask();
      toast('📜 Day 1 / 14', t ? 'Follow the golden light: ' + t.where + '.' : 'Oriaris Village', 'Mỗi ngày đi tới ánh sáng vàng, đọc chuyện hôm nay, rồi về ngủ ở Brown Family Home.');
    });
  }

  // ---- the fourteen days -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  // Each day (1–13): a golden light appears somewhere; there the player reads what happened that day (past tense),
  // learns its past verbs and answers one question. Then they sleep in the Brown Family Home (bed, E) to start the
  // next day. Waking up on day 14 plays the ending (the young soldier runs in with the flag, the colours come back).
  // opts[0] is the right answer (the card shuffles them).
  const DAYS = [
    { at: [25, 17], where: 'the village well', en: 'On the first day, the women carried water from the well to every house.', vi: 'Ngày đầu tiên, các bà các chị gánh nước từ giếng về từng nhà.', verbs: [['carried', 'carry']], q: 'Where did the water come from?', opts: ['the well', 'the river', 'the lake'] },
    { at: [45, 17], where: 'the east rice fields', en: 'On day two, the children helped in the rice fields. They worked until noon.', vi: 'Ngày thứ hai, bọn trẻ ra đồng giúp cấy lúa. Chúng làm đến trưa.', verbs: [['helped', 'help'], ['worked', 'work']], q: 'When did the children stop working?', opts: ['at noon', 'at night', 'early in the morning'] },
    { at: [18, 29], where: 'the south gate', en: 'On day three, the men repaired the south wall. They carried big stones all day.', vi: 'Ngày thứ ba, đàn ông sửa bức tường phía nam. Họ khiêng đá lớn cả ngày.', verbs: [['repaired', 'repair'], ['carried', 'carry']], q: 'What did the men repair?', opts: ['the south wall', 'the well', 'the hall'] },
    { at: [8, 8], where: 'the granary', en: "On day four, Lord Gusest's soldiers brought rice to the granary.", vi: 'Ngày thứ tư, lính của Lãnh chúa Gusest chở gạo đến kho thóc.', verbs: [['brought', 'bring']], q: 'What did the soldiers bring?', opts: ['rice', 'bread', 'water'] },
    { at: [45, 11], where: 'the east gate', en: 'On day five, a guard saw smoke in the north. Everyone was afraid.', vi: 'Ngày thứ năm, một lính gác thấy khói ở phía bắc. Ai cũng sợ hãi.', verbs: [['saw', 'see'], ['was', 'be']], q: 'What did the guard see?', opts: ['smoke', 'a bird', 'a horse'] },
    { at: [29, 17], where: 'the market stall', en: 'On day six, the baker made bread for everyone. Nobody paid for it.', vi: 'Ngày thứ sáu, bác thợ bánh làm bánh mì cho mọi người. Không ai phải trả tiền.', verbs: [['made', 'make'], ['paid', 'pay']], q: 'Who made the bread?', opts: ['the baker', 'the farmer', 'the guard'] },
    { at: [30, 8], where: 'Oriaris Hall', en: 'On day seven, a messenger came with a letter. The knights were still fighting.', vi: 'Ngày thứ bảy, một người đưa tin mang thư đến. Các kỵ sĩ vẫn đang chiến đấu.', verbs: [['came', 'come'], ['were', 'be']], q: 'What did the messenger bring?', opts: ['a letter', 'a flag', 'some food'] },
    { at: [18, 41], where: 'the south rice fields', en: 'On day eight, it rained all day. The rice fields were full of water.', vi: 'Ngày thứ tám, trời mưa suốt cả ngày. Ruộng lúa ngập đầy nước.', verbs: [['rained', 'rain'], ['were', 'be']], q: 'What was the weather like?', opts: ['rainy', 'sunny', 'snowy'] },
    { at: [31, 18], where: 'the market', en: 'On day nine, the market had only potatoes. People shared them.', vi: 'Ngày thứ chín, chợ chỉ còn khoai tây. Mọi người chia nhau ăn.', verbs: [['had', 'have'], ['shared', 'share']], q: 'What did the market have?', opts: ['only potatoes', 'a lot of fish', 'only rice'] },
    { at: [7, 22], where: 'the hen house', en: 'On day ten, a little girl found three eggs and gave them to the guards.', vi: 'Ngày thứ mười, một cô bé tìm được ba quả trứng và đem cho lính gác.', verbs: [['found', 'find'], ['gave', 'give']], q: 'How many eggs did she find?', opts: ['three', 'two', 'ten'] },
    { at: [17, 29], where: 'the south watchtower', en: 'On day eleven, the guards lit more torches. The night was long and cold.', vi: 'Ngày thứ mười một, lính gác thắp thêm đuốc. Đêm dài và lạnh.', verbs: [['lit', 'light'], ['was', 'be']], q: 'What did the guards light?', opts: ['torches', 'candles', 'the houses'] },
    { at: [25, 29], where: 'the Brown Family Home', en: 'On day twelve, Mrs. Brown waited at the door all evening. Her son did not come.', vi: 'Ngày thứ mười hai, bà Brown đứng đợi ở cửa suốt buổi tối. Con trai bà không về.', verbs: [['waited', 'wait'], ['did not come', 'not come']], q: 'Who waited at the door?', opts: ['Mrs. Brown', 'Lord Gusest', 'the baker'] },
    { at: [31, 8], where: 'Oriaris Hall', en: 'On day thirteen, everyone went to Oriaris Hall and prayed for peace.', vi: 'Ngày thứ mười ba, mọi người đến Oriaris Hall và cầu nguyện cho hòa bình.', verbs: [['went', 'go'], ['prayed', 'pray']], q: 'Where did everyone go?', opts: ['Oriaris Hall', 'the rice fields', 'the well'] },
  ];
  const LAST_DAY = 14;

  function chapter() {
    const d = data();
    return d && d.ch ? d.ch : null;
  }
  function startChapter() {
    const d = data();
    if (!d || d.ch || d.done) return;
    d.ch = { day: 1, ok: {}, started: Date.now() };
    save();
  }
  function todayTask() {
    const d = data();
    const ch = d && d.ch;
    if (!ch || d.done || ch.day >= LAST_DAY) return null;
    return Object.assign({ day: ch.day }, DAYS[ch.day - 1]);
  }
  // The golden-light reading card of the day.
  const cardDone = () => {
    const ch = chapter();
    return !!(ch && ch.ok[ch.day]);
  };
  // The whole day: the reading card and the letters of the messenger (js/front.js).
  const todayDone = () => cardDone() && (!window.LQFront || window.LQFront.dayDone());

  const DAY_CSS = `
#lq-day-hud{position:fixed;left:50%;bottom:max(14px,env(safe-area-inset-bottom));transform:translateX(-50%);z-index:9040;background:#1d1a14ee;border:2px solid #c9a24a;border-radius:999px;padding:7px 16px;color:#f4ecd6;font:650 13px/1.3 system-ui,'Segoe UI',sans-serif;box-shadow:0 6px 18px #0008;max-width:calc(100vw - 32px);text-align:center;pointer-events:none}
#lq-day-hud[hidden]{display:none}
#lq-day-hud b{color:#f3c14b}
#lq-day{position:fixed;inset:0;z-index:10030;display:grid;place-items:center;background:#0007;font-family:system-ui,'Segoe UI',sans-serif}
#lq-day .box{width:min(560px,calc(100vw - 32px));box-sizing:border-box;background:linear-gradient(170deg,#2a251c,#1b1813);border:2px solid #c9a24a;border-radius:16px;padding:16px 18px 14px;color:#f4ecd6;box-shadow:0 14px 40px #000b}
#lq-day .tag{font:800 12px system-ui,sans-serif;letter-spacing:.08em;color:#e0c27a;text-transform:uppercase}
#lq-day .en{margin:8px 0 4px;font:600 19px/1.45 Georgia,'Times New Roman',serif}
#lq-day .en b{color:#f3c14b;font-weight:800}
#lq-day .vi{margin:0 0 10px;font:14px/1.4 system-ui,sans-serif;color:#bfb6a0}
#lq-day .verbs{font:13px/1.5 system-ui,sans-serif;background:#ffffff0d;border-radius:10px;padding:7px 10px;margin-bottom:12px}
#lq-day .verbs span{display:inline-block;margin-right:12px;white-space:nowrap}
#lq-day .verbs b{color:#f3c14b}
#lq-day .q{font:700 15px system-ui,sans-serif;margin-bottom:8px}
#lq-day .opts{display:grid;gap:7px}
#lq-day .opts button{background:#3a3226;color:#f4ecd6;border:1px solid #6b5a3a;border-radius:10px;padding:9px 12px;font:600 15px system-ui,sans-serif;text-align:left;cursor:pointer}
#lq-day .opts button:hover{border-color:#e0c27a}
#lq-day .opts button.no{border-color:#b5523b;background:#4a2a22;animation:lqShake .3s}
#lq-day .opts button.yes{border-color:#7fae5a;background:#2f4126}
@keyframes lqShake{0%,100%{transform:translateX(0)}25%{transform:translateX(-5px)}75%{transform:translateX(5px)}}
#lq-day .fb{min-height:20px;margin-top:9px;font:600 14px/1.4 system-ui,sans-serif}
#lq-day .row{display:flex;justify-content:flex-end;gap:8px;margin-top:8px}
#lq-day .row button{background:#c9a24a;color:#1d1a14;border:0;border-radius:8px;padding:7px 14px;font:800 14px system-ui,sans-serif;cursor:pointer}
#lq-day .row button.later{background:none;color:#bfb6a0;text-decoration:underline;font-weight:600}
`;
  const escHtml = (s) => String(s).replace(/[&<>"']/g, (c) => ({ '&': '&amp;', '<': '&lt;', '>': '&gt;', '"': '&quot;', "'": '&#39;' })[c]);
  // Bold the day's past verbs inside the sentence.
  function markVerbs(text, verbs) {
    let html = escHtml(text);
    verbs.forEach(([past]) => {
      html = html.replace(new RegExp('\\b(' + past.replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g, '\\$&') + ')\\b', 'g'), '<b>$1</b>');
    });
    return html;
  }
  let dayCard = null;
  function closeDayCard() {
    if (!dayCard) return;
    dayCard.remove();
    dayCard = null;
    const sc = getScene();
    const kb = sc && sc.input && sc.input.keyboard;
    if (kb) kb.enabled = true;
  }
  function openDayCard(t) {
    if (dayCard || filming) return;
    ensureCss();
    const sc = getScene();
    const kb = sc && sc.input && sc.input.keyboard;
    if (kb) {
      kb.enabled = false;
      try {
        kb.resetKeys();
      } catch (e) {}
    }
    const opts = t.opts.map((o, i) => ({ o, right: i === 0 })).sort(() => Math.random() - 0.5);
    const root = document.createElement('div');
    root.id = 'lq-day';
    root.innerHTML =
      `<div class="box" role="dialog" aria-label="Day ${t.day}"><div class="tag">📜 Day ${t.day} / ${LAST_DAY} · ${escHtml(t.where)}</div>` +
      `<p class="en">${markVerbs(t.en, t.verbs)}</p><p class="vi">${escHtml(t.vi)}</p>` +
      `<div class="verbs">📘 Past tense: ${t.verbs.map(([p, b]) => `<span><b>${escHtml(p)}</b> ← ${escHtml(b)}</span>`).join('')}</div>` +
      `<div class="q">❓ ${escHtml(t.q)}</div><div class="opts">${opts.map((x, i) => `<button type="button" data-i="${i}">${escHtml(x.o)}</button>`).join('')}</div>` +
      '<div class="fb"></div><div class="row"><button type="button" class="later">Later</button></div></div>';
    document.body.appendChild(root);
    dayCard = root;
    const fb = root.querySelector('.fb');
    const row = root.querySelector('.row');
    root.querySelector('.later').onclick = closeDayCard;
    root.querySelectorAll('.opts button').forEach((btn) => {
      btn.onclick = () => {
        const x = opts[Number(btn.dataset.i)];
        if (!x.right) {
          btn.classList.remove('no');
          void btn.offsetWidth;
          btn.classList.add('no');
          fb.textContent = '✗ Not quite. Read the sentence again. · Đọc lại câu chuyện nhé.';
          fb.style.color = '#f0a08a';
          return;
        }
        btn.classList.add('yes');
        root.querySelectorAll('.opts button').forEach((b) => (b.disabled = true));
        const ch = chapter();
        if (ch && !ch.ok[t.day]) {
          ch.ok[t.day] = Date.now();
          save();
        }
        fb.innerHTML = `✓ Correct! Day ${t.day} is done. Now go to the <b>Brown Family Home</b> and sleep (bed, E). <br><span style="color:#bfb6a0">Đúng rồi! Về Brown Family Home và ngủ (đến giường, nhấn E).</span>`;
        fb.style.color = '#b8e08f';
        row.innerHTML = '<button type="button">OK ✓</button>';
        row.querySelector('button').onclick = closeDayCard;
      };
    });
  }

  // HUD line + golden light for today's place.
  let hud = null;
  function updateHud(sc) {
    if (!hud) {
      ensureCss();
      hud = document.createElement('div');
      hud.id = 'lq-day-hud';
      document.body.appendChild(hud);
    }
    const d = inPast(sc) && data();
    const ch = d && d.ch;
    // also hidden while chatting (Lord Gusest, Tom Brown): it would cover the chat's input box
    if (!ch || d.done || filming || ch.day >= LAST_DAY || (sc.chat && sc.chat.isOpen)) {
      hud.hidden = true;
      return;
    }
    const t = todayTask();
    hud.hidden = false;
    const mail = window.LQFront && window.LQFront.hud();
    const parts = [];
    if (!cardDone()) parts.push(`Today: go to <b>${escHtml(t.where)}</b> (golden light)`);
    if (mail) parts.push(mail);
    hud.innerHTML = todayDone()
      ? `📜 Day <b>${ch.day}</b> / ${LAST_DAY} · 🛏 Done! Sleep in the <b>Brown Family Home</b> (bed, E)`
      : `📜 Day <b>${ch.day}</b> / ${LAST_DAY} · ${parts.join('<br>')}`;
  }
  let nearDay = false;
  function drawDayLight(sc, time) {
    const st = sc.__lqPastDay;
    if (!st) return;
    st.g.clear();
    if (!here(sc) || sc.inside || filming || !sc.player) return;
    const t = todayTask();
    if (!t || cardDone()) return;
    const cx = t.at[0] * 32 + 16;
    const cy = t.at[1] * 32 + 16;
    const p = 0.5 + 0.5 * Math.sin(time * 0.004);
    for (let i = 0; i < 6; i++) st.g.fillStyle(0xffd27a, 0.05 + p * 0.03).fillCircle(cx, cy, 30 - i * 4);
    st.g.lineStyle(2, 0xffe6a8, 0.5 + p * 0.4).strokeEllipse(cx, cy + 8, 30 + p * 8, 10 + p * 3);
    const by = cy - 26 - p * 5;
    st.g.fillStyle(0xffe08a, 1).fillTriangle(cx - 6, by, cx + 6, by, cx, by + 9);
    st.g.fillStyle(0xfff4d6, 1).fillCircle(cx, by - 4, 5);
    const close = Math.hypot(sc.player.x - cx, sc.player.y - cy) < 34;
    if (close && !nearDay) openDayCard(t);
    nearDay = close;
  }
  function attachDay(sc) {
    if (sc.__lqPastDay) return;
    sc.__lqPastDay = { g: sc.add.graphics().setDepth(8.7) };
    sc.events.on('update', (time) => drawDayLight(sc, time));
    sc.events.once('shutdown', () => (sc.__lqPastDay = null));
  }

  // Bed in the Brown Family Home (game: bedNear / trySleep hooks in 10-core-characters-and-scene.js).
  const canSleepIn = (name) => here(getScene()) && name === 'Brown Family Home';
  function sleep(sc) {
    if (!here(sc)) return false;
    const d = data();
    if (!d) return false;
    if (d.done) {
      toast('😴 A quiet night', 'The war is over.', 'Chiến tranh đã kết thúc. Ngủ ngon!');
      return true;
    }
    startChapter();
    const t = todayTask();
    if (t && !cardDone()) {
      toast('😴 Not yet', `First, go to ${t.where} (golden light).`, 'Hãy làm việc của hôm nay trước đã.');
      return true;
    }
    if (t && !todayDone()) {
      toast('😴 Not yet', "First, deliver today's letters (see the bar at the bottom).", 'Hãy giao hết thư của hôm nay trước đã.');
      return true;
    }
    sc.sleeping = true;
    save();
    const cam = sc.cameras.main;
    // Force the fade (another fade, e.g. from entering the house, may still be running and would block it), and
    // finish the night on a timer too, so the player can never get stuck "sleeping".
    let night = false;
    const morning = () => {
      if (night) return;
      night = true;
      const ch = d.ch;
      ch.day = Math.min(LAST_DAY, ch.day + 1);
      // The village's own calendar: no sc.newDay() here (it would add a day and the daily pay in the present).
      sc.store.game.minutes = 420;
      const ending = ch.day >= LAST_DAY;
      if (ending && sc.inside) {
        try {
          sc.leaveBuilding();
        } catch (e) {}
      }
      if (ending) sc.player.setPosition(22 * 32 + 16, 21 * 32 + 16);
      save();
      cam.fadeIn(900, 0, 0, 0);
      sc.sleeping = false;
      if (ending) setTimeout(playEnding, 1100);
      else {
        const nt = todayTask();
        toast(`☀ Day ${ch.day} / ${LAST_DAY}`, nt ? `Today: go to ${nt.where}.` : '', 'Một ngày mới ở làng Oriaris.');
      }
    };
    cam.once('camerafadeoutcomplete', morning);
    cam.fade(900, 0, 0, 0, true);
    setTimeout(morning, 1100);
    return true;
  }

  // ---- Day 14: the ending ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  const ENDING = [
    { cam: [18, 40], zoom: 1.1, en: 'On the fourteenth day, early in the morning, someone ran up the south road.', vi: 'Ngày thứ mười bốn, vào sáng sớm, có người chạy lên con đường phía nam.', act: 'run' },
    { cam: [18, 27], zoom: 1.2, who: 'Young soldier', en: 'We won! The war is over! Peace is back!', vi: 'Chúng ta thắng rồi! Chiến tranh kết thúc! Hòa bình đã trở lại!' },
    { cam: [21, 21], zoom: 1.15, en: 'He carried the victory flag. His name was Brown, the father of Mayor Brown.', vi: 'Anh mang theo lá cờ chiến thắng. Tên anh là Brown, cha của Thị trưởng Brown.', act: 'crowd' },
    { cam: [22, 20], zoom: 1.15, en: 'Everybody cheered. His mother cried and hugged her son.', vi: 'Mọi người reo hò. Mẹ anh bật khóc và ôm chầm lấy con trai.', act: 'cheer' },
    { cam: [24, 17], zoom: 1, en: 'From that day, March 29th was the Day of Glory.', vi: 'Từ hôm đó, ngày 29 tháng 3 trở thành Ngày Vinh Quang.', act: 'color' },
    { cam: [24, 17], zoom: 1, en: 'The colors came back to the village, and peace came back too.', vi: 'Màu sắc trở lại với ngôi làng, và hòa bình cũng trở lại.' },
    { cam: null, zoom: 1, who: 'Ms. Green', en: 'And that, dear, is the story of the Wall of Oriaris.', vi: 'Và đó, cháu yêu, là câu chuyện về Bức tường Oriaris.', act: 'end' },
  ];
  function drawSoldier(g, x, y, flagWave) {
    drawPerson(g, x, y, 0x5c5246, 0x3b2a1e);
    g.fillStyle(0x8e9294, 1).fillEllipse(x, y - 13, 13, 7);
    g.fillStyle(0xaeb2b4, 1).fillRect(x - 7, y - 11, 14, 2);
    g.fillStyle(0x4b3a26, 1).fillRect(x + 6, y - 34, 2, 44);
    const w = Math.sin(flagWave) * 2;
    g.fillStyle(0x9a2f2f, 1).fillTriangle(x + 8, y - 34, x + 30, y - 30 + w, x + 8, y - 20);
    g.fillStyle(0x7d2e2e, 1).fillTriangle(x + 8, y - 26, x + 30, y - 30 + w, x + 8, y - 20);
    crest(g, x + 15, y - 28, 0.8);
  }
  function playEnding() {
    return runFilm({
      card: { small: 'Day 14', big: 'The Day of Glory', sub: 'Ngày 14 · Ngày Vinh Quang' },
      lines: ENDING,
      setup(sc, actors) {
        const RC = rnd(99);
        const crowd = [];
        for (let i = 0; i < 18; i++) {
          const ang = (i / 18) * Math.PI * 2;
          crowd.push({ x: (21 + Math.cos(ang) * 3.2) * 32, y: (20.5 + Math.sin(ang) * 1.8) * 32, c: [0x7a6a58, 0x6b7280, 0x8a7a5a, 0x5d6b5a][i % 4], h: [0x3b2a1e, 0x6b5a45, 0x2a2a2a, 0x8a7a6a][(i * 3) % 4], d: RC() });
        }
        const sol = { x: 18.5 * 32, y: 46 * 32, tx: 21 * 32, ty: 20.5 * 32, t0: 0 };
        let showCrowd = false;
        let cheer = 0;
        return {
          tick(time) {
            if (showCrowd) crowd.forEach((p, i) => drawPerson(actors, p.x, p.y - (cheer ? Math.abs(Math.sin(time * 0.012 + i)) * 5 : 0), p.c, p.h));
            if (sol.t0) {
              const k = Math.min(1, (performance.now() - sol.t0) / 5200);
              // up the south road, then across to the middle of the crowd
              const yRoad = sol.y + (27 * 32 - sol.y) * Math.min(1, k / 0.75);
              const x = k < 0.75 ? sol.x : sol.x + (sol.tx - sol.x) * ((k - 0.75) / 0.25);
              const y = k < 0.75 ? yRoad : 27 * 32 + (sol.ty - 27 * 32) * ((k - 0.75) / 0.25);
              const bob = k < 1 ? Math.abs(Math.sin(time * 0.02)) * 3 : cheer ? Math.abs(Math.sin(time * 0.012)) * 5 : 0;
              drawSoldier(actors, x, y - bob, time * 0.01);
              if (cheer && k >= 1) drawPerson(actors, x - 18, y, 0x6b5a6a, 0x8a8a8a);
            }
          },
          act(a) {
            if (a === 'run') sol.t0 = performance.now();
            if (a === 'crowd') showCrowd = true;
            if (a === 'cheer') cheer = 1;
            if (a === 'color') {
              setColor(true, 3.5);
              const d = data();
              if (d && !d.done) {
                d.done = Date.now();
                save();
              }
            }
            if (a === 'end') showCrowd = false;
          },
        };
      },
    }).then(() => finishChapter());
  }
  function finishChapter() {
    const sc = getScene();
    const d = data();
    if (d && !d.rewarded) {
      d.done = d.done || Date.now();
      d.rewarded = Date.now();
      if (sc && sc.store && sc.store.game) sc.store.game.money = (Number(sc.store.game.money) || 0) + 100;
      save();
      toast('🏆 Chapter 2 complete!', 'The Wall of Oriaris · +$100', 'Hoàn thành Chương 2! Về hiện tại và hỏi Mr. Brown về cha của ông.');
    }
    setTimeout(() => {
      const s = getScene();
      if (s && here(s) && s.switchArea) s.switchArea('english-town', { x: 8, y: 23 }, 'Back to today · Ask Mr. Brown about his father');
    }, 3500);
  }
  const isDone = () => {
    const d = data();
    return !!(d && d.done);
  };

  // ---- the village's own calendar ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  // Time in the present stands still while the player is in the past: the present day and clock are kept on the way in
  // (d.present) and given back on the way out. The village counts its own days (ch.day, only by sleeping in the Brown
  // Family Home) and keeps its own clock (d.clock). Day 1 = March 16 … Day 14 = March 29, the Day of Glory.
  const FIRST_DATE = 15;
  function patchNewDay(sc) {
    if (sc.__lqPastNewDay || typeof sc.newDay !== 'function') return;
    sc.__lqPastNewDay = true;
    const orig = sc.newDay;
    // Midnight in the village must not start a new day (and pay the daily money) in the present. Also not in the frame
    // right after leaving, before calendarSync has given the present day back (d.present still set).
    sc.newDay = function () {
      const g = this.store && this.store.game;
      if (inPast(this) || (g && g.ach && g.ach.past && g.ach.past.present)) return;
      return orig.apply(this, arguments);
    };
  }
  function calendarSync(sc) {
    const g = sc.store && sc.store.game;
    if (!g) return;
    patchNewDay(sc);
    if (inPast(sc)) {
      if (sc.switching) return;
      const d = data();
      if (!d) return;
      if (!d.present) {
        d.present = { day: g.day || 1, minutes: g.minutes || 0 };
        g.minutes = typeof d.clock === 'number' ? d.clock : 480;
        save();
      }
      d.clock = g.minutes;
    } else {
      const d = g.ach && g.ach.past;
      if (!d || !d.present) return;
      g.day = d.present.day;
      g.minutes = d.present.minutes;
      d.present = null;
      save();
    }
  }
  function pastDate() {
    const d = data();
    if (d && d.done) return `March ${FIRST_DATE + LAST_DAY} · Day of Glory`;
    const day = (d && d.ch && d.ch.day) || 1;
    return `Day ${day}/${LAST_DAY} · March ${FIRST_DATE + day}`;
  }
  // For the game's HUDs: "Day N" in the present, the village date in the past.
  function dayLabel(day) {
    return inPast(getScene()) ? pastDate() : `Day ${day || 1}`;
  }

  // Go to the village (from the Ms. Green prelude, Ms. Green later, or ?past=1).
  function enter(opts) {
    const o = Object.assign({ film: true }, opts || {});
    const sc = getScene();
    if (!sc || !sc.switchArea) return false;
    if (here(sc)) {
      if (o.film) play();
      return true;
    }
    const d = data();
    if (d) {
      d.visits = (d.visits || 0) + 1;
      d.pendingFilm = !!o.film;
      save();
    }
    sc.switchArea(ID, SPAWN, 'Chapter 2 · 70 Years Ago');
    return true;
  }

  setInterval(() => {
    const sc = getScene();
    if (!sc || !sc.area) return;
    // Check the tall sprites every frame (so they appear/disappear together with the area), hooked once per scene.
    if (!sc.__lqPastHook && sc.events) {
      sc.__lqPastHook = true;
      sc.events.on('update', (time, delta) => {
        calendarSync(sc);
        if (here(sc)) armorNpc(sc);
        syncTall(sc);
        folkUpdate(sc, delta);
        hensUpdate(sc, delta);
      });
    }
    if (sc.add && sc.events && sc.player) attachDay(sc);
    const d = here(sc) && data();
    // After the chapter the village keeps its colours.
    if (d && d.done && !colorBack) setColor(true);
    applyFilter(sc);
    checkSigns(sc);
    updateHud(sc);
    if (d && d.pendingFilm && !sc.switching) {
      d.pendingFilm = false;
      save();
      setTimeout(play, 700);
    } else if (d && !d.ch && !d.done && !d.pendingFilm && !filming && !sc.switching) startChapter();
  }, 300);

  const m = /[?&]past=(\d)\b/.exec(location.search);
  if (m) {
    const wait = setInterval(() => {
      const sc = getScene();
      if (sc && sc.player && sc.switchArea) {
        clearInterval(wait);
        setTimeout(() => enter({ film: m[1] !== '2' }), 900);
      }
    }, 500);
  }

  window.LQPast = {
    id: ID,
    area,
    rooms: ROOMS,
    notes: NOTES,
    signs: SIGNS,
    paint,
    enter,
    play,
    isHere: () => here(getScene()),
    isFilming: () => filming,
    read: () => {
      const d = data();
      return d ? [...SIGNS, ...NOTES].filter((s) => d.read[s.id]).map((s) => s.id) : [];
    },
    setColor,
    // Chapter 2 days (bed hooks in 10-core-characters-and-scene.js)
    canSleepIn,
    sleep,
    isDone,
    days: DAYS,
    // Own calendar (used by the HUDs in 10, 16 and 19)
    dayLabel,
    pastDate,
    // Testing the working day: LQPast.workers() lists where the children and mothers are; LQPast.setTime(6.5) = 06:30.
    workers() {
      const sc = getScene();
      const st = sc && sc.__lqPastFolk;
      return st ? st.folk.filter((p) => p.v.work).map((p) => ({ i: p.i, kind: p.v.kind, mode: p.mode, going: p.route ? p.goal : null, gone: !!p.gone, x: +(p.x / T).toFixed(1), y: +(p.y / T).toFixed(1) })) : null;
    },
    setTime(h) {
      const sc = getScene();
      if (sc && sc.store && sc.store.game) sc.store.game.minutes = Math.round(h * 60);
    },
    status() {
      const d = data();
      return d ? { day: d.ch ? d.ch.day : 0, todayDone: todayDone(), done: !!d.done, answered: d.ch ? Object.keys(d.ch.ok).length : 0 } : null;
    },
    playEnding,
    // Testing: LQPast.setDay(13) jumps to a day (the task of that day still has to be answered).
    setDay(n) {
      const d = data();
      if (!d) return;
      startChapter();
      d.ch.day = Math.max(1, Math.min(LAST_DAY - 1, n | 0));
      save();
    },
    // Testing: start the chapter over (grey again, day 1).
    reset() {
      const d = data();
      if (!d) return;
      d.ch = null;
      d.done = 0;
      d.rewarded = 0;
      save();
      startChapter();
      setColor(false);
    },
  };
})();

</script>
    <!-- V24 · Old Oriaris Royal Island architectural rebuild; other worlds unchanged -->

  function palette(id){return COLORS[id]||COLORS['tech-co'];}
  function col(g,c,x,y,w,h,a=1){g.fillStyle(c,a).fillRect(x,y,w,h);}
  function ellipse(g,c,x,y,w,h,a=1){g.fillStyle(c,a).fillEllipse(x,y,w,h);}
  function stroke(g,c,x,y,w,h,alpha=1){g.lineStyle(2,c,alpha).strokeRect(x,y,w,h);}
  function rnd(seed){return()=>{seed=(Math.imul(seed,1664525)+1013904223)|0;return (seed>>>0)/4294967296;};}
  function sign(g,cx,cy,label,dark=0x25334c){
    const w=Math.max(72,label.length*6+20); col(g,dark,cx-w/2,cy,w,18);stroke(g,0xe5d5a3,cx-w/2,cy,w,18); g.fillStyle(0xf5e8c9,1);
    // Lettering uses Phaser Text in scene overlays to stay crisp and readable.
  }
  // V18 · Tech-Co Neo-Garden Campus. Rendered entirely into the existing cached
  // world texture; no changes to tile geometry, NPC coordinates or collisions.
  function tBox(g,x,y,w,h,fill,edge=0x27495e){
    col(g,edge,x,y,w,h);col(g,fill,x+3,y+3,w-6,h-6);
  }
  function tLine(g,color,x1,y1,x2,y2,width=2,opacity=1){
    g.lineStyle(width,color,opacity);g.lineBetween(x1,y1,x2,y2);
  }
  function tTree(g,x,y,variant=0){
    const greens=[0x305e55,0x3f7864,0x578e73,0x76ad83];
    ellipse(g,0x254a46,x+5,y+21,58,18,.22);
    col(g,0x735e4d,x-5,y-5,10,26);
    col(g,0xaa8b62,x-2,y-4,3,17,.65);
    const nodes=[[-18,-18,37,27,1],[15,-19,38,28,2],[-5,-29,41,27,3],[-17,-5,30,23,2],[12,-4,32,21,1]];
    for(let i=0;i<nodes.length;i++){
      const [dx,dy,w,h,k]=nodes[i];
      ellipse(g,greens[(k+variant)%greens.length],x+dx,y+dy,w,h,.96);
    }
    col(g,0xb9e3aa,x-18,y-34,12,4,.65);
    col(g,0xa2d9a5,x+9,y-28,7,3,.7);
  }
  function tPlanter(g,x,y,w=92){
    ellipse(g,0x254c54,x+w/2,y+17,w+8,17,.20);
    col(g,0x536d68,x,y+7,w,17);col(g,0x87a39c,x+3,y+8,w-6,5);
    col(g,0x3b7b61,x+6,y+2,w-12,11);
    for(let i=0;i<Math.max(3,Math.floor(w/14));i++){
      let xx=x+9+i*13, yy=y+2+(i%3)*3;
      ellipse(g,[0xeedc9c,0xe9a7ab,0xcfc0f0][i%3],xx,yy,7,7,.95);
      col(g,0xfaf4de,xx-1,yy-1,2,2,.88);
    }
  }
  function tBench(g,x,y){
    ellipse(g,0x28545b,x+22,y+18,64,17,.2);
    for(let j=0;j<2;j++){
      col(g,0x4a6576,x+5,y+3+j*8,35,5);
      col(g,0xc1a87d,x+7,y+3+j*8,31,2,.94);
    }

  }
  function tPocketGarden(g,x,y,mode=0){
    // Four small sustainable garden pockets give the outer campus identity.
    ellipse(g,0x28574e,x+5,y+12,108,78,.16);
    col(g,0x477c70,x-46,y-30,96,69);
    col(g,0xa5c4a6,x-42,y-26,88,61);
    col(g,0x508b70,x-37,y-23,78,54);
    for(let k=0;k<11;k++){
      const xx=x-30+(k%4)*20+(k%2)*5;
      const yy=y-13+Math.floor(k/4)*16;
      ellipse(g,[0xe9b1b5,0xeccf94,0xcbd7e9][(k+mode)%3],xx,yy,7,6,.92);
      col(g,0xf8f5dc,xx-1,yy-1,2,2,.84);
    }
    if(mode%2===0)tBench(g,x-20,y+18);
    else{
      col(g,0xcbd9cc,x-21,y+8,43,7);
      col(g,0x416e75,x-18,y+10,37,3);
    }
  }

  // Pure painter utilities: no new colliders, no interactive hit boxes, no external assets.
  // These are static pixels cached in the existing area texture.
  function tcCircuit(g,x,y,mirror=1,seed=0){
    const cyan=0x67eff0,magenta=0xd48dff;
    const lines=[[0,0,54,0],[54,0,54,22],[54,22,88,22],[88,22,88,34]];
    for(const [a,b,c,d] of lines){
      tLine(g,0x18374d,x+a*mirror+2,y+b+2,x+c*mirror+2,y+d+2,6,.55);
      tLine(g,seed%2?magenta:cyan,x+a*mirror,y+b,x+c*mirror,y+d,2,.65);
    }
    for(const [a,b] of [[0,0],[54,22],[88,34]]){
      ellipse(g,0x142f49,x+a*mirror,y+b,14,10,.95);
      ellipse(g,seed%2?0xebbcff:0x9dfbf3,x+a*mirror,y+b,6,5,.94);
    }
  }
  function tcPad(g,x,y,w=72,c=0x75dfe3){
    col(g,0x1a354d,x,y,w,34,.96);
    col(g,0x46697a,x+3,y+3,w-6,28);
    col(g,0x83afba,x+5,y+5,w-10,24,.42);
    for(const dx of [7,w-12]){
      col(g,c,x+dx,y+6,5,3,.9);
      col(g,c,x+dx,y+25,5,3,.9);
    }
    for(let i=0;i<5;i++)col(g,i%2?0x6bb6c2:0xa7d7d1,x+12+i*(w-26)/5,y+17,7,2,.72);
  }
  function tcSolar(g,x,y,w,h,variant=0){
    col(g,0x182e47,x-5,y-5,w+10,h+10,.92);
    col(g,0x37637b,x,y,w,h);
    const cw=(w-10)/4,ch=(h-10)/3;
    for(let yy=0;yy<3;yy++)for(let xx=0;xx<4;xx++){
      const px=x+5+xx*cw,py=y+5+yy*ch;
      col(g,(xx+yy+variant)%3===0?0x398fb4:0x244f7a,px,py,cw-3,ch-3);
      col(g,0x7ae5f0,px+1,py+1,cw-5,2,.68);
      col(g,0xb3faff,px+2,py+4,2,Math.max(2,ch-9),.43);
    }
  }

    }

  }

  function tcBioTree(g,x,y,v=0){
    // Biotech trees with data-lit stems: greenery with an intentional future style.
    const hi=[0x8df8d7,0xa4e7fc,0xd2b1ff][v%3],leaf=[0x2e7768,0x287f8f,0x6678a5][v%3];
    ellipse(g,0x18374b,x+4,y+10,51,14,.29);
    col(g,0x234e5c,x-5,y-23,11,39);
    col(g,0x67bec0,x-2,y-18,3,30,.87);
    col(g,0xe3f5dd,x-3,y-9,6,3,.78);
    for(const [dx,dy,w,h] of [[-17,-23,28,21],[14,-26,29,22],[-1,-37,32,22]]){
      ellipse(g,leaf,x+dx,y+dy,w,h,.93);
      ellipse(g,hi,x+dx-5,y+dy-4,Math.max(6,w*.37),Math.max(4,h*.25),.6);
      col(g,0xd9f8e3,x+dx-2,y+dy+3,4,2,.67);
    }
    col(g,0x2b4e66,x-19,y+16,39,4);
    for(let i=0;i<4;i++)col(g,hi,x-15+i*10,y+17,4,2,.87);
  }
