  function techFutureGround(g){
    // Architectural cyber lanes and smart glass sidewalks. Draw before buildings
    // so entrance and NPC foregrounds retain their visual priority.
    const ww=W*T,hh=H*T;
    // V20 signature: a graphite-maglev avenue instead of an empty pale road.
    // Visual markings only. This must never change the 56x34 walkability graph.
    col(g,0x1c3d5a,0,16.04*T,ww,2.93*T,.91);
    col(g,0x264e69,0,16.25*T,ww,2.51*T,.89);
    col(g,0x193854,0,17.21*T,ww,.56*T,.95);
    col(g,0x6bdeeb,0,16.11*T,ww,3,.84);
    col(g,0xd9afff,0,18.83*T,ww,3,.76);
    for(let tx=0;tx<W;tx++){
      const xx=tx*T;
      col(g,tx%4===0?0xf9ce9a:0x83dff5,xx+4,17.46*T,19,3,.58);
      if(tx%2===0){
        col(g,0x4a8da1,xx+2,16.48*T,26,2,.49);
        col(g,0x8af4e2,xx+9,18.42*T,14,2,.55);
      }
    }
    // The cross-avenue is tiled rather than rendered as a featureless stripe.
    col(g,0x24536d,26.17*T,0,3.8*T,hh,.76);
    col(g,0x347086,26.5*T,0,3.13*T,hh,.77);
    for(let ty=0;ty<H;ty++){
      const yy=ty*T;
      col(g,0x5edbe9,26.18*T,yy+3,3,23,.5);
      col(g,0xcbb0ff,29.87*T,yy+5,3,21,.48);
      if(ty%2===0)col(g,0x8de6dc,27.44*T,yy+11,35,2,.28);
    }

    for(let xx=0;xx<ww;xx+=32){
      col(g,0x0d3248,xx,15.52*T,29,2,.33);
      col(g,0x78f7eb,xx+5,15.54*T,17,2,.75);
      col(g,0x17374e,xx,19.28*T,29,3,.4);
      col(g,xx%128===0?0xd5a7ff:0x6be2f5,xx+7,19.31*T,13,2,.73);
      if(xx%(T*3)===0){
        col(g,0x24465d,xx+4,17.7*T,23,3,.49);
        col(g,0x82f3f5,xx+9,17.7*T,12,2,.66);
      }
    }
    for(let yy=0;yy<hh;yy+=32){
      col(g,0x15465b,26.06*T,yy+4,3,25,.58);
      col(g,0x78eae9,26.1*T,yy+7,2,12,.66);
      col(g,0x193e57,30.02*T,yy+3,3,25,.55);
      col(g,yy%96<32?0xd7aeff:0x82e1ea,30.07*T,yy+9,2,9,.66);
    }
    // Circuitboard plaza engraving and circuitry branching into forecourts.
    for(const [x,y,m,k] of [[20.7,13.3,-1,0],[34.4,13.3,1,1],[20.6,21,-1,1],[34.4,21,1,0]])
      tcCircuit(g,x*T,y*T,m,k);
    for(const [x,y,k] of [[7,13.1,0],[18,13.1,1],[37,13.1,2],[47,13.1,0],[8,22,1],[18,22,2],[38,22,0],[47,22,1]])
      tcPad(g,x*T,y*T,72,[0x6bf5ec,0xe2a4fc,0xffd58f][k]);
    // Carefully clustered biotech trees and delivery docks bring human scale to
    // the campus without crossing any building door, spawn tile or mission pad.
    for(const [x,y,v] of [
      [4.1,3.65,0],[22.3,3.2,1],[33.1,3.2,2],[52.1,3.65,1],
      [4.2,11.5,2],[51.9,11.6,0],[4.1,21.05,0],[51.85,21.05,1],
      [4.3,31.4,1],[22.1,31.3,0],[33.1,31.3,2],[51.9,31.4,2]
    ])tcBioTree(g,x*T,y*T,v);
    for(const [x,y,v] of [[21.45,5.5,0],[34.0,5.5,1],[21.45,29.2,2],[34.2,29.2,0]])
      tcChargingDock(g,x*T,y*T,v);
    // Thin perimeter light rails: safe painted markings (not physical walls).
    for(const y of [2.7,32.5]){
      col(g,0x163849,2*T,y*T,52*T,9,.88);
      col(g,0x6ff7eb,2*T,y*T,52*T,2,.83);
      for(let x=2;x<54;x++)if(x%4===0)col(g,0xce99ff,x*T,y*T+3,15,3,.94);
    }
    // Clustered flower gardens around terminals keep the campus human and lively.
    for(const [x,y,w] of [[2.3,6.1,79],[51,6.1,78],[2.2,26.2,74],[51.1,26.4,76]]){
      tcGardenArray(g,x*T,y*T,w,62);
    }
    // Warp portals visually say "this way to the next world" without new colliders.
    for(const [xx,sign] of [[0.95,1],[55.05,-1]]){
      const x=xx*T,y=17.5*T;
      col(g,0x132c49,x-18,y-52,36,103,.44);
      for(let i=0;i<7;i++){
        const yy=y-43+i*14;
        col(g,i%2?0xb8a2ff:0x78f5e6,x+sign*7-4,yy,7,5,.77);
      }
      col(g,0x9deeff,x-14,y-52,27,3,.78);
      col(g,0xf8c995,x-14,y+48,27,3,.74);
    }
    // Deliberately nonblocking circuit medallions on three mission gathering pads.
    for(const [x,y,i] of [[22.2,13.85,0],[33,13.85,1],[32,23.7,2]]){
      let px=x*T,py=y*T;
      ellipse(g,0x18364f,px,py,39,15,.5);
      ellipse(g,[0x7cf8e4,0x92d6ff,0xe0b4ff][i],px,py,27,9,.55);
      col(g,0xeafafa,px-8,py-1,16,2,.78);
    }
    // Decorative sidewalks for a polished city footprint, no invisible blockers.
    for(const x of [3.3,52.2])for(const y of [13.1,21.8]){
      tcTerminal(g,x*T,y*T,(x+y)|0);
    }
  }
  function techFutureBuilding(g,b,index){
    const x=b.x*T,y=b.y*T,w=b.w*T,h=b.h*T,d=(b.door+.5)*T;
    const top=y+18,cy=y+h-32;
    const accent=[0x76f9f1,0xbca3ff,0x95ebf7,0xffc38e][index];
    // Bright perimeter / stepped metalwork gives each silhouette stronger materiality.
    col(g,0x0d2a48,x+13,y+4,w-26,6,.82);
    col(g,accent,x+18,y+5,w-36,3,.92);
    col(g,0x102f49,x+9,y+Math.floor(h*.61)-13,w-18,5,.94);
    col(g,accent,x+19,y+Math.floor(h*.61)-11,w-38,2,.72);
    for(const side of [-1,1]){
      const xx=side<0?x+13:x+w-23;
      col(g,0x163d58,xx,y+18,10,Math.floor(h*.61)-31,.9);
      for(let i=0;i<5;i++)col(g,accent,xx+3,y+26+i*22,4,7,.72);
    }
    // Lit storefront display next to the exact door coordinate. No collision edits.
    const sx=d+((index%2)?-78:44);
    col(g,0x234258,sx,cy-11,39,28);
    col(g,0x13556c,sx+3,cy-8,33,22);
    for(let i=0;i<3;i++){
      col(g,[accent,0xc7f7ff,0xf6d39b][i],sx+7,cy-4+i*6,10+i*5,2,.89);
    }
    col(g,0xaccfdb,sx+4,cy+14,31,2,.86);
    // Animated scan-lines are on the dynamic layer; fixed header and pinstripes are cached.
    for(let j=0;j<7;j++){
      const xx=x+35+j*(w-70)/7;
      col(g,accent,xx,y+Math.floor(h*.61)+9,13,2,j%2?.46:.82);
    }
    // Air-duct highlights add colored underglow and depth to the building base.
    for(let k=0;k<7;k++){
      const px=x+21+k*(w-50)/7;
      col(g,k%3===0?0xdcabff:accent,px,y+h-7,13,2,.72);
    }

    if(index===0){
      // HQ: solar roof and transparent datacenter bridge.
      tcSolar(g,x+29,top+2,110,78,0);
      tcSolar(g,x+w-145,top+2,108,78,1);
      col(g,0x172d4b,x+w*.39,y+38,w*.22,12);
      col(g,0xa5f4f5,x+w*.41,y+40,w*.18,6,.86);
      for(let i=0;i<5;i++)col(g,0xe3c6ff,x+w*.44+i*13,y+53,5,2,.9);
    }else if(index===1){
      // Meeting Studio: glass skylight, slim holographic conference modules.
      for(let k=0;k<5;k++){
        const xx=x+63+k*64;
        col(g,0x1b476c,xx,y+65,44,24);
        col(g,0xa8ecff,xx+3,y+68,38,3,.78);
        for(let j=0;j<3;j++)col(g,j===k%3?0xe4c5ff:0x74d8eb,xx+5+j*12,y+78,8,5,.88);
      }
      col(g,0xe9f9f4,x+w/2-42,y+28,84,3,.83);
    }else if(index===2){
      // Support Hub: visible server heat sinks, radio sensors and service panels.
      for(let k=0;k<4;k++){
        const xx=x+37+k*92;
        col(g,0x152c45,xx,y+Math.floor(h*.61)-47,53,24);
        col(g,0x75e0e7,xx+4,y+Math.floor(h*.61)-44,45,4,.83);
        for(let j=0;j<5;j++)col(g,0x6babc1,xx+6+j*9,y+Math.floor(h*.61)-34,5,8,.78);
      }
      for(let k=0;k<3;k++){
        const xx=x+w*.3+k*58;
        col(g,0xc4e8dd,xx,y+22,3,15,.86);
        ellipse(g,0xf6cb93,xx+1,y+19,7,5,.9);
      }
    }else{
      // Creative hub: luminous collaborative desk panels plus lush modular greenhouse.
      tcGardenArray(g,x+22,y+Math.floor(h*.61)-44,130,33);
      for(let k=0;k<3;k++){
        const xx=x+178+k*64;
        col(g,0x214f70,xx,y+37,47,52,.92);
        col(g,0x76cbea,xx+4,y+41,39,38,.9);
        col(g,0xe0f9f5,xx+7,y+44,32,3,.8);
        col(g,0xbdf5e4,xx+9,y+60+k%2*6,26,2,.8);
      }
    }
    // Pixel edge glow along each entry, without a large halo covering the NPC.
    col(g,accent,d-16,y+h-60,3,48,.68);
    col(g,accent,d+13,y+h-60,3,48,.68);
    col(g,0xd4fff5,d-25,y+h-78,50,2,.83);
  }
  function techFutureNexus(g){
    const x=27.5*T,y=9.7*T;
    // Vertical reactor shield: 200-pixel wings with violet/blue pixel glass.
    for(const side of [-1,1]){
      const xx=x+side*66;
      col(g,0x183c5b,xx-19,y-114,38,101,.94);
      col(g,0x367e9d,xx-15,y-108,30,92,.92);
      for(let j=0;j<6;j++){
        col(g,j%2===0?0xbdaaff:0x92f4f2,xx-11,y-103+j*15,22,6,.82);
        col(g,0x144b70,xx-8,y-101+j*15,16,3,.9);
      }
      col(g,0xf0d2fd,xx-20,y-116,40,4,.83);
      col(g,0x67edf2,xx-23,y-17,46,6,.85);
    }
    // Orbiting-energy apparatus designed as part of the static landmark silhouette.
    for(const radius of [56,74,92]){
      g.lineStyle(3,radius===92?0xbcafff:0x76e9eb,.68);
      g.strokeEllipse(x,y-57,radius*2,radius*.64);
    }
    for(let i=0;i<16;i++){
      const a=i*Math.PI*2/16;
      const xx=x+91*Math.cos(a),yy=y-58+29*Math.sin(a);
      col(g,i%3===0?0xe3b4ff:0xa6e7f3,xx-3,yy-2,6,4,.82);
    }
    // Scalloped base and ion conduits give the tower a unique landmark silhouette.
    for(let j=0;j<13;j++){
      const xx=x-108+j*18;
      col(g,j%3===0?0x9ce8f5:0x33637f,xx,y+33,15,7,.88);
      col(g,j%4===0?0xe4b6ff:0x77dad3,xx+4,y+41,7,3,.75);
    }
    // Nexus core, extra parallax ridges and floating energy bridges.
    for(const side of [-1,1]){
      const xx=x+side*95;
      col(g,0x163b56,xx-13,y-20,26,35);
      col(g,0x4ca7b9,xx-9,y-17,18,27);
      col(g,0xb6faff,xx-5,y-14,10,3,.87);
      col(g,0xdaafff,xx-4,y-4,8,11,.8);
      col(g,0x78eddf,xx-13,y+13,26,3);
    }
    col(g,0xe4c3ff,x-20,y-119,40,3,.77);
    col(g,0xa0faf0,x-9,y-138,18,4,.78);
    for(let j=0;j<5;j++){
      const xx=x-39+j*20;
      col(g,j%2?0x8cecf1:0xc7aaff,xx,y+38,10,3,.9);
    }
  }
  function techFutureGarden(g){
    const x=27.5*T,y=27.7*T;
    // Eco-tech garden with modular digital displays while retaining trees and flowers.
    for(const side of [-1,1]){
      const xx=x+side*124;
      col(g,0x284a62,xx-11,y-47,22,72);
      col(g,0x6fd5d8,xx-8,y-44,16,66,.93);
      for(let j=0;j<5;j++)col(g,j%2?0xe1f1dc:0x98edcb,xx-6,y-40+j*12,12,4,.85);
      col(g,0xc1a2ff,xx-12,y+26,24,5,.82);
    }
    for(let i=0;i<12;i++){
      const a=i*Math.PI/6;
      const xx=x+Math.cos(a)*102,yy=y+Math.sin(a)*40;
      ellipse(g,0xcaf9dd,xx,yy,6,4,.82);
      col(g,0x297761,xx-1,yy-2,2,2);
    }
    for(const dx of [-88,76]){
      col(g,0x123c53,x+dx,y-49,33,17);
      col(g,0x69d6cf,x+dx+3,y-46,27,11);
      col(g,0xd9f7e0,x+dx+7,y-43,16,2,.92);
      col(g,0xc6a8ff,x+dx+7,y-39,10,2,.78);
    }
  }
  // Dynamic cyber city lights — one existing Graphics object, throttled with the
  // old effects ticker; no per-frame DOM creation, textures, listeners or timers.
  function techFuturePulse(g,time){
    const sec=time/1000,fast=sec*1.4,done=state(),minutes=Number(game()?.minutes??480);
    const night=minutes<360||minutes>1110,bright=night?1:.7;
    // Smart guidance lanes: precisely bounded moving luminous packets.
    for(let i=0;i<23;i++){
      const x=((i*93+time*.065)%(W*T));
      col(g,i%4===0?0xd5adff:0xb8f9ff,x,15.55*T,12,2,.34*bright);
      col(g,i%3===0?0xffc98c:0x6cf4e5,W*T-x-14,19.29*T,10,3,.28*bright);
    }
    for(let i=0;i<12;i++){
      const yy=(i*115+time*.045)%(H*T);
      col(g,0x9ef5ed,26.09*T,yy,2,12,.4*bright);
      col(g,0xdab0ff,30.04*T,H*T-yy-14,2,11,.36*bright);
    }
    // Express pod stays on the entirely ornamental north and south overhead rails.
    const range=50*T;
    const px=3*T+((time*.045)%range);
    tcBus(g,px,2.57*T+Math.sin(sec*1.6)*2,0x72f5f3);
    tcBus(g,53*T-((time*.028)%range),32.36*T+Math.sin(sec*1.4+2)*2,0xd2a6ff);
    // Three service drones loop locally on rooftops / above plaza, not blocking movement.
    for(let i=0;i<3;i++){
      const a=sec*(.35+i*.05)+i*2.1;
      const cx=[12.8,42.7,27.5][i]*T,cy=[3.05,3.0,12.15][i]*T;
      const xx=cx+Math.cos(a)*[78,65,73][i],yy=cy+Math.sin(a*1.4)*[15,11,26][i];
      ellipse(g,0x1d4261,xx,yy+8,29,9,.18);
      col(g,0x284967,xx-10,yy-3,21,9);
      col(g,i===1?0xbba9ff:0x72f2f0,xx-6,yy-6,13,5,.9);
      for(const side of [-1,1]){
        col(g,0xd7f9ed,xx+side*14-5,yy-2,10,2,.78);
        col(g,0x79dcfa,xx+side*13-1,yy+3,4,2,.82);
      }
    }
    // 4 animated roof hologram monitors: flowing UI elements, not flat random sparks.
    for(const [i,b] of SPECS['tech-co'].buildings.entries()){
      const bx=b.x*T,by=b.y*T,w=b.w*T,roof=Math.floor(b.h*T*.61);
      const y=by+roof-24;
      const x=bx+((time*.02+i*83)%(w-67))+25;
      col(g,[0x9afaff,0xc9b0ff,0xa3e6fc,0xffd49d][i],x,y,16,2,.37*bright);
      for(let p=0;p<4;p++){
        const xx=bx+48+p*(w-96)/4;
        ellipse(g,p===i?0xffd99f:0x72f2e8,xx,by+11,5,4,.43*bright);
      }
      const status=!!done['tech-co:'+['priya','jonah','erin'][i%3]];
      col(g,status?0x9df8d5:0xb7cdff,bx+w*.5-20,by+roof-36,40,2,(.25+.2*Math.sin(fast+i))*bright);
    }
    // Four holographic terminal displays on the public promenade.
    for(const [i,x,y] of [[0,3.3,13.1],[1,52.2,13.1],[2,3.3,21.8],[3,52.2,21.8]]){
      const px=x*T,py=y*T,scan=(time*.028+i*13)%31;
      col(g,i%2?0xd6b7ff:0x97f9ef,px-13,py-32+scan,27,2,.38*bright);
      col(g,0xccf5fa,px-10+Math.sin(sec+i)*3,py-26,14,2,.55*bright);
    }
    // Progress-sensitive nexus orbital ring, with a crisp moving energy node.
    const x=27.5*T,y=9.7*T;
    for(let ring=0;ring<2;ring++){
      const r=59+ring*18,phase=sec*(ring?-.55:.73);
      for(let j=0;j<8;j++){
        const a=phase+j*Math.PI/4;
        const xx=x+Math.cos(a)*r,yy=y-57+Math.sin(a)*r*.32;
        ellipse(g,ring?0xd6b4ff:0x9dfcf1,xx,yy,7,4,.4*bright);
      }
    }
    for(const [i,id] of ['priya','jonah','erin'].entries()){
      if(!done['tech-co:'+id])continue;
      const xx=x-32+i*27;
      ellipse(g,0xa8ffd9,xx,y+28,21+Math.sin(fast+i)*4,11,.24*bright);
    }
    // Water garden becomes a living energy fountain, still readable in daylight.
    const fx=27.5*T,fy=27.7*T;
    ellipse(g,0xb6ffff,fx,fy-21+Math.sin(sec*2)*3,6,10,.45*bright);
    for(let i=0;i<6;i++){
      const a=sec*.35+i*Math.PI/3;
      const xx=fx+Math.cos(a)*80,yy=fy+Math.sin(a)*30;
      col(g,i%2?0x97ffcf:0xd5b3ff,xx,yy,4,2,.45*bright);
    }
  }

  function techBuilding(g,b,index){
    const x=b.x*T,y=b.y*T,w=b.w*T,h=b.h*T, d=(b.door+.5)*T;
    const roofH = Math.floor(h*.61);
    const schemes=[
      {roof:0x265574,shade:0x193b57,hi:0x5393ae,wall:0xe0ede8,frame:0x506d80,glass:0x77c3d6},
      {roof:0x82bac4,shade:0x446f85,hi:0xc0e9df,wall:0xf0f0dd,frame:0x5d818e,glass:0x7fdae2},
      {roof:0x29475e,shade:0x182f43,hi:0x5a8291,wall:0xdce5e3,frame:0x4a6473,glass:0x62b8c8},
      {roof:0x46796b,shade:0x294f54,hi:0x8aaf93,wall:0xeee5d3,frame:0x8d8070,glass:0x91c9bd}
    ];
    const q=schemes[index];
    // A consistent three-tier façade: shadow, solid wall, authored roof silhouette.
    ellipse(g,0x1c3d49,x+w/2+12,y+h+9,w+24,32,.22);
    col(g,q.frame,x+5,y+roofH-2,w-10,h-roofH+1);
    col(g,q.wall,x+10,y+roofH+6,w-20,h-roofH-14);
    col(g,0x45606b,x+1,y+roofH,w-2,13,.58);
    col(g,q.shade,x+6,y+10,w-12,roofH-1);
    col(g,q.roof,x+12,y+6,w-24,roofH-5);
    col(g,q.hi,x+17,y+9,w-34,6,.78);
    col(g,0x142c3f,x+9,y+roofH-9,w-18,12,.78);
    col(g,0xe3f0de,x+15,y+roofH-11,w-30,3,.52);
    // Regular pilasters and glass windows are on the wall, not on the roof.
    const wy=y+roofH+21;
    for(let i=0;i<7;i++){
      const xx=x+32+i*(w-64)/6;
      if(Math.abs(xx-d)<40)continue;
      tBox(g,xx-12,wy,25,44,q.glass,q.shade);
      col(g,0xcdf3e9,xx-8,wy+5,6,12,.60);
      col(g,q.frame,xx-1,wy+4,2,34);
      col(g,0x6c8b8b,xx-15,wy+44,31,5,.90);
    }
    // Door is always in the exact, existing tile position.
    col(g,0x334c5d,d-23,y+h-65,46,62);
    col(g,0xa7c5bd,d-19,y+h-60,38,57);
    col(g,0x326b80,d-17,y+h-57,34,52);
    col(g,0x8fd8d5,d-13,y+h-54,12,34,.90);
    col(g,0x6fb6c1,d+3,y+h-54,9,34,.87);
    col(g,0x264b67,d-1,y+h-54,3,51);
    col(g,0xffe4a1,d+11,y+h-27,4,4);
    col(g,0x4c6b73,d-29,y+h-5,58,6);
    col(g,0xd9e4db,d-25,y+h-4,50,2,.80);
    // Bespoke roofs, each fundamentally different: glass atrium, pavilion,
    // service hub, warm creative studio.
    if(index===0){
      // Headquarters: deep blue steel lattice and striking central glass atrium.
      for(let k=0;k<5;k++){
        const xx=x+27+k*(w-54)/4;
        col(g,0x16374c,xx,y+17,7,roofH-28,.90);
        col(g,0x6cb5c4,xx+1,y+19,2,roofH-34,.85);
      }
      tBox(g,x+w*.26,y+35,w*.48,roofH-62,0x5eaabf,0x153a58);
      for(let k=0;k<6;k++){
        let xx=x+w*.28+k*(w*.44)/6;
        col(g,k%2?0x80d3d9:0x4b94ae,xx,y+43,w*.44/6-3,roofH-79,.96);
      }
      col(g,0xeffff8,x+w*.30,y+47,w*.40,5,.45);
      col(g,0x193e59,x+24,y+roofH-35,79,10,.90);
      for(let i=0;i<3;i++) col(g,0xd9eadd,x+34+i*21,y+roofH-34,14,7,.85);
    }else if(index===1){
      // Meeting Studio: pale ceramic stepped roof and long glazed skylights.
      col(g,0xc8e4df,x+25,y+25,w-50,roofH-52);
      col(g,0x709eae,x+36,y+34,w-72,roofH-72);
      for(let k=0;k<4;k++){
        let yy=y+39+k*(roofH-80)/3;
        col(g,0x477e9b,x+48,yy,w-96,11,.96);
        col(g,0x9fe1e2,x+49,yy+2,w-98,3,.78);
      }
      for(let i=0;i<4;i++){
        col(g,0xf1eade,x+25+i*(w-50)/4,y+16,8,roofH-25,.77);
      }
      col(g,0x4a8193,x+w/2-44,y+22,88,7);
      col(g,0xd9e7e1,x+w/2-29,y+23,58,3,.94);
    }else if(index===2){
      // Help Desk: graphite service roof, three cooling cells and code panels.
      for(let i=0;i<5;i++){
        const xx=x+23+i*(w-46)/5;
        col(g,0x182e40,xx,y+17,8,roofH-28,.88);
        col(g,0x7395a0,xx+1,y+19,2,roofH-32,.56);
      }
      for(let i=0;i<3;i++){
        const xx=x+75+i*(w-150)/2;
        tBox(g,xx-37,y+36,74,62,0x496d7b,0x142f43);
        ellipse(g,0x183c50,xx,y+67,42,42,1);
        ellipse(g,0x93c4be,xx,y+67,27,27,.92);
        col(g,0x28536c,xx-4,y+52,8,30);
        col(g,0x28536c,xx-15,y+63,30,8);
      }
      for(let i=0;i<4;i++) col(g,0x8ce9cb,x+29+i*22,y+roofH-27,12,4,.82);
    }else{
      // Mail & Projects: timber-and-green roof with solar bands and mail icon.
      for(let i=0;i<4;i++){
        const xx=x+29+i*(w-58)/4;
        col(g,0x234b56,xx,y+23,43,roofH-47);
        for(let k=0;k<4;k++) col(g,0x5d9eb3,xx+5,y+29+k*19,33,13,.95);
      }
      col(g,0xd4d0af,x+w*.36,y+33,w*.27,58,.92);
      col(g,0x2e6c75,x+w*.36+8,y+43,w*.27-16,34);
      col(g,0xeff4d8,x+w*.36+10,y+45,w*.27-20,5);
      col(g,0xc6d9cf,x+w*.46,y+65,w*.08,4);
      for(let k=0;k<7;k++) col(g,0x90b397,x+20+k*55,y+roofH-21,24,4,.76);
    }
    // Carefully drawn entrance awnings, small planted accents outside walls.
    col(g,q.shade,d-34,y+h-76,68,10);

    if(index===3){col(g,0xc4a17e,d+48,y+h-63,38,43);col(g,0xe7cba1,d+52,y+h-53,30,3);}
    techFutureBuilding(g,b,index);
  }
  function techNexus(g){

  function techGarden(g){
    const x=27.5*T,y=27.7*T;

  }
  function techGround(g,a){
    const ww=W*T,hh=H*T,R=rnd(521802);
    col(g,0x478a91,0,0,ww,hh);
    // Authored grass patches: clustered pixels, not a dense random spray.
    for(let y=0;y<H;y++)for(let x=0;x<W;x++){
      const xx=x*T,yy=y*T;
      col(g,(x*3+y*7)%11<5?0x79c5ac:0x378e8b,xx,yy,T,T,.25);
      if((x*7+y*13)%7===0){
        col(g,0x4e927e,xx+7,yy+11,6,2,.42);
        col(g,0xb8d8b5,xx+17,yy+24,9,3,.55);
      }
    }
    for(let k=0;k<420;k++){
      const x=R()*ww,y=R()*hh;
      if((x>5*T&&x<51*T)&&(y>4*T&&y<31*T))continue;
      col(g,k%3?0x427f72:0xe6d69c,x,y,3+R()*4,2+R()*3,.25);
    }
    // Raised walkways of the original 56x34 traversal graph remain visible.
    col(g,0x487f82,0,15.4*T,ww,4.2*T);
    col(g,0x649fae,0,15.6*T,ww,3.8*T);
    col(g,0x9dc8c7,0,15.9*T,ww,3.3*T);
    col(g,0x4c878c,25.7*T,0,4.8*T,hh);
    col(g,0x649aa8,25.9*T,0,4.4*T,hh);
    col(g,0xa4ccc8,26.1*T,0,4*T,hh);
    // Main plaza plus four building forecourts.
    const pavements=[[21,11.6,14,12.3],[6,12,15,3],[35,12,15,3],[6,20.6,15,3.4],[35,20.6,15,3.4]];
    for(const [x,y,w,h] of pavements){
      col(g,0x639c98,x*T,y*T,w*T,h*T);
      col(g,0x9cc7c6,x*T+7,y*T+7,w*T-14,h*T-14);
      for(let ty=Math.ceil(y);ty<y+h;ty++)for(let tx=Math.ceil(x);tx<x+w;tx++){
        const xx=tx*T,yy=ty*T;
        if(xx<(x*T+7)||yy<(y*T+7)||xx>(x+w)*T-28||yy>(y+h)*T-28)continue;
        tLine(g,0x90b9b2,xx+2,yy+30,xx+29,yy+30,1,.31);
        tLine(g,0xffffff,xx+3,yy+3,xx+24,yy+3,1,.20);
        if((tx+ty)%3===0)col(g,0x85aaa8,xx+7,yy+8,5,3,.22);
      }
    }
    // Discrete curb and stepping-stone rhythm along the main road.
    for(let x=0;x<W;x++){
      let xx=x*T;
      col(g,0x78a9a9,xx+2,15.5*T,26,3,.65);
      col(g,0xf9f4df,xx+8,19.3*T,16,2,.72);
      if(x%3===0)col(g,0xffffff,xx+12,17.3*T,12,3,.28);
    }
    for(let y=1;y<H-1;y++){
      if(y>=16&&y<=19)continue;
      col(g,0xffffff,26.12*T,y*T+13,3,12,.28);
      col(g,0x619995,30*T-1,y*T+16,3,11,.32);
    }
    // Embedded circuit motif: the engraving belongs to the ground, not a blocking prop.
    for(let sign of [-1,1]){
      let xx=27.5*T+sign*138;
      tLine(g,0x72b7ae,xx,13.1*T,xx,15.1*T,3,.6);
      tLine(g,0x72b7ae,xx,13.1*T,xx-sign*45,13.1*T,3,.6);
      ellipse(g,0xc4ead7,xx-sign*45,13.1*T,11,11,.76);
      tLine(g,0x72b7ae,xx,21.2*T,xx,23.5*T,3,.6);
      ellipse(g,0xd2ece0,xx,23.4*T,10,10,.68);
    }
    techFutureGround(g);
    // Non-blocking perimeter landscaping and roof-side paths.
    for(const [x,y,v] of [[2,3,0],[53,3,1],[2,29,2],[53,30,1],[3,10,3],[53,11,2],[3,23,1],[52,24,3]]){
      tTree(g,x*T,y*T,v);
    }
    for(const [x,y,w] of [[6,13.8,108],[14,13.8,96],[36,13.8,98],[45,13.8,90],[6,22.5,105],[14,22.5,94],[36,22.5,95],[45,22.5,104]]){
      tPlanter(g,x*T,y*T,w);
    }
    for(const [x,y] of [[20.4,12.2],[34.5,12.2],[20,22.7],[34.6,22.7],[23.1,14.8],[32,14.8]])tLamp(g,x*T,y*T);
    for(const [x,y] of [[7,12.4],[38,12.4],[9,22],[38,22]])tBench(g,x*T,y*T);
    // Small authored green pockets fill the perimeter without blocking paths.
    for(const [x,y,k] of [[3.5,7,0],[52.3,7.2,1],[3.5,25.7,2],[52.3,26.1,3]]){
      tPocketGarden(g,x*T,y*T,k);
    }
    // Two quiet pedestrian crossings visually connect the office courts.
    for(const bx of [19.1,35.1]){
      for(let k=0;k<4;k++){
        const xx=(bx+k*.42)*T;
        col(g,0xf9f2df,xx,16.35*T,10,2.45*T,.56);
        col(g,0xaacac7,xx+1,16.38*T,2,2.39*T,.31);
      }
    }
    a.buildings.forEach((b,i)=>techBuilding(g,b,i));
    techNexus(g);
    techGarden(g);
    // Contextual platforms behind each teacher, without interactive blockers.
    function workPad(x,y,kind){
      const xx=x*T,yy=y*T;
      col(g,0x476e73,xx-42,yy-12,84,44,.82);
      col(g,0xbdd8cc,xx-39,yy-9,78,38,.96);
      for(let i=0;i<3;i++)col(g,0x80aaa7,xx-31+i*22,yy+18,16,3,.7);
      if(kind==='meeting'){
        col(g,0x2c5971,xx-29,yy-7,58,18);
        col(g,0xe1cb8c,xx-23,yy-3,46,4,.91);
        for(let i=0;i<3;i++)col(g,0xa6e4d9,xx-18+i*16,yy+3,10,4,.8);
      }else if(kind==='issue'){
        col(g,0x2a4d65,xx-29,yy-8,58,21);
        for(let i=0;i<3;i++)col(g,[0xe5ac87,0xe8d898,0x93dcc2][i],xx-23+i*17,yy-1,11,6,.91);
      }else{
        col(g,0x284f5e,xx-27,yy-9,54,20);
        col(g,0xe5e6d6,xx-20,yy-5,40,12);
        col(g,0x78b5b7,xx-15,yy,29,2);
      }
    }
    workPad(22.2,12.7,'meeting');
    workPad(33,12.7,'issue');
    workPad(32,22.7,'mail');
    // Company gateway and wayfinder at the east border, staying off warp tiles.
    col(g,0x325f72,51.3*T,13.5*T,90,16);
    col(g,0x97cdbd,51.4*T,13.58*T,84,5,.92);
    tLine(g,0xe2e5ca,51.5*T,13.95*T,51.5*T,15*T,2,.84);
  }

  /* V21 ROYAL CAPITAL: self-contained graphics, neither reuse nor recolor other maps. */
  const RC={ivory:0xf1dfbb,gold:0xdab766,burgundy:0x813e50,blue:0x405f7d,
    stone:0xc1ad90,ink:0x56465a,deep:0x29374d,green:0x567052,water:0x739bb5};
  const rRect=(g,c,x,y,w,h,a=1)=>{if(w>0&&h>0){g.fillStyle(c,a);g.fillRect(Math.round(x),Math.round(y),Math.round(w),Math.round(h));}};
  const rOval=(g,c,x,y,w,h,a=1)=>{g.fillStyle(c,a);g.fillEllipse(Math.round(x),Math.round(y),Math.round(w),Math.round(h));};
  const rLine=(g,c,ax,ay,bx,by,w=2,a=1)=>{g.lineStyle(w,c,a);g.lineBetween(ax,ay,bx,by);};
  function royalFlower(g,x,y,i=0){
    rRect(g,0x456348,x-1,y-3,2,9);const c=[0xe9a3b1,0xf2d590,0xb3a8d6,0xf8ece1][i%4];
    rOval(g,c,x,y-5,8,5,.95);rRect(g,0xffe6a8,x-1,y-6,2,2);
  }
  function royalHedge(g,x,y,w,h=12){
    rRect(g,0x334e40,x+3,y+4,w-6,h,0.4);rRect(g,0x405d43,x,y,w,h);
    rRect(g,0x73966b,x+2,y+1,w-5,Math.max(3,h/2));
    for(let a=5;a<w-5;a+=11)rRect(g,0xa6bc83,x+a,y+2,5,2,.73);
  }
  function royalTree(g,x,y,scale=1){
    const c=(v)=>v*scale;
    rOval(g,0x423b32,x+4,y+10,c(35),c(15),.25);

    rRect(g,0x9a795c,x-3*scale,y-c(21),c(2),c(29),.7);
    rOval(g,0x334f43,x,y-c(39),c(33),c(64));
    rOval(g,0x5e7e5a,x-c(4),y-c(43),c(26),c(55));
    rOval(g,0x86a377,x-c(8),y-c(53),c(10),c(22),.85);
    for(let j=0;j<4;j++)rRect(g,0xc6d4a5,x-c(9)+j*c(5),y-c(53)+j*c(8),c(4),c(3),.6);
  }

  function royalFlag(g,x,y,alt=false){

  }
  function royalBench(g,x,y,flip=0){

  function royalPlanter(g,x,y,mode=0){

  function royalCanopy(g,x,y,mode=0){

  function royalRuinCluster(g,x,y){

  }

  function royalParterre(g,x,y,w,h,flip=0){
    // Formal parterre rather than a flat grass rectangle: carved borders,
    // clipped hedges, mirrored scrollwork and controlled color accents.
    rRect(g,0x8d816b,x-6,y-5,w+12,h+10,.72);
    rRect(g,0xe4d6b8,x-3,y-3,w+6,h+6);
    rRect(g,0x496c4b,x+5,y+5,w-10,h-10);
    rRect(g,0x75936a,x+10,y+10,w-20,h-20);
    royalHedge(g,x+4,y+3,w-8,9);
    royalHedge(g,x+4,y+h-12,w-8,9);
    const cx=x+w/2,cy=y+h/2;
    rOval(g,0x3b5d42,cx,cy,Math.min(w*.77,150),Math.min(h*.7,90));
    rOval(g,0xb2c19a,cx,cy,Math.min(w*.65,125),Math.min(h*.6,68));
    rOval(g,0x53744e,cx,cy,Math.min(w*.50,99),Math.min(h*.51,49));
    rOval(g,0xd8c59a,cx,cy,Math.min(w*.23,43),Math.min(h*.31,25));
    rLine(g,0xc6ce9d,x+12,cy,x+w-12,cy,4,.7);
    // Two mirrored ribbon beds, never spilling onto adjacent roads.
    for(const side of [-1,1]){
      const xx=cx+side*w*.28;
      rOval(g,0x436c48,xx,cy,Math.min(w*.17,38),Math.min(h*.39,35));
      rOval(g,0xe0ceac,xx,cy,Math.min(w*.07,18),Math.min(h*.17,17));
      for(let j=0;j<6;j++){
        const py=y+14+j*(h-28)/5;
        royalFlower(g,xx+(side*5),py,(j+flip+(side>0?1:0))%4);
      }
    }
    // Accent flowers on the formal outer border only.
    for(let j=0;j<9;j++){
      const px=x+17+j*(w-34)/8;
      royalFlower(g,px,y+15,flip+j);
      royalFlower(g,px,y+h-16,flip+j+2);
    }
  }

  function royalRoadNetwork(g,routes){
    const drawing=(border)=>{
      for(const {path,width} of routes){
        const size=width+(border?9:0),color=border?0x9c876b:0xe6d2af;
        for(let i=1;i<path.length;i++){
          const a=path[i-1],b=path[i];
          rLine(g,color,a[0],a[1],b[0],b[1],size);
        }
        // Round the joints and termini to prevent broken joins on diagonals.
        for(const pt of path)rOval(g,color,pt[0],pt[1],size,size);
      }
    };
    drawing(true);
    drawing(false);
    // Cobble joints are intentionally small, offset and low-contrast.
    // There are no thick centre-lines crossing junctions or sharp end caps.
    for(const {path,width} of routes){
      for(let i=1;i<path.length;i++){
        const a=path[i-1],b=path[i],d=Math.hypot(b[0]-a[0],b[1]-a[1]);
        const n=Math.max(1,Math.floor(d/36));
        for(let j=1;j<n;j++){
          const q=j/n,x=a[0]+(b[0]-a[0])*q,y=a[1]+(b[1]-a[1])*q;
          rRect(g,0x9f886d,x-4,y-2,8,2,.13);
        }
      }
    }
  }
  function royalRoad(g,path,width){royalRoadNetwork(g,[{path,width}]);}

  // V27.2: paths are authored as a connected topological street graph.
  // Separate branches never run through the occupied house footprint.
  function v27DoorRoutes(a){
    const t=(x,y)=>[x*T,y*T];
    const specs=[
      {name:'CROWN PALACE',width:57,p:[[44.5,19],[44.5,25.8]]},
      {name:'GRAND OPERA',width:36,p:[[16.5,36],[16.5,36.75],[26.6,36.75],[30,39.8]]},
      {name:'ROYAL ACADEMY',width:36,p:[[71.5,36],[71.5,36.75],[61.4,36.75],[58,39.8]]},
      {name:'GALLERY OF DYNASTIES',width:37,p:[[71.5,54],[71.5,54.8],[62,54.8],[62,42],[58.2,40.1]]},
      {name:'HOUSE OF AURELIA',width:34,p:[[15.5,17],[15.5,17.9],[24.6,17.9],[24.6,24.4],[26.3,26.1]]},
      {name:'HOUSE OF VALMONT',width:34,p:[[72.5,17],[72.5,17.9],[63.4,17.9],[63.4,24.4],[61.7,26.1]]},
      {name:'HOUSE OF SERAPHINE',width:35,p:[[15.5,53],[15.5,54.8],[25,54.8]]},
      {name:'HOUSE OF CORVEN',width:35,p:[[29.5,54],[29.5,54.8],[25,54.8]]}
    ];
    // Single shared lane for the two southwest houses; no parallel stray
    // outlines and no illegal path across the fortified gate tower.
    specs.push({name:'ROYAL SOUTHWEST LANE',width:39,
      p:[[25,54.8],[25,42.4],[30,40.1]]});
    return specs.map(entry=>{
      const b=a.buildings.find(v=>v.name===entry.name);
      if(b){entry.p[0]=[b.door+.5,b.y+b.h];}
      return {name:entry.name,path:entry.p.map(v=>t(...v)),width:entry.width};
    });
  }
  function v27DoorAprons(g,a){
    // Same physical threshold as the E-key interaction. Two very shallow
    // steps stop at the doorstep and do not close the walking lane.
    for(const b of a.buildings){
      const cx=(b.door+.5)*T,y=(b.y+b.h)*T;
      rRect(g,0xc7ac87,cx-25,y-1,50,3,.72);
      rRect(g,0xf6e8ce,cx-22,y-1,44,2,.94);
    }
  }

  function royalFountain(g,cx,cy){
    // Multi-tier marble fountain scaled for an imperial ceremonial court.
    rOval(g,0x3a3b45,cx+7,cy+24,248,117,.28);
    rOval(g,0x8f8179,cx,cy+8,244,143);
    rOval(g,0xf8ebd2,cx,cy+3,234,132);
    rOval(g,0xa99072,cx,cy-1,219,117);
    rOval(g,0x315a74,cx,cy-4,210,107);
    rOval(g,0x5791a1,cx-6,cy-14,188,84);
    rOval(g,0x8cc4c5,cx-15,cy-19,123,38,.6);
    for(let j=0;j<16;j++){
      const angle=j*Math.PI*2/16;
      const px=cx+Math.cos(angle)*112,py=cy+Math.sin(angle)*57;
      rRect(g,0xe8d2a9,px-5,py-3,10,6);
      rRect(g,0xf8e7c5,px-5,py-3,7,2);
    }
    // Pedestal and second basin: layered elevations and sculpted edging.
    rOval(g,0x394e5d,cx,cy-16,102,48);
    rOval(g,0xebd6aa,cx,cy-22,105,50);
    rOval(g,0x6396a0,cx,cy-25,83,35);
    rRect(g,0xc2a57c,cx-14,cy-78,28,55);
    rRect(g,0xf6e3bb,cx-8,cy-86,16,63);
    rOval(g,0xf4dfb8,cx,cy-86,49,21);
    rOval(g,0x81c4c8,cx,cy-89,28,10);
    rRect(g,0xe8d5ab,cx-6,cy-118,12,31);
    rOval(g,0xf7eccf,cx,cy-122,30,13);
    rOval(g,0xb7eded,cx,cy-128,10,12,.86);
    // Four small natural water spouts, no neon-like glow.
    for(const [dx,dy] of [[-63,-9],[63,-9],[-35,21],[35,21]]){
      rOval(g,0xb6e4e3,cx+dx,cy+dy,7,12,.78);
      rRect(g,0xd4f4e9,cx+dx-2,cy+dy-13,4,14,.57);
    }
  }

  // V26 · Bespoke architectural reconstruction. Independent building masses,
  // shared stonecutting vocabulary only. All geometry stays inside prior footprints.
  const V26={ivory:0xf1e5cf,lit:0xfff2db,stone:0xcbb695,edge:0x92795d,
    copper:0x6d857c,slate:0x405770,burgundy:0x743b50,glass:0x6d94a1,
    ink:0x344251,shadow:0x383b46,gold:0xc8a56e};
  function v26Shadow(g,x,y,w,h){
    // Only the ground-contact shadow: never surround a bespoke silhouette with
    // a solid, rectangular underlay. The previous backplate caused the dark
    // square artifacts seen beside the conservatory and turret.
    rOval(g,0x493e39,x+w/2+6,y+h+3,w-18,19,.20);
    rRect(g,0x8b795f,x+16,y+h-5,w-32,4,.20);
  }
  function v26Stone(g,x,y,w,h,kind=0){
    const colors=[0xeadac0,0xe2d2b8,0xd0d0c8,0xe4d9c5,0xf0dac5];
    const c=colors[kind]||colors[0];
    rRect(g,0x9c8772,x,y,w,h);
    rRect(g,c,x+5,y+4,w-10,h-7);
    rRect(g,0xffffff,x+7,y+5,w-14,3,.2);
    for(let j=1;j<4;j++){
      const yy=y+10+j*(h-26)/4;
      rRect(g,0x775d53,x+8,yy,w-16,2,.12);
      for(let k=0;k<7;k++){
        const xx=x+12+k*(w-24)/7+(j%2)*8;
        rRect(g,0x8f7b63,xx,yy-10,2,10,.095);
      }
    }
    rRect(g,0xf8e9d4,x+8,y+6,4,h-14,.38);
    rRect(g,0x7c6559,x+w-11,y+6,4,h-14,.23);
  }
  function v26Mansard(g,x,y,w,h,shade=V26.slate,dormers=3){
    // Multi-layer stepped mansard with visible upper crown and lower eaves.
    rRect(g,0x293847,x+6,y+14,w-12,h-4);
    rRect(g,shade,x+14,y+5,w-28,h-12);
    rRect(g,0x9bb0aa,x+22,y+8,w-44,3,.38);
    rRect(g,0x213348,x+3,y+h-21,w-6,13,.48);
    for(let t=0;t<3;t++)rRect(g,0x243649,x+13,y+18+t*13,w-26,2,.20);
    for(let j=0;j<dormers;j++){
      const xx=x+w*(j+1)/(dormers+1);
      v26Dormer(g,xx,y+18,shade,Math.min(1.12,w/(dormers*80)));
    }
    v24Cornice(g,x+3,y+h-5,w-6,0xceae78);
  }
  function v26Dormer(g,cx,y,roof=V26.slate,s=1){
    const q=(n)=>Math.round(n*s);
    rRect(g,0x8d795d,cx-q(15),y+q(3),q(30),q(29));
    rRect(g,0xf5e6c9,cx-q(12),y+q(5),q(24),q(24));
    rRect(g,0x314c61,cx-q(8),y+q(10),q(16),q(18));
    rRect(g,0x91b1b2,cx-q(6),y+q(12),q(5),q(14),.8);
    rRect(g,0xd7e1d5,cx+q(2),y+q(12),q(2),q(12),.38);
    g.fillStyle(0x28384c,1).fillTriangle(cx-q(20),y+q(7),cx,y-q(12),cx+q(20),y+q(7));
    g.fillStyle(roof,1).fillTriangle(cx-q(17),y+q(5),cx,y-q(9),cx+q(17),y+q(5));
    rRect(g,0xf8e5be,cx-q(17),y+q(5),q(34),q(3));
  }
  function v26HighWindow(g,x,y,variant=0,s=1,balcony=false){
    const k=(n)=>n*s,w=k(20),h=k(38),glass=[0x65859c,0x678b8f,0x8d7085,0x62798f][variant%4];
    rRect(g,0x99846b,x-k(6),y-k(9),w+k(12),h+k(16));
    rRect(g,0xfcf0d9,x-k(4),y-k(7),w+k(8),h+k(11));
    rRect(g,0x344b60,x,y+9*s,w,h-9*s);
    rOval(g,0x344b60,x+w/2,y+8*s,w,17*s);
    rRect(g,glass,x+k(2),y+k(11),w-k(4),h-k(12));
    rRect(g,0xcbe2df,x+k(4),y+k(13),k(3),h-k(19),.60);
    rRect(g,0x152e43,x+w/2,y+k(10),k(2),h-k(10));
    rRect(g,0x183247,x,y+k(23),w,k(2),.8);
    rRect(g,0xe8caa0,x-k(7),y+h-k(1),w+k(14),k(5));
    rRect(g,0x9c7c5b,x-k(8),y+h+k(3),w+k(16),k(3));
    if(balcony){
      rRect(g,0x9b805f,x-k(10),y+h+k(5),w+k(20),k(9));
      for(let j=0;j<5;j++)rRect(g,0xd8b77f,x-k(6)+j*k(8),y+h+k(11),k(3),k(15));
      rRect(g,0xf2d79c,x-k(12),y+h+k(24),w+k(24),k(4));
    }
  }
  function v26Windows(g,x,y,w,count,kind=0,s=1,balcony=false){
    for(let j=0;j<count;j++){
      const cx=x+(j+.5)*w/count;
      v26HighWindow(g,cx-10*s,y,kind+((j===0||j===count-1)?1:0),s,balcony&&(j%3===1));
    }
  }
  function v26Portico(g,x,y,w,h,columns=4){
    // Open arcade: columns carry the upper entablature, not an applied pattern.
    rRect(g,0x977b60,x,y+5,w,h);
    rRect(g,0xf1e3c8,x+5,y+5,w-10,h-10);
    for(let j=0;j<columns;j++){
      const xx=x+10+j*(w-20)/(columns-1);
      royalColumn(g,xx,y+16,h-21);
    }
    v24Cornice(g,x-4,y,w+8,0xe0ba7d);
    rRect(g,0x927253,x-4,y+h-4,w+8,9);
    rRect(g,0xdcbf91,x-5,y+h+3,w+10,4);
  }
  function v26GrandDoor(g,b,yExtra=0,arch=0){
    const bottom=(b.y+b.h)*T+yExtra,cx=(b.door+.5)*T;
    const width=arch?61:52,height=arch?79:67;
    rOval(g,0x383541,cx+3,bottom+6,100,25,.2);
    rRect(g,0x8b725b,cx-width/2-9,bottom-height-11,width+18,height+16);
    rRect(g,0xf5e5c9,cx-width/2-5,bottom-height-10,width+10,height+10);
    rRect(g,0x344054,cx-width/2,bottom-height+9,width,height-9);
    rOval(g,0x384c5c,cx,bottom-height+9,width,36);
    rRect(g,0x714b56,cx-width/2+4,bottom-height+18,width-8,height-18);
    rRect(g,0xd3ac77,cx-2,bottom-height+20,4,height-20);
    for(let j=0;j<3;j++){
      rRect(g,0x4d3844,cx-width/2+6,bottom-height+27+j*12,width-12,4,.5);
      rRect(g,0xe4c68d,cx-width/2+10,bottom-height+29+j*12,width-20,2,.55);
    }
    rOval(g,0xf4d99a,cx+11,bottom-24,5,5);
    for(let i=0;i<3;i++){
      const step=10+i*9;
      rRect(g,0x947b66,cx-width/2-step,bottom+i*5,width+2*step,8);
      rRect(g,0xf6e8cf,cx-width/2-step,bottom+i*5,width+2*step,3);
    }
  }
  function v26Tower(g,x,y,w,h,roof=V26.slate,round=false){
    rRect(g,0x86796a,x+6,y+22,w-12,h-14);
    rRect(g,round?0xa9afb2:0xeee0c8,x+10,y+20,w-20,h-22);
    if(round){
      rOval(g,0xc7c9c6,x+w/2,y+h/2,w-9,h*.9,.43);
      rOval(g,0x445566,x+w/2,y+58,w*.70,28);
    }
    rRect(g,0x5c6980,x-4,y+8,w+8,34);
    rRect(g,roof,x+1,y+4,w-2,31);
    g.fillStyle(roof,1).fillTriangle(x-8,y+8,x+w/2,y-23,x+w+8,y+8);
    rRect(g,0xe8c58e,x-6,y+34,w+12,5);
    v26HighWindow(g,x+w/2-10,y+67,round?3:0,.9);
    rRect(g,0x89725d,x+3,y+h-10,w-6,8);
  }
  function v26Relief(g,x,y,w,c=0xd1ab71){
    rRect(g,0x6f6355,x-6,y+8,w+12,7,.65);
    rRect(g,c,x-8,y,w+16,8);
    rRect(g,0xffecc9,x-6,y+1,w+12,2,.7);
    for(let i=0;i<Math.floor(w/20);i++)rRect(g,0xf1d7a8,x+8+i*20,y+9,8,5,.76);
  }
  function v26HeraldicClock(g,cx,cy){
    rOval(g,0xa9844c,cx,cy,57,57);
    rOval(g,0xf2e5cb,cx,cy-1,48,48);
    rOval(g,0x526270,cx,cy-1,40,40);
    for(let k=0;k<12;k++){
      const t=k*Math.PI/6;
      rRect(g,0xf3e2bb,cx+Math.sin(t)*16-1,cy-1-Math.cos(t)*16,2,3);
    }
    rLine(g,0xffe1a1,cx,cy-1,cx+6,cy-12,2);
    rLine(g,0xffe1a1,cx,cy-1,cx-10,cy-6,2);
  }
  function v26Facade(g,b){
    const x=b.x*T,y=b.y*T,w=b.w*T,h=b.h*T,mid=x+w/2,base=y+h;
    v26Shadow(g,x,y,w,h);
    if(b.name==='CROWN PALACE'){
      // Crown Palace: coherent central corps-de-logis, lower long wings, terminal pavilions.
      v26Stone(g,x+14,y+104,w-28,h-106,0);
      const wingW=285;
      for(const [sx,side] of [[x+15,-1],[x+w-15-wingW,1]]){
        v26Stone(g,sx,y+121,wingW,h-126,0);
        v26Mansard(g,sx-6,y+45,wingW+12,80,0x3e526b,4);
        v26Relief(g,sx+10,y+209,wingW-20);
        v26Windows(g,sx+12,y+231,wingW-24,5,0,.87,false);
        v26Windows(g,sx+12,y+286,wingW-24,5,0,.93,true);
        v26Portico(g,sx+15,y+351,wingW-30,50,6);
        v26Tower(g,sx+(side<0?-6:wingW-96),y+34,100,155,0x44566a);
      }
      // Shallow central projecting block is genuinely in front of the wings.
      v26Stone(g,mid-219,y+104,438,h-107,0);
      v26Mansard(g,mid-230,y+34,460,90,0x354d68,7);
      // Cupola: drum, dome, top lantern.
      rRect(g,0xbfa584,mid-74,y+8,148,65);
      rRect(g,0xf2e3c8,mid-62,y+15,124,54);
      for(let z=-2;z<=2;z++)v26HighWindow(g,mid+z*23-8,y+26,0,.55);
      rOval(g,0x304b65,mid,y+5,168,105);
      rOval(g,0x6e899b,mid-10,y-17,110,55);
      rRect(g,0xe0bd81,mid-9,y-77,18,37);
      rOval(g,0xf5d48e,mid,y-80,17,13);
      v24Gable(g,mid,y+118,240,0x526782);
      v26Windows(g,mid-201,y+158,402,7,0,1.05,false);
      v26Relief(g,mid-213,y+224,426,0xe0bd85);
      v26Windows(g,mid-199,y+243,398,7,0,1.01,true);
      v26Portico(g,mid-190,y+321,380,74,8);
      v24Heraldry(g,mid,y+147,0xe5c486);
      // The door must remain exactly aligned with the existing portal.
      v26GrandDoor(g,b,0,1);
      for(const sx of [mid-182,mid+182])v24Statue(g,sx,base-6);
      return;
    }
    if(b.name==='GRAND OPERA'){
      // Opera: convex foyer, burgundy monumental roof and wings around a stage axis.
      v26Stone(g,x+17,y+91,w-34,h-94,4);
      for(const sx of [x+15,x+w-116]){
        v26Stone(g,sx,y+108,101,h-115,0);
        v26Mansard(g,sx-7,y+44,115,67,0x743e51,2);
        v26Windows(g,sx+8,y+162,85,2,2,.95,true);
        v26Windows(g,sx+8,y+224,85,2,2,.88,false);
      }
      v26Mansard(g,mid-171,y+37,342,82,0x754151,5);
      rOval(g,0x604454,mid,y+83,194,124);
      rOval(g,0xa36b73,mid,y+75,163,97);
      rOval(g,0xe7bea1,mid,y+71,121,62);
      v24Gable(g,mid,y+135,196,0x7c3e53);
      v26Stone(g,mid-168,y+149,336,h-152,4);
      v26Windows(g,mid-154,y+181,308,5,2,1.02,true);
      v26Relief(g,mid-172,y+248,344,0xe6bf87);
      v26Portico(g,mid-150,y+261,300,72,7);
      v24Heraldry(g,mid,y+153,0xe5bf7e);
      v26GrandDoor(g,b,0,1);
      v24Statue(g,x+47,base-17);v24Statue(g,x+w-47,base-17);
      return;
    }
    if(b.name==='ROYAL ACADEMY'){
      // Academy: glazed archival roof, square turrets and central clock bay.
      v26Stone(g,x+17,y+91,w-34,h-93,0);
      v26Mansard(g,x+25,y+39,w-50,72,0x496e69,6);
      for(const xx of [x+11,x+w-116]){
        v26Tower(g,xx,y+34,106,166,0x446a67);
        v26Relief(g,xx+7,y+188,92,0xcaaa75);
        v26HighWindow(g,xx+38,y+224,1,.9,true);
      }
      v26Stone(g,mid-150,y+108,300,h-113,0);
      v24Gable(g,mid,y+125,210,0x4c736d);
      v26HeraldicClock(g,mid,y+89);
      v26Windows(g,mid-143,y+169,286,5,1,.97,false);
      v26Relief(g,mid-143,y+225,286,0xbca577);
      v26Windows(g,mid-143,y+243,286,5,1,.92,true);
      v26Portico(g,mid-111,y+283,222,62,5);
      v26GrandDoor(g,b);
      return;
    }
    if(b.name==='GALLERY OF DYNASTIES'){
      // Neoclassical museum: skylight roof and a single legible giant order.
      v26Stone(g,x+13,y+88,w-26,h-91,0);
      v26Mansard(g,x+19,y+32,w-38,70,0x526779,1);
      for(let k=0;k<6;k++){
        const sx=x+65+k*(w-130)/5;
        rRect(g,0x243e55,sx-17,y+51,34,23);
        rRect(g,0x9db9bb,sx-13,y+53,26,19,.84);
        rRect(g,0xe0e8d9,sx-11,y+55,9,16,.62);
      }
      v24Gable(g,mid,y+122,278,0x4c6071);
      v24Heraldry(g,mid,y+110,0xd9ba8d);
      v26Relief(g,x+19,y+166,w-38,0xe5c58e);
      v26Windows(g,x+28,y+180,w-56,7,0,1.0,false);
      v26Portico(g,x+24,y+248,w-48,81,9);
      for(const sx of [x+53,x+w-53])v24Statue(g,sx,base-23);
      v26GrandDoor(g,b,0,1);
      return;
    }
    if(b.name==='HOUSE OF AURELIA'){
      // Rococo manor: lateral rose salons, central curved projection, balcony.
      v26Stone(g,x+11,y+87,w-22,h-91,4);
      for(const sx of [x+9,x+w-109]){
        v26Stone(g,sx,y+82,100,h-90,4);
        v26Mansard(g,sx-4,y+26,108,65,0x7b4055,2);
        v26HighWindow(g,sx+37,y+149,2,1.02,true);
        rRect(g,0xe2bc96,sx+12,y+204,75,8);
      }
      v26Mansard(g,mid-143,y+37,286,73,0x7f4054,4);
      v26Windows(g,mid-136,y+127,272,5,2,.95,true);
      rOval(g,0xa17b7e,mid,y+224,190,38);
      rOval(g,0xe8c69f,mid,y+220,164,26);
      v24Heraldry(g,mid,y+102,0xdfbc83);
      v26GrandDoor(g,b);
      return;
    }
    if(b.name==='HOUSE OF VALMONT'){
      // French classical estate with two true roof towers and disciplined stone bays.
      v26Stone(g,x+17,y+94,w-34,h-97,2);
      v26Mansard(g,x+48,y+45,w-96,75,0x354d65,5);
      v26Tower(g,x+10,y+35,102,153,0x374d65);
      v26Tower(g,x+w-112,y+35,102,153,0x374d65);
      v26Windows(g,x+121,y+153,w-242,4,3,1.04,true);
      v26Relief(g,x+108,y+222,w-216,0xc3ac80);
      v26Windows(g,x+121,y+238,w-242,4,3,.82,false);
      v24Heraldry(g,mid,y+126,0xc6b28b);
      v26GrandDoor(g,b);
      return;
    }
    if(b.name==='HOUSE OF SERAPHINE'){
      // V27.3: a genuine attached conservatory, not a full-height glass rectangle.
      // Both wings share the plinth and eave datum; the garden room is lower than
      // the mansion and its canopy is supported by two stone corner piers.
      const mainW=Math.floor(w*.64), joinX=x+mainW-7;
      const gx=joinX,gw=x+w-10-gx, plinthY=y+h-37;
      v26Stone(g,x+10,y+86,mainW+8,h-91,4);
      v26Mansard(g,x+6,y+34,mainW+16,69,0x8e6852,4);
      v26Windows(g,x+23,y+142,mainW-30,4,1,.96,true);
      v26Relief(g,x+18,y+204,mainW-14,0xccad86);

      // Roof gutters and the stone connection belong to both facades.
      rRect(g,0x8b785e,gx-8,y+111,19,h-118);
      rRect(g,0xf5e6ce,gx-4,y+116,11,h-126);
      // Foundation: solid stone masonry below the conservatory glazing.
      rRect(g,0x917c67,gx-3,plinthY,gw+6,36);
      rRect(g,0xe8d9bf,gx+1,plinthY+3,gw-2,28);
      rRect(g,0xfbebd1,gx+5,plinthY+5,gw-12,3,.7);
      // Greenhouse glazing has a wall face, not an opaque rectangle covering
      // the original building. Vertical piers make the side volume structural.
      const glassTop=y+139,glassBottom=plinthY;
      rRect(g,0x3e635e,gx-1,glassTop-4,gw+2,glassBottom-glassTop+5);
      rRect(g,0xbcd3bd,gx+8,glassTop+3,gw-16,glassBottom-glassTop-10);
      for(let j=0;j<4;j++){
        const sx=gx+12+j*(gw-28)/3;
        rRect(g,0xe5f2dc,sx,glassTop+6,(gw-28)/3*.35,glassBottom-glassTop-14,.21);
      }
      for(const yy of [glassTop+52,glassTop+105]){
        if(yy>glassBottom-14)continue;
        rRect(g,0x486e67,gx+6,yy,gw-12,6);
        rRect(g,0xe8e5cf,gx+8,yy+1,gw-16,2,.82);
      }
      const bays=4;
      for(let j=0;j<=bays;j++){
        const px=gx+7+j*(gw-14)/bays;
        rRect(g,0x52756d,px-2,glassTop,5,glassBottom-glassTop);
        rRect(g,0xe2e9d9,px-1,glassTop,2,glassBottom-glassTop-4,.85);
      }
      // Roof: glazed pitched canopy, crisp copper ridge and continuous eaves;
      // its ridge is lower than the main mansard roof and neither triangle
      // projects into the neighbouring estate or the street.
      const eavesY=glassTop+1,apexY=y+90,midG=gx+gw/2;
      g.fillStyle(0x3e635e,1).fillTriangle(gx-6,eavesY,midG,apexY-8,gx+gw+6,eavesY);
      g.fillStyle(0x7fada1,1).fillTriangle(gx+4,eavesY-4,midG,apexY+3,gx+gw-4,eavesY-4);
      g.fillStyle(0xbfd6c4,.9).fillTriangle(gx+17,eavesY-8,midG,apexY+14,midG-15,eavesY-8);
      g.fillStyle(0xdce3cc,.30).fillTriangle(midG+12,apexY+16,midG+20,apexY+22,gx+gw-22,eavesY-6);
      rLine(g,0x456a65,gx-7,eavesY,midG,apexY-9,6);
      rLine(g,0x456a65,midG,apexY-9,gx+gw+7,eavesY,6);
      rLine(g,0xe2e7d0,gx-4,eavesY-1,midG,apexY-5,2,.78);
      rLine(g,0xe2e7d0,midG,apexY-5,gx+gw+4,eavesY-1,2,.78);
      rRect(g,0x48675e,gx-7,eavesY-1,gw+14,9);
      rRect(g,0xd5d3b8,gx-6,eavesY+1,gw+12,3);
      // Stone posts close the wall-to-greenhouse seam and the exposed end.
      for(const px of [gx-6,gx+gw-4]){
        rRect(g,0xa99980,px,glassTop-1,10,glassBottom-glassTop+7);
        rRect(g,0xf0e4cc,px+2,glassTop+3,6,glassBottom-glassTop-4);
        rRect(g,0xc4a783,px-2,glassBottom-1,14,8);
      }
      rRect(g,0xb09a79,gx-7,y+h-8,gw+14,7);
      rRect(g,0xf1dfbf,gx-5,y+h-8,gw+10,2);
      v24Heraldry(g,x+mainW*.46,y+112,0xdfc28e);
      v26GrandDoor(g,b);
      return;
    }
    // V27.3 Corven: narrower western townhouse with a deliberate setback from
    // the sovereign gate. Unlike V27.2, its turret cannot penetrate gate masonry.
    v26Stone(g,x+12,y+91,w-27,h-95,2);
    v26Mansard(g,x+8,y+59,w-16,74,0x515b6a,3);
    // An asymmetric cross-gable defines the manor entrance independently of
    // the turret; the facade stays within [x, x+w] with a small roof overhang.
    const hallCx=x+w*.43;
    g.fillStyle(0x364655,1).fillTriangle(hallCx-101,y+90,hallCx,y+2,hallCx+101,y+90);
    g.fillStyle(0xb6b9b7,1).fillTriangle(hallCx-81,y+87,hallCx,y+18,hallCx+81,y+87);
    rRect(g,0xf1e8d5,hallCx-39,y+87,78,12);
    rRect(g,0x987e61,hallCx-40,y+98,80,5);
    for(const rowY of [y+147,y+218])v26Windows(g,x+17,rowY,w-151,3,3,.82,false);
    // Turret is tangent to the right bay and has its own footing and cornice.
    const tx=x+w-99;
    v26Tower(g,tx,y+26,90,229,0x505b6a,true);
    rRect(g,0x988779,tx+4,base-8,84,7);
    rRect(g,0xe0d0b9,tx+8,base-8,76,2);
    v24Heraldry(g,hallCx,y+129,0xc9b48f);
    v26GrandDoor(g,b);
  }

  function v26GroundFinishing(g,a){
    // Controlled, district-aware cobbles: the single repeated 32px square is gone.
    const R=rnd(260026),townW=a.w*T,townH=a.h*T;
    // Low-contrast stone seams form groups, never a mechanical checker grid.
    for(let k=0;k<440;k++){
      const xx=144+R()*(townW-288), yy=145+R()*(townH-290);
      const ix=Math.floor(xx/T),iy=Math.floor(yy/T);
      if(a.buildings.some(b=>ix>b.x-1&&ix<b.x+b.w+1&&iy>b.y-1&&iy<b.y+b.h+1))continue;
      if(Math.abs(ix-44)<3||Math.hypot((ix-44)/12,(iy-32)/8)<1.0)continue;
      const span=10+Math.floor(R()*41),shade=R()>.62?0xf7e6c7:0x917c63;
      rRect(g,shade,xx,yy,span,2,.075+R()*.09);
      if(k%3===0)rRect(g,0x756952,xx+span-3,yy-6,2,6,.08);
    }
    // Palace garden axis: ornamental radial paving stops short of collision zones.
    for(let i=0;i<17;i++){
      const x=(35.2+i*1.05)*T,y=23.2*T;
      rRect(g,0xa88e6e,x,y,20,3,.19);
      rRect(g,0xffffff,x+2,y+1,9,1,.14);
    }
    // Entry-court cobbles: short staggered courses, visually subordinate to buildings.
    for(let row=0;row<7;row++)for(let k=0;k<10;k++){
      const xx=(37.7+k*1.34+(row%2)*.39)*T,yy=(49+row*.89)*T;
      if(Math.abs(xx-44*T)<48)continue;
      rRect(g,0x9b8266,xx,yy,29,2,.22);
    }
    // Sculpted round tree beds are away from the center, door tiles and garden paths.
    for(const [tx,ty] of [[6.3,25],[81.7,25],[6.5,48.7],[81.8,48.7]]){
      rOval(g,0x8b8069,tx*T,ty*T,78,39,.5);
      rOval(g,0xe5d4b0,tx*T,ty*T-4,70,29,.9);
      rOval(g,0x496749,tx*T,ty*T-6,58,22);
      royalTree(g,tx*T,ty*T-.3*T,.82);
    }
  }

  // V26: keep the tested 88×60 royal capital footprint and active quest geometry.
  function royalGround(g,a){
    const W2=a.w*T,H2=a.h*T,ct=(x,y)=>[x*T,y*T];
    // Warm stone is the quiet background; architecture supplies visual complexity.
    // Broad continuous masonry replaces the 5280 repeated tile squares.
    rRect(g,0xbca789,0,0,W2,H2);
    const royalRandom=rnd(26013);
    for(let i=0;i<440;i++){
      const px=royalRandom()*W2,py=royalRandom()*H2;
      const shade=i%5===0?0xf2dfbb:i%3===0?0xa9977a:0xd6c4a3;
      rRect(g,shade,px,py,35+royalRandom()*145,12+royalRandom()*70,.07+royalRandom()*.09);
    }
    const rw=4*T;
    // Deep enclosing river. Bridges and quays are later drawn over water.
    for(const [x,y,w,h] of [[0,0,W2,rw],[0,H2-rw,W2,rw],[0,0,rw,H2],[W2-rw,0,rw,H2]]){
      rRect(g,0x395c70,x,y,w,h);
      rRect(g,0x4f8192,x+5,y+5,w-10,h-10,.9);
      for(let i=0;i<Math.floor((w*h)/(32*600));i++){
        const px=x+12+((i*173+37)%(Math.max(14,w-24)));
        const py=y+10+((i*113+29)%(Math.max(13,h-20)));
        rRect(g,0x90c1c1,px,py,25+(i%4)*9,2,.13);
      }
    }
    // Retaining walls and a shadowed inner embankment.
    for(const yy of [rw-8,H2-rw]){
      rRect(g,0x344950,rw-6,yy+4,W2-2*rw+12,10,.38);
      rRect(g,0x867a70,rw-6,yy,W2-2*rw+12,8);
      rRect(g,0xdcc9a7,rw-6,yy,W2-2*rw+12,3);
    }
    for(const xx of [rw-8,W2-rw]){
      rRect(g,0x344950,xx+4,rw-8,10,H2-2*rw+16,.3);
      rRect(g,0x91816f,xx,rw-8,8,H2-2*rw+16);
      rRect(g,0xe1cdb0,xx,rw-8,3,H2-2*rw+16);
    }
    // Three broad stone bridges (the south one is the main ceremonial approach).
    const bridgeH=(x,y,w,h)=>{
      rRect(g,0x443a39,x-4,y+5,w+8,h+6,.34);
      rRect(g,0x9f8668,x-6,y-6,w+12,h+12);
      rRect(g,0xe1cead,x,y,w,h);
      for(let j=9;j<w-7;j+=29)rRect(g,0xc5ae89,x+j,y+6,3,h-12,.42);
      rRect(g,0xf7e4c2,x-6,y-7,w+12,7);
      rRect(g,0x765f53,x-6,y+h,w+12,8);
    };
    bridgeH(40*T,H2-rw,8*T,rw+6);
    bridgeH(0,29*T,rw+7,5*T);
    bridgeH(W2-rw-7,29*T,rw+7,5*T);
    // Upper terrace has a bespoke masonry base and a much clearer silhouette.
    rRect(g,0x9d8769,26*T,5.0*T,36*T,15*T);
    rRect(g,0xd6c2a2,26*T,5*T,36*T,14.6*T);
    rRect(g,0x92795f,26*T,19.25*T,36*T,10);
    for(let j=0;j<6;j++)rRect(g,j%2?0xf6e8c7:0xbdaa86,26*T,19.25*T+j*2,36*T,2,.8);
    // Continuous network: draw every outline, then every paved core.
    // Round joints include all branches, so streets connect without dark seams.
    const front=[],back=[];
    for(let i=0;i<=44;i++){
      const a=Math.PI-(i/44)*Math.PI;
      front.push(ct(44+22*Math.cos(a),32+10*Math.sin(a)));
    }
    for(let i=0;i<=28;i++){
      const a=Math.PI-(i/28)*Math.PI;
      back.push(ct(44+23.8*Math.cos(a),26-5.8*Math.sin(a)));
    }
    royalRoadNetwork(g,[
      {path:[ct(44.5,60),ct(44.5,54),ct(44.5,47),ct(44.5,40.5),ct(44.5,33),ct(44.5,25),ct(44.5,19.5)],width:132},
      {path:[ct(-2,31.5),ct(10,31.5),ct(21,32),ct(30,32.5)],width:69},
      {path:[ct(58,32.5),ct(67,32),ct(78,31.5),ct(90,31.5)],width:69},
      {path:front,width:49}, {path:back,width:38},
      {path:[ct(20,30),ct(26,26),ct(33,21.5)],width:40},
      {path:[ct(68,30),ct(62,26),ct(55,21.5)],width:40},
      // Opera and Academy forecourts already merge into the crescent;
      // a second diagonal here created pointed double-outline artefacts.
      ...v27DoorRoutes(a)
    ]);
    // Court of state: oval, ceremonial paving, a readable ring around the water.
    rOval(g,0x827361,44*T,32*T,21*T,12*T,.91);
    rOval(g,0xf0dfbd,44*T,31.8*T,20.4*T,11.4*T);
    rOval(g,0xcbb28a,44*T,31.8*T,17.6*T,9.0*T);
    rOval(g,0xf7e9cf,44*T,31.8*T,16.8*T,8.2*T);
    for(let i=0;i<32;i++){
      const t=i*Math.PI*2/32;
      rOval(g,0xac8f69,44*T+Math.cos(t)*9.65*T,31.8*T+Math.sin(t)*5.18*T,9,7,.55);
    }
    // Palace esplanade and stairs are deliberately kept free from furniture.
    rRect(g,0xe5d4b2,35*T,20*T,18*T,5*T);
    for(let i=0;i<7;i++){
      rRect(g,i%2?0xfaf1db:0xcbb18a,35*T,19.4*T+i*5,18*T,3,.75);
    }
    // Entrance court is part of the existing axial road, not a giant
    // square underlay that fakes a closed gate or leaves dark straight borders.
    // Subtle longitudinal joints only within the paved axis and bridge.
    for(const dx of [-1.55,-.62,.62,1.55]){
      rLine(g,0xb99d7b,(44.5+dx)*T,48.7*T,(44.5+dx)*T,59.7*T,2,.16);
    }
    // Two large formal gardens; no random scatter or fake solid objects.
    royalParterre(g,7.5*T,18.9*T,16*T,4.4*T,0);
    royalParterre(g,64.5*T,18.9*T,16*T,4.4*T,1);
    royalParterre(g,7.5*T,37.6*T,15*T,3.45*T,2);
    royalParterre(g,65.5*T,37.6*T,15*T,3.45*T,3);
    royalParterre(g,28*T,25.1*T,9.5*T,3.4*T,4);
    royalParterre(g,50.5*T,25.1*T,9.5*T,3.4*T,5);
    // Stone planting beds: a controlled number of trees read as wealth and order.
    for(const [x,y,s] of [[5.7,13,.91],[24.5,14.2,.88],[63.5,14.2,.88],[82.3,13,.91],
       [5.9,40.8,.86],[24.9,41.6,.86],[60.6,41.6,.86],[82.2,40.8,.86],
       [27.0,23.1,.73],[61.0,23.1,.73],[29.2,48.3,.75],[58.8,48.3,.75]]){
      royalTree(g,x*T,y*T,s);
    }
    for(const [x,y] of [[29,21],[59,21],[29,38],[59,38],[36.2,47],[51.8,47],[37,55],[51,55]])royalBollard(g,x*T,y*T);
    // Extra civic dressing: benches, planters, canopies and memorial stones make the capital feel inhabited.
    [[11.5,29.2,0],[17.2,29.2,1],[70.8,29.2,0],[76.5,29.2,1],[35.6,45.8,0],[52.3,45.8,1]].forEach(([x,y,f])=>royalBench(g,x*T,y*T,f));
    [[24.7,13.7,0],[63.3,13.7,1],[24.7,41.7,2],[63.3,41.7,3],[37.3,24.8,1],[50.7,24.8,2],[41.8,54.1,0],[47.2,54.1,1]].forEach(([x,y,m])=>royalPlanter(g,x*T,y*T,m));
    [[10.5,35.6,0],[77.5,35.6,1],[18.5,44.2,1],[69.5,44.2,0]].forEach(([x,y,m])=>royalCanopy(g,x*T,y*T,m));
    royalMemorial(g,12.4*T,49.0*T); royalMemorial(g,76.0*T,49.0*T);
    royalRuinCluster(g,19.8*T,50.2*T); royalRuinCluster(g,68.5*T,50.0*T);
    // Animated royal banners are drawn independently in the live-world layer.
    royalFountain(g,44*T,31.8*T);
    // Roads and doorway paths have already been composited into one network.
    // Landscape dressing never redraws their border as a stray rectangular box.
    v26GroundFinishing(g,a);
    // Fine-grain life after paving so it stays readable.
    [[7.3,11.8],[81.0,11.8],[7.5,39.4],[80.8,39.4],[29.0,16.5],[58.8,16.5]].forEach(([x,y],i)=>royalPlanter(g,x*T,y*T,4+i));
    [[13.8,33.5],[74.2,33.5],[23.0,52.0],[66.0,52.0]].forEach(([x,y],i)=>royalBench(g,x*T,y*T,i%2));
    v27DoorAprons(g,a);
    a.buildings.forEach(b=>v26Facade(g,b));
    // The open arch is centred between solid support cells, matching collisions.
    royalGate(g,44.5*T,55.5*T);
  }

  function royalWindow(g,x,y,tint=RC.blue,scale=1){
    const w=14*scale,h=24*scale;
    rRect(g,0x8d724f,x-3*scale,y-3*scale,w+6*scale,h+7*scale);
    rRect(g,0xf0d29b,x-1*scale,y-1*scale,w+2*scale,h+3*scale);
    rRect(g,tint,x,y,w,h);
    rOval(g,tint,x+w/2,y+1*scale,w,11*scale);
    rRect(g,0xbad4d3,x+3*scale,y+3*scale,3*scale,h-7*scale,.7);
    rRect(g,0x2d4766,x+w/2-1*scale,y,2*scale,h);
    rRect(g,0x90774e,x-5*scale,y+h-2*scale,w+10*scale,3*scale);
  }
  function royalColumn(g,x,y,h){
    rRect(g,0x9c8a77,x+2,y+1,15,h,.6);rRect(g,0xf6e7c6,x,y,14,h);
    rRect(g,0xc6b294,x+9,y,3,h);
    rRect(g,0xfff2d5,x-3,y,19,5);
    rRect(g,0xa18469,x-5,y+h-6,22,6);
    for(let q=0;q<3;q++)rRect(g,0xdcc9a4,x+3+q*3,y+6,1,h-15);
  }
  function royalRoof(g,x,y,w,h,c=RC.blue){
    rRect(g,0x252f48,x+4,y+h-1,w,13,.31);
    rRect(g,c,x,y+6,w,h);
    rRect(g,0x263e56,x,y+8,w,8,.7);
    rRect(g,0x698ba1,x+6,y+9,w-12,4,.76);
    for(let i=11;i<w-5;i+=17){rRect(g,0xb0c0bd,x+i,y+17,8,2,.55);rRect(g,0x1a364b,x+i+3,y+24,8,2,.45);}
    rRect(g,0xe0c48d,x-4,y+h,w+8,6);
    rRect(g,0x94744b,x-4,y+h+4,w+8,3);
  }
  function royalDoor(g,b,x,y,w,h){
    const dx=(b.door+.5)*T;
    rOval(g,0x5d5b63,dx,y+h-12,58,14,.35);
    rRect(g,0x886c4f,dx-23,y+h-52,46,54);
    rOval(g,0xd5b37c,dx,y+h-53,46,29);
    rRect(g,0x493d43,dx-17,y+h-48,34,49);
    rRect(g,0x7b5260,dx-14,y+h-45,28,44);
    rRect(g,0xc9a568,dx-2,y+h-42,3,43);
    for(let j=0;j<3;j++)rRect(g,0xe9c994,dx-13,y+h-31+j*9,26,2,.72);
    rOval(g,0xffdf8e,dx+11,y+h-23,5,5);
    rRect(g,0xefdfc3,dx-32,y+h-1,64,8);
    rRect(g,0xbeab90,dx-38,y+h+7,76,5);
  }
  // V24 architectural vocabulary: mansard roofs, stone cornices, recessed arches,
  // dormers, carved balustrades and distinctly proportioned historic façades.
  function v24Cornice(g,x,y,w,shade=0xc4a06b){
    rRect(g,0x7d6752,x-5,y+5,w+10,6);
    rRect(g,shade,x-8,y,w+16,7);
    rRect(g,0xf8e8c5,x-7,y+1,w+14,2,.9);

  function v24Roof(g,x,y,w,h,c=0x445d76){

  function v24Window(g,x,y,s=1,tint=0x567992,balcony=false){

  function royalGate(g,cx,cy){
    // V39 Sovereign Gate: taller towers, flanking walls, forecourt statuary and a more ceremonial silhouette.
    // Geometry still respects waterBarriers(): tower tiles 37..39 / 49..51,
    // supports 41 / 47 and open walking lanes 42..46.
    const xL=37*T,xR=49*T,top=49*T,bottom=56*T;
    // Forecourt plinth and steps make the approach feel important without blocking the passage.
    for(let i=0;i<4;i++){
      rRect(g,i%2?0x9e8668:0xbda07b,39*T-i*7,56*T+3+i*6,11*T+i*14,7);
      rRect(g,0xf3e6cf,39*T-i*7,56*T+3+i*6,11*T+i*14,3,.74);
    }
    for(const [sx,alt] of [[xL,false],[xR,true]]){
      const roof=alt?0x58718a:0x435f7d;
      rOval(g,0x55493e,sx+48,bottom+4,114,18,.23);
      rRect(g,0x8b745b,sx-4,top+11,3*T+8,bottom-top-8);
      rRect(g,0xf0e2ca,sx+4,top+16,3*T-8,bottom-top-20);
      rRect(g,0xd4b189,sx+2,bottom-17,3*T-4,8);
      rRect(g,0x8d6a52,sx+2,bottom-9,3*T-4,7);
      // Secondary belfry tier
      rRect(g,0xa48d70,sx+18,top-18,60,32);
      rRect(g,0xf5e7cf,sx+23,top-14,50,24);
      rRect(g,0xc7a677,sx+20,top-18,56,5);
      // Main roof + crown roof
      g.fillStyle(0x34485c,1).fillTriangle(sx-11,top+18,sx+48,top-45,sx+3*T+11,top+18);
      g.fillStyle(roof,1).fillTriangle(sx-6,top+16,sx+48,top-34,sx+3*T+6,top+16);
      g.fillStyle(0x2f4256,1).fillTriangle(sx+10,top-12,sx+48,top-51,sx+86,top-12);
      g.fillStyle(alt?0x7ea0b3:0x6e8da2,1).fillTriangle(sx+18,top-12,sx+48,top-41,sx+78,top-12);
      rRect(g,0xe2c79c,sx-7,top+16,3*T+14,6);
      v26HighWindow(g,sx+39,top+42,alt?1:0,.9);
      v26HighWindow(g,sx+39,top+103,alt?2:0,.88);
      rRect(g,0x7e5d48,sx+16,top-5,16,19); rRect(g,0xead8b8,sx+19,top-2,10,12);
      rRect(g,0x7e5d48,sx+64,top-5,16,19); rRect(g,0xead8b8,sx+67,top-2,10,12);
      v24Heraldry(g,sx+48,top+102,0xd7b678);
      royalFlag(g,sx+48,top-40,alt);
      royalFlag(g,sx+18,top-10,!alt);
      royalFlag(g,sx+78,top-10,alt);
    }
    // Flanking curtain walls visually widen the gate ensemble.
    for(const [x,w] of [[33*T,4*T],[52*T,4*T]]){
      rRect(g,0x9b8264,x,52*T+6,w,2*T-6);
      rRect(g,0xf1e3c9,x+4,52*T+11,w-8,2*T-18);
      for(let k=0;k<4;k++)rRect(g,0xceae7f,x+8+k*28,52*T+7,14,5,.92);
      rRect(g,0x7d654d,x-2,56*T-4,w+4,5);
    }
    // Solid end supports occupy one tile each; no hidden wall in the opening.
    for(const tile of [41,47]){
      const x=tile*T;
      rRect(g,0x998168,x-1,53*T-8,T+2,3*T+12);
      rRect(g,0xefe0c7,x+4,53*T-5,T-8,3*T+4);
      rRect(g,0xceaa77,x-6,53*T-12,T+12,10);
      rRect(g,0x88674e,x-5,56*T-7,T+10,8);
      rRect(g,0xf9efdb,x+10,53*T+11,12,28,.35);
    }
    const ax=41*T,aw=7*T;
    rRect(g,0x8f7458,ax-2,50*T+20,aw+4,58);
    rRect(g,0xf1e3c9,ax+5,50*T+25,aw-10,46);
    v24Cornice(g,ax-1,50*T+11,aw+2,0xd6b77d);
    g.fillStyle(0x405c79,1).fillTriangle(ax-8,50*T+12,cx,50*T-63,ax+aw+8,50*T+12);
    g.fillStyle(0x7291a0,1).fillTriangle(ax+10,50*T+8,cx,50*T-46,ax+aw-10,50*T+8);
    v24Heraldry(g,cx,50*T+45,0xe2be80);
    // Arch remains open.
    const archY=53*T+26,rx=3*T,ry=54;
    const arc=(color,width,dx=0,dy=0)=>{
      for(let j=0;j<32;j++){
        const a=Math.PI-j*Math.PI/32,b=a-Math.PI/32;
        rLine(g,color,cx+dx+rx*Math.cos(a),archY+dy-ry*Math.sin(a),cx+dx+rx*Math.cos(b),archY+dy-ry*Math.sin(b),width);
      }
    };
    arc(0x675246,17,0,4);
    arc(0xcaa983,14,0,1);
    arc(0xf8e7cc,5,0,-3);
    for(let j=1;j<11;j++){
      const a=Math.PI-j*Math.PI/11,px=cx+rx*Math.cos(a),py=archY-ry*Math.sin(a);
      const dx=4*Math.cos(a),dy=-4*Math.sin(a);
      rLine(g,0x9b8063,px-dx,py-dy,px+dx,py+dy,1,.60);
    }
    rRect(g,0xf2dfc1,cx-7,archY-ry-7,14,7);
    rRect(g,0xa68c6b,cx-7,archY-ry,14,2,.5);
    // Gate lamps and statuary at the approach.
    for(const [lx,ly] of [[39.4,54.9],[49.6,54.9]]) royalBollard(g,lx*T,ly*T);
    v24Statue(g,38.2*T,56.0*T-8); v24Statue(g,50.8*T,56.0*T-8);
    royalPlanter(g,36.0*T,55.4*T,2); royalPlanter(g,53.0*T,55.4*T,3);
  }

  function royalCarriage(g,x,y,mode=0){
    const sign=mode?-1:1;
    rOval(g,0x242831,x,y+21,112,21,.27);
    rRect(g,0x4e3b43,x-34,y-11,62,28);
    rRect(g,mode?0x526174:0x793f54,x-31,y-24,56,22);
    rRect(g,0xe1bc7d,x-34,y-28,62,6);
    rRect(g,0xf3ddb7,x-24,y-19,20,13);
    rRect(g,0x6a8598,x-3,y-19,19,13);
    rRect(g,0xf1d9a8,x-30,y+13,62,5);
    for(const d of [-19,19]){
      rOval(g,0x33333b,x+d,y+19,20,21);
      rOval(g,0xa98f70,x+d,y+19,9,9);
    }
    const hx=x+52*sign;
    rRect(g,0x94714f,hx-13,y-11,26,21);
    rRect(g,0xb48b61,hx+sign*12-11,y-19,14,17);
    rRect(g,0x372d30,hx+sign*11,y-27,7,13);
    for(const dx of [-8,8])rRect(g,0x463b39,hx+dx,y+9,4,17);
    rLine(g,0xdbc395,x+24*sign,y+1,hx-14*sign,y+1,3,.9);
    rRect(g,0xe9c889,x-10,y-24,7,3);
  }
  function royalPulse(g,time,a){
    const s=time*.001,w=a?.w||88,h=a?.h||60;
    const fx=44*T,fy=31.8*T;
    // Three elegant fountain ripple rings. Small, capped and cheap to render.
    for(let k=0;k<3;k++){
      const r=15+((time*.012+k*21)%72);
      g.lineStyle(2,0xe5faf3,.05+.19*(1-r/90));
      g.strokeEllipse(fx,fy-5,r*1.5,r*.55);
    }
    rOval(g,0xebfff0,fx,fy-64-Math.sin(s*2)*2,7,8,.8);
    // Gate lantern breath and ceremonial gleam.
    for(const [gx,gy] of [[39.4*T,54.9*T],[49.6*T,54.9*T],[44.5*T,50.2*T]]){
      const rr=18+Math.sin(s*2+gx*.01)*3;
      g.lineStyle(2,0xf9efc1,.07); g.strokeEllipse(gx,gy,rr,rr*.55);
      rOval(g,0xfff6cf,gx,gy-18-Math.sin(s*2+gy*.01),7,6,.55);
    }
    // Slow perimeter water: every ripple stays inside a water band, never on stone.
    const usableX=(w-11)*T,usableY=(h-11)*T;
    for(let k=0;k<18;k++){
      const ph=(time*(.013+(k%4)*.002)+k*149)%usableX;
      const px=5*T+ph;
      const x2=5*T+((ph+300)%usableX);
      const alpha=.09+(Math.sin(s*1.1+k*2)+1)*.04;
      rOval(g,0xd4eff0,px,(1.9+(k%3)*.65)*T,28+k%4*8,3,alpha);
      rOval(g,0xa9dce0,x2,(h-2.1-(k%3)*.5)*T,33+k%3*8,3,alpha);
    }
    for(let k=0;k<11;k++){
      const ph=(time*.014+k*137)%usableY;
      const y=5*T+ph;
      rOval(g,0xb8e0e0,(1.6+(k%3)*.7)*T,y,4,24+k%4*8,.12);
      rOval(g,0xb8e0e0,(w-1.7-(k%3)*.65)*T,5*T+((ph+180)%usableY),4,24+k%4*8,.12);
    }
    // Carriages follow short stretches of the lower crescent road, never cross
    // the formal gardens. Smooth yoyo motion avoids teleporting at route ends.
    const sway=(Math.sin(time*.00038)+1)*.5;
    royalCarriage(g,(30+7*sway)*T,(40.5+.8*sway)*T,Math.cos(time*.00038)<0?1:0);
    royalCarriage(g,(58-7*sway)*T,(40.5+.8*sway)*T,Math.cos(time*.00038)>0?1:0);
    const mins=Number(game()?.minutes??480),night=mins>1040||mins<355;
    if(night){
      for(const [x,y] of [[29,21],[59,21],[29,38],[59,38],[36,47],[52,47]]){
        rOval(g,0xffd995,x*T,y*T-26,54,44,.045+(Math.sin(s*1.4+x)+1)*.012);
      }
    }
    // Drifting petals and plaza birds add subtle life to the capital.
    for(let i=0;i<8;i++){
      const px=(33+Math.sin(time*.00042+i*1.3)*18 + i*2.1)%w, py=27+Math.cos(time*.00035+i*.7)*10;
      rOval(g,i%2?0xf0d0d4:0xf4e5b6,px*T,py*T,7,4,.40);
    }
    for(let i=0;i<6;i++){
      const bx=(16+i*9+Math.sin(s*1.2+i)*2.2)*T, by=(34+Math.cos(s*1.5+i*.8)*1.4)*T;
      rOval(g,0x3f444b,bx,by,9,4,.45); rOval(g,0xd9ddd9,bx+1,by-1,6,2,.35);
    }
  }

  // V26 · Live animation uses one scene-owned Graphics layer. No animated
  // elements are baked into the 88×60 static texture; no texture generation
  // takes place inside the per-frame drawing loop.
  function v26Banner(g,x,y,time,blue=false,small=false){
    const ph=time*.0016+x*.0008,wind=Math.sin(ph)*4+Math.sin(ph*.43)*2;
    const height=small?37:58, width=small?24:43;
    rRect(g,0x5d6066,x,y-height,3,height);
    rOval(g,0xeacb86,x+1,y-height,7,7);
    const by=y-height+9;
    for(let k=0;k<4;k++){
      const shift=Math.round(Math.sin(ph+k*.75)*3+(k/3)*wind);
      const yy=by+k*(small?4:6);
      rRect(g,blue?0x415b78:0x7b3d54,x+3+shift,yy,width-k*3,small?5:7);
      rRect(g,blue?0x8eafba:0xba7e87,x+6+shift,yy+1,width-k*5-2,1,.74);
    }
    rRect(g,0xe2c58d,x+10+Math.round(wind*.3),by+6,12,4);
    rRect(g,0xffe5af,x+13+Math.round(wind*.3),by+5,3,7,.86);
  }
  function v26Carriage(g,cx,cy,mirror,time,style=0){
    // Both the wheels and the horse have articulated cyclic gait.
    const dir=mirror?-1:1,phase=time*.0105+style*1.5,
      lift=Math.abs(Math.sin(phase))*2.1,spoke=phase*.9;
    const body=style?0x4b5e72:0x763b50,steel=0x333a43;
    rOval(g,0x282a31,cx,cy+20,128,21,.24);
    const bx=cx-25*dir;
    for(const ox of [-23,23]){
      const wx=bx+ox,wy=cy+16;
      rOval(g,steel,wx,wy,24,24);
      rOval(g,0xc3a075,wx,wy,16,16);
      rOval(g,0x33363d,wx,wy,10,10);
      for(let k=0;k<4;k++){
        const a=spoke+k*Math.PI/2;
        rLine(g,0xe2c28f,wx,wy,wx+Math.cos(a)*7,wy+Math.sin(a)*7,2);
      }
    }
    rRect(g,0x604d48,bx-37,cy-17-lift,74,31);
    rRect(g,body,bx-33,cy-39-lift,66,34);
    rRect(g,0xd6b47e,bx-37,cy-40-lift,74,7);
    for(const xx of [-21,7]){
      rRect(g,0xe9d5b7,bx+xx,cy-30-lift,19,16);
      rRect(g,0x70949f,bx+xx+3,cy-27-lift,13,11);
      rRect(g,0xcce4df,bx+xx+4,cy-26-lift,4,8,.56);
    }
    rRect(g,0xe7c789,bx-38,cy+5-lift,76,6);
    rRect(g,0x342d37,bx-14,cy-46-lift,30,7);
    // Horse body, neck, mane and reins.
    const hx=bx+71*dir,hy=cy-1;
    rOval(g,0x8c674d,hx,hy,46,27);
    rRect(g,0xb48a63,hx+6*dir-7,hy-22,18,25);
    rRect(g,0xb58d69,hx+20*dir-7,hy-27,16,14);
    rRect(g,0x46383b,hx+20*dir-6,hy-39,4,12);
    rOval(g,0x332f35,hx+27*dir,hy-24,3,3);
    rLine(g,0xd6b98f,bx+31*dir,cy-17,hx-14*dir,hy-12,2);
    // Four legs with out-of-phase ankle, not static vertical rectangles.
    for(let i=0;i<4;i++){
      const ax=hx+([-13,-4,10,17][i])*dir;
      const swing=Math.sin(phase+(i%2?Math.PI:0)+(i>1?Math.PI*.45:0))*6;
      rLine(g,0x74543e,ax,hy+9,ax+swing*.65,hy+20,4);
      rLine(g,0x403336,ax+swing*.65,hy+20,ax+swing,hy+25-(i%2)*2,3);
    }
    rRect(g,0xe9c691,bx-2,cy-41-lift,4,3);
  }
  function v26FlyingBird(g,x,y,phase){
    const flap=Math.sin(phase)*5;
    rRect(g,0x4f5660,x-2,y,5,3);
    rLine(g,0xb9bdc0,x,y,x-8,y-3-flap,2,.86);
    rLine(g,0xb9bdc0,x,y,x+8,y-3-flap,2,.86);
  }
  function v26Pulse(g,time,a){
    const s=time*.001,w=a?.w||88,h=a?.h||60,TW=w*T,TH=h*T;
    const cx=44*T,cy=31.8*T;
    // Moving fountain sheets and cascading droplets.
    for(let k=0;k<4;k++){
      const r=19+((time*.017+k*22)%85),alpha=.04+.23*(1-r/110);
      g.lineStyle(2,0xdbfff0,Math.max(.01,alpha));g.strokeEllipse(cx,cy-7,r*1.9,r*.65);
    }
    for(let k=0;k<8;k++){
      const theta=k*Math.PI/4,hh=(time*.027+k*10)%51;
      const px=cx+Math.cos(theta)*30;
      const py=cy-73+hh;
      rRect(g,0xd0fff5,px,py,2,5,.24+.26*(1-hh/52));
    }
    // Water ripples are short streaks in bands, never ellipses on pavement.
    for(let side=0;side<4;side++)for(let i=0;i<13;i++){
      const p=(time*(.009+(i%3)*.002)+i*233+side*47);
      let xx,yy,dx,dy;
      if(side<2){
        xx=145+p%(TW-290);yy=side===0?(1.2+i%3*.77)*T:(h-1.1-i%3*.65)*T;
        dx=11+(i%4)*6;dy=0;
      }else{
        xx=side===2?(1.1+i%3*.75)*T:(w-1.1-i%3*.68)*T;
        yy=145+p%(TH-290);dx=0;dy=10+(i%3)*7;
      }
      rLine(g,0xc1e6e2,xx,yy,xx+dx,yy+dy,2,.12+.075*Math.sin(s+i));
      rRect(g,0xffffff,xx,yy,side<2?5:2,side<2?1:5,.06);
    }
    // Banner physics, controlled stagger; flag has 4 independent cloth strips.
    for(const [x,y,blue] of [[24,20,false],[64,20,true],[26,37,false],[62,37,true],
      [38,49.5,false],[50,49.5,true],[35.1,20,false],[52.9,20,true]])
      v26Banner(g,x*T,y*T,time,blue,true);
    for(const dx of [-180,180])v26Banner(g,cx+dx,55.5*T-228,time,dx>0,false);
    // Six different wave phases across the central palace's crown.
    for(const [x,y] of [[28.8,18.8],[59.2,18.8],[42.1,7.5],[45.9,7.5]])
      v26Banner(g,x*T,y*T,time,x>44,false);
    // Two slower carriages travel on the wide lower crescent. Smooth turn at ends.
    const phase=time*.00032,unit=(1-Math.cos(phase))/2,
      eased=unit*unit*(3-2*unit),forward=Math.sin(phase)>=0;
    v26Carriage(g,(30.5+6.5*eased)*T,(40.1+.7*eased)*T,!forward,time,0);
    v26Carriage(g,(57.5-6.5*eased)*T,(40.1+.7*eased)*T,forward,time+800,1);
    // A light flock only, fully inside the plaza and garden sky area.
    for(let i=0;i<4;i++){
      const tx=(38.1+i*3.7+(time*.000012*(i+1))%1.7)*T;
      const ty=(27.1+Math.sin(s*.5+i)*.65)*T;
      v26FlyingBird(g,tx,ty,s*3+i*2.2);
    }
    const mins=Number(game()?.minutes??480),night=mins>1040||mins<355;
    if(night){
      for(const [x,y] of [[29,21],[59,21],[29,38],[59,38],[36,47],[52,47]])
        rOval(g,0xffd995,x*T,y*T-25,54,42,.038+(Math.sin(s*1.2+x)+1)*.011);
    }
  }

  const v26AmbientActors = new WeakMap();
  const V26_WALKERS=[
    {role:'guard',name:'Royal Guard',at:[40,54],route:[[40,54],[40,55]],coat:0x4c637d},
    {role:'guard',name:'Royal Guard',at:[48,54],route:[[48,54],[48,55]],coat:0x4c637d},
    {role:'steward',name:'Palace Attendant',at:[39,24],route:[[39,24],[38,25],[39,25]],coat:0x75516b},
    {role:'steward',name:'Palace Attendant',at:[50,24],route:[[50,24],[49,25],[50,25]],coat:0x75516b},
    {role:'scholar',name:'Academy Scholar',at:[68,38],route:[[68,38],[68,39],[70,39],[68,39]],coat:0x4e736f},
    {role:'visitor',name:'Opera Visitor',at:[17,37],route:[[17,37],[19,36],[23,36]],coat:0x884b61},
    {role:'visitor',name:'Opera Visitor',at:[23,37],route:[[23,37],[25,37],[25,39]],coat:0x884b61},
    {role:'noble',name:'Noble Resident',at:[35,39],route:[[35,39],[37,40],[37,41]],coat:0x78617f},
    {role:'noble',name:'Noble Resident',at:[54,39],route:[[54,39],[51,40],[51,41]],coat:0x78617f},
    {role:'gardener',name:'Royal Gardener',at:[25,40],route:[[25,40],[27,40],[27,41]],coat:0x537159},
    {role:'merchant',name:'Court Merchant',at:[30,26],route:[[30,26],[28,26],[26,27],[28,27]],coat:0x936548},
    {role:'merchant',name:'Court Merchant',at:[58,26],route:[[58,26],[60,26],[62,27],[60,27]],coat:0x936548},
    {role:'chronicler',name:'Royal Chronicler',at:[63,40],route:[[63,40],[61,40],[59,39],[61,39]],coat:0x546681},
    {role:'musician',name:'Court Musician',at:[28,40],route:[[28,40],[30,40],[31,39],[29,39]],coat:0x8a4f66},
    {role:'page',name:'Palace Page',at:[44,23],route:[[44,23],[43,24],[45,24],[44,25]],coat:0x8b7558},
    {role:'pilgrim',name:'Pilgrim Visitor',at:[44,47],route:[[44,47],[42,48],[46,48],[44,49]],coat:0x6e7757}
  ];
  function v26TownspersonTexture(sc,role,dir,frame){
    const key=`lq26_people_${role}_${dir}_${frame}`;
    if(sc.textures.exists(key))return key;
    const g=sc.add.graphics(),paint=(color,x,y,w,h)=>g.fillStyle(color,1).fillRect(x,y,w,h);
    const type=V26_WALKERS.find(a=>a.role===role),coat=type?.coat||0x5a6f82;
    const skin=role==='guard'?0xe0b391:role==='gardener'?0xc58d6f:0xe3ac8a;
    const hair=role==='noble'?0x6c5049:0x343746;
    const gait=frame===1?3:frame===2?-3:0,side=dir==='left'||dir==='right';
    paint(0x2e3039,7,44,19,2);
    paint(0x353743,9+gait,39,6,6);paint(0x353743,19-gait,39,6,6);
    paint(0x343b48,10,29,5,12);paint(0x343b48,19,29,5,12);
    paint(coat,8,20,18,15);
    paint(0xd7bc92,14,20,5,11);
    paint(coat,5,21+(gait>0?2:0),4,12);paint(coat,26,21+(gait<0?2:0),4,12);
    paint(skin,6,31,3,3);paint(skin,27,31,3,3);
    if(role==='guard'){
      paint(0xd6b77b,8,23,18,2);paint(0xd6b77b,16,25,2,10);
      paint(0x344252,7,9,21,3);paint(0xcbb47f,10,5,15,5);
    }else if(role==='noble'){
      paint(0xe2cfb3,7,30,20,9);paint(coat,6,35,22,6);
      paint(0xd4b98a,10,20,3,15);
    }else if(role==='gardener'){
      paint(0x8e7b59,8,20,17,4);paint(0xd2bf8e,9,7,17,4);
      paint(0x697653,6,30,4,10);
    }else if(role==='scholar'){
      paint(0xe8d49f,7,27,7,7);paint(0x647a8a,9,28,4,4);
    }else if(role==='visitor'){
      paint(0xeac88d,8,20,3,11);paint(0xeac88d,23,20,3,11);
    }
    paint(skin,12,10,12,12);
    if(dir==='up')paint(hair,10,7,16,12);
    else if(side){paint(hair,11,6,15,7);paint(hair,dir==='left'?10:23,12,3,8);}
    else{
      paint(hair,10,6,15,7);paint(hair,10,12,3,6);paint(hair,23,12,3,6);
      paint(0x2b2731,14,16,2,frame===3?1:2);
      paint(0x2b2731,21,16,2,frame===3?1:2);
    }
    if(frame===3)paint(0xd8bba2,9,23,2,2);
    g.generateTexture(key,32,48);g.destroy();
    try{sc.textures.get(key).setFilter(Phaser.Textures.FilterMode.NEAREST);}catch(_){ }
    return key;
  }
  function v26AttachTownspeople(sc){
    // Re-create ambient sprites on each visit. The Phaser scene object can be
    // reused after switchArea() destroys its previous area objects.
    const previous=v26AmbientActors.get(sc)||[];
    for(const old of previous){
      if(old.sprite?.active)old.sprite.destroy();
